### [1] [EGGHead: Efficient Generalizable Gaussian Heads from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26898)
*Pilseo Park,Andrew Hou,Michel Sarkis,Ning Bi,Yiying Tong,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EGGHead：提出语义区域感知的高斯分配算法，在固定高斯预算下把高斯高效分配给头部语义区域，单次前向即可生成可实时渲染的照片级真实人头模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人头建模存在表示能力、效率与泛化性的三难：3DMM网格高效但表示力弱；NeRF保真但慢；3DGS兼顾两者，但渲染开销随高斯数量增长，且逐主体优化（enrollment）耗时高。目标是降低高斯数量同时尽量不损失表示能力，并实现单图前馈生成。

Method: 以固定高斯预算为前提，设计新的高斯分配算法，依据头部语义区域（如皮肤、头发、五官等）自适应分配高斯密度；网络采用单次前向预测（single forward pass）直接输出头部高斯表示，无需逐主体优化。

Result: 相较以往单图人脸/人头建模方法，在重建质量、新视角合成质量与渲染速度上均取得更优表现，同时所需高斯数量减少。

Conclusion: 通过语义感知的高斯预算分配，可在保持表示能力的同时显著提升效率，验证了“分配策略优于单纯增加高斯数量”的思路，为人头前馈高斯建模提供高效范式。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic human heads typically involves trade-offs between representation power, efficiency, and generalizability. Traditional mesh-based solutions relying on 3DMMs generally sacrifice representation power but are efficient to render. On the other hand, NeRF-based methods improve representation power and fidelity while sacrificing efficiency. Gaussian splatting methods attempt to resolve these trade-offs by offering both high representation power and efficiency with real-time rendering. However, their efficiency nonetheless depends on the number of rasterized Gaussians, and the time needed to optimize and prepare the head (the enrollment time) is often high. In light of this, we propose EGGHead, which introduces a novel Gaussian allocation algorithm that efficiently assigns Gaussians to the head's semantic regions given a fixed Gaussian budget. Our algorithm reduces the number of Gaussians needed while minimizing the impact on representation power. EGGHead operates in a single forward pass and outperforms previous single-image face modeling methods in reconstruction quality, novel view synthesis, and rendering speeds.

</details>


### [2] [Beckmann World Models: Direct Terminal-Map Learning for One-Step Action-Conditioned Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26903)
*Adam Lee,Shobhit Agarwal,June Pyo Suh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BWM将Beckmann Transport Model的终末映射守恒律适配到动作条件视频预测，无需预训练生成教师，直接从噪声-数据对训练；通过端点对齐训练弥补推理时纯噪声输入的一致性缺口。PushT/Robomimic Can上64帧MSE较同骨干回归基线降68%/57%，策略评估IoU误差最低0.0100，24.8ms/四帧预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型需准确一步预测器以支撑规划与策略评估，但现有方法训练开销大：依赖预训练生成教师（如扩散蒸馏），或需条件生成样本族群（DriftWorld）来估计漂移场。作者希望用更省算力的方式学会从噪声直接映射到未来帧。

Method: 将Beckmann Transport Model的terminal-map守恒律推广至动作条件视频预测，训练目标仅需噪声-数据对，无需条件生成族群；发现插值输入上的传输一致性不能保证纯噪声推理的准确性，因而引入端点对齐训练：直接监督源端预测、强化运动与空间结构、并训练从自生成历史中恢复。所有组件共享同一映射网络，每个未来chunk仅一次网络调用；另给出RT-1/Bridge-V2潜空间扩展，用特征监督。

Result: PushT与Robomimic Can上64帧预测MSE相对同骨干回归基线分别降68%、57%；策略评估平均IoU误差0.0100为所评模型最低，PushT四帧预测24.8ms；Robomimic Can上带运动监督与生成历史的BWM阶段比局部一步DriftWorld吞吐高3.3倍、峰值显存低78%；潜空间RT-1/Bridge-V2等更新对照显示生成历史下的特征监督提升感知预测。

Conclusion: 终末映射学习可同时实现高效训练与准确的一步视觉预测，且无需预训练生成教师或条件样本族群；端点对齐是弥合训练/推理分布差距的关键。局限在于主要验证于2D仿真推物任务与离线策略评估，缺少真机闭环操作与长时程规划的充分证据。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models support planning and policy evaluation by predicting future states. However, learning an accurate one-step predictor can require substantial training computation. We introduce Beckmann World Models (BWM), which learn a direct map from noise, visual history, and actions to future frames without a pretrained generative teacher. We adapt the terminal-map conservation law of Beckmann Transport Models to action-conditioned video prediction, training directly from noise-data pairs without conditional populations of generated samples required by DriftWorld. However, transport consistency on interpolated inputs does not ensure accurate prediction from the pure noise used at inference. We address this gap with endpoint-aligned training that directly supervises the source prediction, emphasizes motion and spatial structure, and teaches recovery from generated histories. All components train the same map and retain one network call per future chunk. On PushT and Robomimic Can, the final model reduces 64-frame prediction MSE by 68% and 57%, respectively, relative to same-backbone regression baseline. For policy evaluation, BWM achieves the lowest mean IoU error among the evaluated models, 0.0100, at 24.8 ms per four-frame PushT prediction. On Robomimic Can, the BWM stage trained with motion supervision and generated histories achieves 3.3 times higher throughput and 78% lower peak memory than local one-step DriftWorld. In a latent-space extension to RT-1 and Bridge-V2, equal-update controls show that feature supervision under generated histories improves perceptual prediction. These findings show how terminal-map learning can combine efficient training with accurate one-step visual prediction.

</details>


### [3] [Uncertainty Gates the Gradient: Adaptive Updates for Generalized Few-Shot 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26909)
*Yifei Zhao,Fanyu Zhao,Shengtang Wu,Han Zhang,Ruike Cao,Yinsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UGA框架，将不确定性升级为更新级门控，用于广义少样本3D点云分割：UG-Update通过类内离散度估计episode可靠性并门控残差梯度更新，UB-Filter过滤低置信伪标签，在ScanNet200/ScanNet++上平均+3.08 HM IoU领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义少样本3D点云分割中，支持集标注常受稀疏与遮挡影响，盲目适配低质量样本会学到虚假的几何模式而非鲁棒的新类概念。3D视觉语言模型虽可补充监督，但现有loss重加权等信号级方法无法阻止不可靠episode驱动优化轨迹。

Method: UGA框架包含两个模块：UG-Update（Uncertainty-Gated Update）基于合并支持集与伪标签新类监督的类内离散度估计episode可靠性，并选择性门控残差梯度更新，可靠episode学习新语义、不可靠episode衰减更新；UB-Filter（Uncertainty-Based Pseudo Label Filter）用不确定性筛除低置信伪标签，仅从高置信稠密信号学习。

Result: 在ScanNet200与ScanNet++的1-shot/5-shot设定下取得新的SOTA，相对最强基线平均提升+3.08 HM IoU。

Conclusion: 将不确定性从损失加权层面提升至梯度更新层面的门控机制，可有效抑制低质量episode对优化的误导，是3D点云少样本分割中一种可行且有效的训练动态控制范式。

Abstract: In generalized few-shot 3D point cloud segmentation, support annotations are often compromised by sparsity and occlusion. Blindly adapting to such low quality samples risks learning spurious geometric patterns rather than robust novel concepts. Although 3D Vision-Language Models have been introduced to sup plement supervision, the potential of uncertainty to actively govern adaptation dynamics remains underexplored. Existing signal-level methods like loss reweight ing fail to prevent unreliable episodes from steering the optimization trajectory. To address this, we propose Uncertainty-Gated Adaptive (UGA), to the best of our knowledge the first framework that elevates uncertainty to update-level gating for generalized few-shot 3D point cloud segmentation. The core mod ule, Uncertainty-Gated Update (UG-Update), estimates episodic reliability via intra-class dispersion over merged support and pseudo-labeled novel supervision, and selectively gates residual gradient updates. This encourages the model to learn novel semantics from reliable episodes while attenuating updates during unreliable ones. Complementing this, we introduce an Uncertainty-Based Pseudo Label Filter (UB-Filter) to enforce learning from high-confidence dense signals. On ScanNet200 and ScanNet++ (1-/5-shot), UGA sets new state of the art with an average +3.08 HM IoU over the strongest prior baseline.

</details>


### [4] [Memory Internalization via Test-Time Training for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26910)
*Haonan Zhao,Jiaxuan Chen,Qingyang Liu,Shuochen Chang,Siyu Sun,Jiangtong Li,Li Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIT^3：用测试时训练把长视频“写”进模型参数，并用因果追踪选择写入层、用视觉记忆蒸馏保留视觉证据的内部效应，实现免视频、仅文本的长视频问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化视频记忆主要依赖合成文本监督和固定的适配配置，未回答“知识应写入何处”以及“视觉证据应如何塑造记忆”两个问题；而常规视频-语言模型需对每个问题重复编码帧或检索证据，成本高。

Method: 逐视频的测试时适配框架：在多尺度紧凑监督下，用因果追踪（causal tracing）度量各层对视频落地回答的因果记忆效用，选出视频特定的适配位置；随后进行视觉记忆蒸馏，训练纯文本记忆去复现相关视频片段所诱导的表示变化，从而在文本监督之外保留视觉证据的内部效应。

Result: 在 LVBench、LongVideoBench、Video-MME 上实现数据高效的免视频问答，所需合成样本显著少于既有参数化视频记忆方法。

Conclusion: 有效的视频内化同时取决于“知识写入位置”和“视觉证据蒸馏方式”两个维度，二者缺一不可。

Abstract: Long-video understanding often requires answering many questions about a single video whose evidence is distributed across extended temporal contexts. Conventional video-language models repeatedly encode frames or retrieve visual evidence for every question. Parameterized video memory offers a write-once alternative, but existing methods rely primarily on synthesized text and fixed adaptation configurations, leaving open where video knowledge should be written and how visual evidence should shape the memory. We introduce Memory Internalization via Test-Time Training (MIT^3), a per-video adaptation framework that writes each long video into compact, video-specific adaptive parameters for video-free, text-only question answering. Under compact multi-scale supervision, causal tracing measures the causal memory utility of each model layer for video-grounded answering and selects video-specific adaptation locations. Visual memory distillation then trains the text-only memory to reproduce the representation change induced by the relevant video clip, preserving the internal effect of visual evidence beyond textual supervision. Across LVBench, LongVideoBench, and Video-MME, MIT^3 achieves data-efficient video-free question answering with substantially fewer synthesized samples than prior parameterized video-memory methods, demonstrating that effective video internalization depends on both where knowledge is written and how visual evidence is distilled into the model.

</details>


### [5] [CodecMotion-OAD: Efficient Online Action Detection with Compressed-Domain Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26915)
*Zepeng Sun,Naichuan Zheng,Hailun Xia,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodecMotion-OAD 用编解码器提供的运动矢量（MVs）替代光流，配合两阶段训练（无标签的 MV-光流特征接口对齐 + RGB+MV 检测器蒸馏训练），在 THUMOS'14 上实现帧级在线动作检测，精度接近 RGB+Flow，同时大幅降低解码到预测的耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线动作检测（OAD）中，稠密光流提取是主要计算瓶颈；而编解码器的运动矢量在解码阶段即可免费获得。作者先量化差距：纯运动输入下光流比 MV 高 11.2 mAP，但 RGB 融合后差距缩小到 3.7 mAP，且差异主要体现在动作类别判别上，说明 MV 具备替代潜力。

Method: 提出 CodecMotion-OAD，保留现有时间检测器架构，仅将光流替换为解码器 MV。两阶段训练：第一阶段不使用动作标签，将 MV 特征与预训练光流特征接口对齐；第二阶段用真值标签和冻结的 RGB+Flow 教师模型的软预测，联合训练 RGB+MV 检测器。据称是首次将编解码 MV 用于帧级 OAD。

Result: THUMOS'14 + MiniROAD 设置下达到 68.3 mAP，对比同蒸馏设置的 RGB+Flow 的 70.9 mAP；解码到预测的处理时间在 CPU 上降低 11.48 倍，GPU 上降低 2.04 倍。

Conclusion: 编解码 MV 可作为光流的低成本替代，通过特征接口对齐与教师蒸馏弥补精度差距，在保持检测器结构不变的前提下显著提升吞吐效率；适用于对实时性敏感的流式视频理解场景。

Abstract: Online action detection (OAD) identifies ongoing actions in streaming videos. Many existing OAD systems combine RGB appearance with optical flow, but dense-flow extraction remains a major computational bottleneck. Codec motion vectors (MVs) are available during decoding, avoiding a separate optical-flow computation. On THUMOS'14, optical flow exceeds MVs by 11.2 mAP points with motion-only inputs, whereas the gap narrows to 3.7 points after RGB fusion, with the largest reduction in action-category discrimination. We propose CodecMotion-OAD, which replaces optical flow with decoder-provided MVs while retaining existing temporal detector architectures. Its two-stage training first aligns MV features with a pretrained flow-feature interface without action labels and then trains RGB+MV detectors using ground-truth labels and soft predictions from a frozen RGB+Flow teacher. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first application of codec MVs to frame-level OAD. On THUMOS'14 with MiniROAD, CodecMotion-OAD achieves 68.3 mAP versus 70.9 mAP for matched-distillation RGB+Flow, while reducing decoder-to-prediction processing time by 11.48-fold on CPU and 2.04-fold on GPU.

</details>


### [6] [GTSM: Gaussian Splatting with Thermal State-Space Dynamics for Spatio-Temporal Thermal Field Reconstruction and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26918)
*Yunpeng Zhao,Haoxiang Zhang,Chao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GTSM：以共享场景级隐过程和每个高斯的局部热状态建模热演化，用可微RK2积分传播连续动力学，可从二维多视角多时相红外观测重建并外推4D温度场，兼容3DGS/4DGS骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业与动态环境中的温度场重建与预测对状态监测和过程分析很重要；现有方法依赖预定义3D模型、热力学方程或预设升降温轨迹，无法仅从2D观测直接恢复4D时空热状态场。

Method: 提出高斯热状态空间模型（GTSM）：场景级共享隐变量刻画全局热演化，高斯基元各自的局部热状态；用可微RK2积分传播连续动力学，从而由多视角多时相观测推断热演化并向观测区间外连续外推。作为骨干无关的热动力学模块，可插入静态3DGS与动态4DGS。

Result: 在真实红外序列与合成动态热数据上，重建精度优于代表性基线，时间外推能力显著更强，并验证了对形变目标的适用性；支持新视角热渲染与未来时刻热状态预测。

Conclusion: 将热状态空间动力学与高斯表示结合，可在无需预设几何、方程与轨迹的条件下实现4D温度场重建与外推，并为热感知下的动态场景建模提供可复用模块。

Abstract: Temperature-field reconstruction and prediction are important for condition monitoring, process analysis, and state assessment in industrial and dynamic environments. This work aims to recover a four-dimensional spatio-temporal thermal state field directly from two-dimensional observations, without requiring a predefined 3D model, prescribed thermodynamic equation, or predefined heating/cooling trajectory. To this end, we propose the Gaussian Thermal State-Space Model (GTSM), which represents thermal evolution through a shared scene-level latent process and Gaussian-specific local thermal states. The resulting continuous dynamics are propagated using differentiable RK2 integration, allowing thermal evolution to be inferred from multi-view and multi-temporal observations and continuously extrapolated beyond the observed interval. GTSM is designed as a backbone-agnostic thermal dynamics module for Gaussian representations and can be integrated with both static 3D Gaussian Splatting and dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting, supporting thermal reconstruction and prediction under both static and time-varying target geometry. The reconstructed representation further enables novel-view thermal rendering and future-time thermal-state prediction. Experiments on real infrared sequences and synthetic dynamic thermal data demonstrate improved reconstruction accuracy and substantially stronger temporal extrapolation than representative baselines, while also validating the applicability of GTSM to deforming targets.

</details>


### [7] [Controllable Multi-Object Insertion for Detection Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26922)
*Jiyang Huang,Tianwei Zhang,Xiaoqin Zhang,Ling Shao,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneP2F：两阶段可训练框架，将“场景感知联合规划（ScenePlan）”与“布局对齐生成（SceneAlign-Fill）”串联，基于显式实例级布局实现可控的类别-数量精确目标插入，用于检测数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控目标插入方法难以同时满足精确类别-数量请求与视觉一致性，跨类别/实例协调不足，且对指定类别多目标的定位不准。检测数据增强需要类别-数量保真、标注对齐与源内容保持，而非单纯视觉真实感。

Method: ScenePlan：为每个请求实例分配一个带类别标签的查询，联合预测场景兼容的目标中心，再以规划中心与场景上下文为条件分别估计框尺寸。SceneAlign-Fill：结合几何与外观感知条件、框-类别引导的区域注意力，以及标注区域加权的 flow-matching 目标函数，在渲染计划目标的同时限制对源内容的非预期改动。

Result: 在 UAVDT、VisDrone、BDD100K 三个基准上，SceneP2F 一致优于代表性通用图像编辑器和目标插入方法，验证了实例级规划与生成协同对类别定向检测增强的价值。

Conclusion: 显式实例级布局规划与布局对齐生成的两阶段协同，是解决可控目标插入中类别-数量精确性与视觉一致性矛盾的有效途径，可直接服务于检测数据集扩充。

Abstract: Controllable object insertion provides a targeted approach to augmenting object detection datasets that require category--count fidelity, annotation alignment, and source-content preservation beyond visual realism. Several approaches have been proposed to address this grand challenge, but most suffer from different constraints in precise category--count requests and visual consistency, limited coordination across categories and instances, and poor localization of multiple objects of specific category and count. We design SceneP2F, a two-stage trainable framework that connects scene-aware joint planning with layout-aligned generation leveraging explicit instance-level layouts. SceneP2F consists of two novel designs. The first is ScenePlan which allocates one class-labeled query per requested instance, jointly predicts scene-compatible object centers, and separately estimates box sizes conditioned on the planned centers and scene context. The second is SceneAlign-Fill which combines geometry- and appearance-aware conditioning, box--class-guided regional attention, and an annotation-region-weighted flow-matching objective to render the planned objects while limiting unintended changes to source content. Extensive experiments on three challenging benchmarks UAVDT, VisDrone, and BDD100K show that SceneP2F outperforms representative general-purpose editors and object-insertion methods consistently, demonstrating the value of coordinating instance-level planning and generation for category-targeted detection augmentation.

</details>


### [8] [CaMeWM: A Causal Medical World Model for Generative Tumor Evolution and Treatment Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26923)
*Huanlong Gao,Xuelei He,Fengjun Zhao,Xiaowei He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaMeWM：一个因果医学世界模型，将TACE术后肿瘤演化建模为"干预→决策"闭环：先用经验治疗支持做可比历史筛选，再以动作条件响应描述符+确定性锚点+残差3D扩散生成病灶局部随访世界，最后用响应-Cox效用模型映射到RMST，并在交叉拟合AIPW下评估受限策略，得到9.60周RMST增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗规划本质是反事实的：需比较同一病人在不同方案下肿瘤与生存的演化，但回顾性队列只记录实际所给方案与单次随访影像。直接在这种数据上训练的条件生成器会复制历史治疗分配（混杂），而非模拟可用于方案排序的干预性病程动力学。

Method: 提出CaMeWM，把三个独立预测任务整合为单一 intervention-to-decision 闭环：(1) 支持性准入——仅对具有经验治疗支持的可比病史接受查询方案（positivity/可比性约束）；(2) 生成——动作条件的response descriptor、确定性anchor与残差3D扩散转移模型，输出病灶局部随访世界的分布；(3) 决策——将采样世界映射为受限平均生存时间RMST，用response-Cox效用模型实现。在预处理调整与正性假设下，该闭环估计一个预设的、支持受限的 g-formula 目标；所有选择与nuisance估计均fold-local，策略通过交叉拟合、删失校正的双稳健（AIPW）估计评估。

Result: 79个配对折外episode上，随访CT转移动SSIM 0.618、变化图SSIM 0.528；生成世界的生存通路C-index 0.615；仅用事实数据的AIPW对冻结策略给出RMST增益9.60周（95% CI 0.61–18.13）。

Conclusion: CaMeWM为回顾性、支持受限的治疗规划提供了可检查（inspectable）的基础，把生成式世界模型置于显式的因果识别框架内，而非单纯拟合历史分配。

Abstract: Treatment planning is intrinsically counterfactual: clinicians must compare how a patient's tumor and survival would evolve under competing regimens, whereas a retrospective cohort records only the regimen received and one follow-up scan. A treatment-conditioned generator trained on such data can therefore reproduce historical treatment allocation rather than simulate the interventional disease dynamics needed to rank regimens. We introduce \textbf{CaMeWM}, a causal medical world model for supported post-TACE tumor evolution and treatment planning. CaMeWM implements one intervention-to-decision loop rather than three independent prediction tasks: it first admits a queried regimen only for clinically comparable histories with empirical treatment support; it then generates a distribution of lesion-local follow-up worlds through an action-conditioned response descriptor, deterministic anchor, and residual 3D diffusion transition; and it finally maps sampled worlds to restricted mean survival time (RMST) with a response-Cox utility model. Under pre-treatment adjustment and positivity, this loop estimates a prespecified, support-restricted g-formula target; all selection and nuisance estimation are fold-local, and the induced policy is evaluated by cross-fitted, censoring-adjusted doubly robust estimation. Across 79 paired out-of-fold episodes, CaMeWM achieves 0.618 SSIM and 0.528 change-map SSIM for follow-up CT transition, while its generated-world survival pathway attains a 0.615 C-index; factual-only AIPW estimates a 9.60-week RMST gain (95\% CI 0.61--18.13) for the frozen policy. CaMeWM provides an inspectable basis for retrospective, support-restricted treatment planning.

</details>


### [9] [SuperClip Pyramid for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26925)
*Sanghyeok Lee,Juyeon Ko,Joonmyung Choi,Jongha Kim,Dohwan Ko,Ji Soo Lee,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCVTG，一种基于多尺度时间金字塔的视频时序定位框架，通过聚合相邻片段为语义连贯的SuperClip并用时间自适应核加权聚合，结合相对显著性损失，在五个基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时序定位（时刻检索MR与高光检测HD）方法多采用DETR式解码器或时间金字塔结构，但均依赖单尺度特征图或均匀降采样表示，难以捕捉语义边界与变长事件，限制了对不同粒度显著事件的建模能力。

Method: 构建SuperClip多尺度时间金字塔：将相邻clip分组为语义连贯的SuperClip段；引入时间自适应核（temporally adaptive kernel），以时间条件权重对变时长、变位置的SuperClip进行灵活聚合；并在其上设计相对显著性损失，引导模型学习clip间重要性排序。

Result: 在五个视频时序定位基准上取得最优性能，并获得显著提升。

Conclusion: 多尺度语义片段层级配合时间自适应聚合与相对显著性监督，能有效提升变长事件定位与高光检测能力；但工作聚焦视频理解任务，与具身智能、3D重建、VLA等方向关联较弱。

Abstract: With the rapid expansion of online video content, video temporal grounding has become essential for localizing moments based on user-specific queries, comprising two subtasks: moment retrieval (MR) and highlight detection (HD). While recent approaches adopt DETR-style decoders or temporal pyramid architectures, they rely on single-scale feature maps or uniformly downsampled representations, limiting their ability to capture semantic boundaries and variable-length events. In this paper, we present SuperClip Video Temporal Grounding (SCVTG) built on a multi-scale temporal hierarchy that better captures distinctive events at various granularities. SCVTG constructs a SuperClip pyramid by grouping adjacent clips into semantically coherent segments, termed SuperClips. Further, to aggregate these SuperClips having variable duration and temporal location, we introduce a temporally adaptive kernel that aggregates SuperClips through temporally conditioned weights, enabling flexible aggregation. On top of this, we design relative saliency losses to guide the model in learning inter-clip importance, enhancing its ability to distinguish relevant events. Extensive experiments show that SCVTG achieves the best performance across five video temporal grounding benchmarks with promising gains.

</details>


### [10] [Rate as a Feature, Not a Nuisance: Curriculum FiLM Training for Event-Based Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26933)
*Kavyansh Tyagi,Andreas Ziegler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以每条记录的log-rate标量经FiLM调制PointNet++各阶段的事件GNN流水线，用两阶段课程训练，在N-Caltech101与DVS128 Gesture上分别提升6.3pp和14.0pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机的事件率随场景运动、纹理、光照与对比度阈值变化，N-Caltech101内部事件率差异约26倍。现有流水线要么做逐记录时间戳归一化（不可逆，损失信息），要么训练率不变性（以精度换鲁棒性），二者都丢弃了可用信息。

Method: 以逐记录log事件率作为条件标量，通过FiLM（初始化为恒等映射，训练起点等价于原始基线）逐阶段调制PointNet++骨干网络；采用两阶段课程：先在自然事件率上训练，再做事件率增强的微调。额外参数量约0.2M。

Result: N-Caltech101达67.2%（+6.3pp），DVS128 Gesture达82.5%（+14.0pp）。逐记录归一化带来约6pp自然率精度损失。等容量对照实验表明增益来自速率条件化而非模型容量增加。

Conclusion: 事件率应作为可用的条件信息而非被归一化或不变化消除；轻量FiLM条件化配合速率课程能以极小参数代价获得一致增益，方法简单可迁移，代码将在接收后公开。

Abstract: An event camera emits events at a rate given by the scene motion, object texture, illumination, its own contrast thresholds, and other settings. Across the N-Caltech101 dataset, recorded by a single event camera, the event rate varies by roughly 26x. Standard event-based Graph Neural Network GNN pipelines do not make use of the variable event rate. They either normalize each event's timestamp into a fixed window or train the network to be invariant to the event rate. We argue that both choices lose information that could instead be used to improve the performance of a pipeline. Per-recording normalization is non-invertible, incurring about 6pp (percentage points) of natural-rate accuracy loss in our setting. And invariance training trades accuracy for robustness to shifts in the event rate. We instead take a different path and condition on the event rate directly. We add a per-recording log-rate scalar that modulates every stage of a PointNet++ backbone through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), initialized to identity, so that training begins at the unmodified baseline. A two-stage curriculum (natural event rates first, then rate-augmented fine-tuning) reaches 67.2% on N-Caltech101 (+6.3pp) and 82.5% on DVS128 Gesture (+14.0pp) with an additional ~0.2M parameters. A matched-capacity control experiment shows that the gain is not from the increase in model capacity but from the rate conditioning. We will make the implementation publicly available upon acceptance of this work.

</details>


### [11] [Deep Auxiliary Learning via Bilevel Feature Optimization for Label-Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26954)
*Fuping Wu,Xi Jia,Le Zhang,Jiahua Li,Rima Mustafa,Thomas E. Nichols,Bartlomiej W. Papiez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种双层优化（bilevel optimization）辅助学习框架，辅助任务标签自动由已有分割标注派生，无需额外人工标注，通过特征通信模块实现任务间知识迁移，在三个医学图像分割基准的少标注设置下优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助学习（AL）方法通常需要人工标注的辅助任务标签，在医学影像等专家标注昂贵的场景中成本过高。作者希望验证：能否利用从现有分割标注自动派生标签的辅助任务来提升分割性能。

Method: 将辅助学习形式化为双层优化问题，外层优化主任务目标、内层优化辅助任务，并设计特征通信模块（feature communication module）在任务间传递特征以实现有效知识迁移；辅助标签由原始分割掩膜自动生成。

Result: 在三个医学图像分割基准的有限标签设置下，方法相对现有SOTA方法取得一致提升，标签效率更优；代码已公开。

Conclusion: 自动派生辅助标签配合双层优化与特征通信，可在不增加标注成本的前提下提升分割精度与标签效率，为标注昂贵领域的辅助学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Auxiliary learning (AL), which can improve the performance of a primary task by leveraging related auxiliary tasks, is commonly regarded as a special case of multi-task learning. Existing AL approaches typically require manually annotated auxiliary labels, increasing annotation costs, particularly in medical imaging where expert labeling is expensive. In this work, we investigate whether segmentation performance can be improved using auxiliary tasks whose labels are automatically derived from existing segmentation annotations, requiring no additional manual labeling. To address this, we propose a novel auxiliary learning framework, formulated as a bilevel optimization problem. Our method enables effective knowledge transfer between tasks via a feature communication module, enhancing the primary segmentation task. We evaluate the method on three medical image segmentation benchmarks under limited-label settings. The results demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior label efficiency. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [12] [REST: Reliability-Guided Spatial Transcriptomics Supervision for Gene Expression Prediction from Histopathological HE-Stained Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26988)
*Lanfeng Zhong,Xiangyu Sun,Guixuan Xu,Rongzhao Zhang,Jie Xu,Shaoting Zhang,Guotai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 REST 框架：以生物学通路/细胞类型基因集构建 Program Bank（BPB），用 spot 级可靠性加权对比对齐 HE 与空间转录组，实现从 HE 图像预测基因表达、通路活性与细胞特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HE 形态学不直接反映分子状态；HE–ST 配对数据在检测覆盖度与 spot 信息量上不均衡，等权监督会引入噪声，需要按可靠性加权的表征学习。

Method: Program 结构化的 ST 监督框架：BPB 由 curated signature/pathway/cell-type 基因集构建，作为 ST 编码、可靠性估计与分子重建的共享结构；Spot-Reliability Contrastive Alignment（SRCA）依据 assay 级 program 覆盖度与 spot 级 activity 离散度估计可靠性并对齐加权；Biology-Informed Program Reconstruction（BIPR）用可学习 program query 从形态学重建成员基因表达。

Result: 训练集 209 样本（16 器官、407,475 spots），评测 70 个不重叠样本（8 器官、230,056 spots）。基因表达、通路活性、细胞 signature 预测上 Macro 相关最高，优于 SOTA ST 监督方法与现有病理基础模型。

Conclusion: 将异构监督可靠性显式建模与生物学先验结构结合，能有效提升 HE→ST 分子预测；可靠性重加权是关键设计，可推广到其他弱配对多组学监督场景。

Abstract: Histopathology is central to cancer diagnosis, but morphology in Hematoxylin and Eosin (HE)-stained images does not directly reveal the molecular states that characterize disease heterogeneity and inform tumor characterization, prognostic assessment, and biomarker discovery. Spatial Transcriptomics (ST) measures spatially resolved gene expression, providing molecular information within tissue architecture, yet differences in assay coverage and spot informativeness make paired HE--ST observations unequally reliable for representation learning. We propose Reliability-Guided Spatial Transcriptomics (REST) supervision with Biological Program Bank (BPB), a program-structured and reliability-guided ST-supervised training framework for predicting molecular information from H\&E images. REST constructs BPB from curated signature, pathway, and cell-type gene sets to provide a shared structure for ST encoding, reliability estimation, and molecular reconstruction. Guided by BPB, Spot-Reliability Contrastive Alignment (SRCA) measures the reliability of each spot from assay-level program coverage and spot-level activity dispersion, and reweights HE--ST alignment accordingly. Biology-Informed Program Reconstruction (BIPR) further uses learnable program queries to reconstruct member-gene expression from histological morphology. REST's training corpus comprised 209 samples (407,475 spots across 16 organs); evaluation used 70 disjoint samples (230,056 spots across eight organs) for gene-expression, pathway-activity, and cell-signature prediction. REST achieved the highest Macro correlation among state-of-the-art ST-supervised training methods and outperformed existing pathology foundation models. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [13] [Long-wave Infrared Polarimetric Remote Sensing Image Fusion for Vehicle Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27018)
*Wenjuan Zhang,Xiangrui Peng,Zhen Li,Caimeng Yi,Xuesong Ma,Jianwei Wang,Jie Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于Mamba的长波红外偏振遥感图像融合模型，结合S0与DoLP的物理先验引导特征与双向状态空间扫描实现跨模态融合，并构建AIR-LWIR-Pol数据集，提升下游车辆检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外遥感图像中车辆轮廓模糊、对比度低，限制检测性能；现有融合方法易引入偏振噪声且未能有效整合互补信息，因此需利用偏振信息（反映表面材质与几何特性）作为红外强度的互补模态。

Method: 以长波红外总强度S0与线偏振度DoLP为输入；利用受物理启发的偏振响应代理与空间结构连续性提取引导特征；采用沿水平、垂直及两条对角线方向的双向状态空间扫描实现全图跨模态融合；联合引入先验引导多窗口结构相似性、重建约束和偏振显著性相对下界损失，以抑制噪声并保留全局背景结构与目标偏振特征；同时构建AIR-LWIR-Pol数据集（1901对配准图像，机载分孔径LWIR偏振成像系统），并用YOLO11评估下游检测。

Result: 在熵、结构相似性等四项融合指标上优于对比方法；下游车辆检测mAP50为55.0%，mAP50-95为29.4%。

Conclusion: 将物理先验与状态空间建模结合用于长波红外偏振图像融合是有效的，可支撑复杂遥感场景下的目标检测。

Abstract: Blurred vehicle contours and low contrast limit detection performance in infrared remote sensing imagery, while existing fusion methods may introduce polarization noise and fail to integrate complementary information effectively. To address these limitations, we propose a Mamba-based infrared polarimetric remote sensing image fusion model. Infrared polarization information reflects surface material properties and geometry, providing complementary information to infrared intensity. The proposed method first combines long-wave infrared total intensity S0 and the degree of linear polarization (DoLP), using a physics-inspired polarization-response proxy and spatial structural continuity to extract guidance features. It then employs bidirectional state space scanning along horizontal, vertical, and both diagonal orientations to enable cross-modal feature fusion across the entire image. Prior-guided multi-window structural similarity, reconstruction constraints, and a relative lower-bound loss for polarization saliency are jointly introduced to suppress noise while preserving global background structures and target polarization features. In addition, we construct AIR-LWIR-Pol, a dataset of 1,901 accurately registered image pairs acquired using an airborne division-of-aperture long-wave infrared polarimetric imaging system, and evaluate downstream vehicle detection using YOLO11. The experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms the comparison methods on four fusion metrics, including entropy and structural similarity. Vehicle detection mAP50 and mAP50-95 reach 55.0% and 29.4%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining physical priors with state space modeling for long-wave infrared polarization image fusion and support its use for target detection in complex remote sensing scenes.

</details>


### [14] [Robustness of Internal Signals for Hallucination Detection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27041)
*Allison Chen,William Yang,Salma Abdel Magid,Jonathan Williams,Olga Russakovsky,Esin Tureci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文系统分析VLM物体幻觉检测方法的鲁棒性，发现简单的token位置基线即具竞争力，部分检测方法实为依赖token位置的捷径，且检测性能主要来自语言侧而非视觉 grounding，多方法集成可互补提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在安全关键场景中易产生物体幻觉。部分现有检测方法利用模型生成文本时的内部信号进行检测，但这些方法理论上可能使用了导致幻觉的同一信号，其鲁棒性和有效性缺乏深入检验。

Method: 开展三方面分析：(1) 以生成词在响应中的token位置作为naive基线，与现有内部信号检测方法对比，并检验方法对token位置捷径的依赖；(2) 分别扰动语言与视觉模态，评估检测方法对两类信号来源的敏感性；(3) 将不同检测方法进行集成，验证信号互补性。

Result: token位置基线达到有竞争力的检测性能；依赖token位置的检测方法表现更少视觉 grounding，当幻觉出现在文本较早位置时鲁棒性下降；强检测性能多由语言基于的幻觉驱动；集成中即使加入较弱方法也能带来性能提升。

Conclusion: VLM内部信号虽可能有助于幻觉检测，但当前方法存在依赖token位置捷径、视觉 grounding 不足等显著局限，需更严格地评估与设计检测方法。

Abstract: When given an image and a text prompt, vision-language models (VLMs) can produce seemingly sophisticated text responses. However, they are also prone to hallucinate objects in their output, which can have detrimental consequences in safety-critical applications. To detect these hallucinations, certain methods interestingly leverage internal signals from the VLM as it generates the text response; potentially, these methods use the same signals that led to a hallucination to detect it. We conduct an in-depth analysis to understand the robustness of these detection methods. First, we show that a naive baseline of a generated word's token position in the VLM text response achieves competitive performance. Further, we reveal how some detection methods rely on token position and use this heuristic as a shortcut. Second, we explore detection methods' sensitivity to language and vision modalities. Our experiments suggest that strong detection performance is often driven by detecting language-based hallucinations, and when methods rely on token position, they tend to be less visually grounded and exhibit reduced robustness when hallucinations occur earlier in a text response. Lastly, we demonstrate that different detection methods can carry complementary signals; when ensembling methods together, adding weaker methods to an ensemble of higher performing ones still offers performance improvements. Altogether, while there may be useful internal VLM signals to detect hallucinations, our investigation reveals important limitations of the current methods that try to leverage them.

</details>


### [15] [VitronEdit: Universal Visual Editing across Images, Videos, and 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27049)
*Jing Wang,Yanlin Li,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VitronEdit：以共享序列表示+渐进式多模态训练，将图像/视频编辑知识迁移到数据稀缺的3D场景编辑，并用几何感知后训练（深度与相机位姿监督）提升多视图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景编辑长期独立于图像/视频编辑，且配对3D训练数据稀缺；而图像与视频编辑监督丰富且编辑语义跨模态共通，因此希望将已有编辑知识迁移到3D。

Method: 统一框架VitronEdit：采用共享序列表示承载图像、视频与3D场景编辑；渐进式多模态训练实现跨模态知识迁移；引入几何感知后训练，借助预训练几何重建模型提供深度与相机位姿监督，且仅施加于3D样本。

Result: 仅对3D样本施加几何监督时整体效果最佳；图像与视频编辑虽无直接几何监督亦有提升，说明几何正则可通过共享主干迁移。图像/视频训练主要向3D传递编辑语义，3D监督主要提升多视图一致性。

Conclusion: 统一序列表示加渐进多模态训练可有效缓解3D编辑数据稀缺问题，几何后训练具有跨模态正则化收益；该工作为统一2D/视频/3D编辑提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in unified visual editing have begun to consolidate image and video editing within a single model, while 3D scene editing remains largely separate and constrained by scarce paired training data. Given the rich image and video editing supervision now available and the common editing semantics shared across modalities, a natural question is whether this knowledge can be transferred to data-scarce 3D editing. We present VitronEdit, a universal framework for image, video, and 3D scene editing. VitronEdit adopts a shared sequence representation and progressive multimodal training to transfer editing knowledge across modalities. We further introduce geometry-aware post-training, which leverages a pretrained geometry reconstruction model to provide depth and camera-pose supervision. Surprisingly, applying geometric supervision only to 3D samples yields the best overall performance, while image and video editing also improve despite receiving no direct geometric supervision, suggesting that geometric regularization transfers through the shared backbone. Our analysis further shows that image and video training mainly transfers editing semantics to 3D, whereas 3D supervision primarily improves multi-view consistency. The project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [16] [BUMP: Body-driven Unified Multi-object Physics for HOI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27066)
*Wonjung Woo,Hyeonwoo Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BUMP：文本驱动多物体人-物交互生成框架。先生成受语义意图驱动的全身人体运动，再条件化预测少量“驱动物体”轨迹，其余“响应物体”由物理仿真给出运动结果，从而支持任意数量周围物体而无需扩大轨迹输出空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物体HOI中运动成因异质：人体运动反映语义意图，被操作的驱动物体需与人保持协调，而周围物体运动主要由接触/碰撞的物理后果决定。统一用学习模型回归所有物体轨迹会导致输出维度随物体数膨胀且缺乏物理一致性。

Method: “学习意图 + 仿真后果”的分解式公式。①文本+场景上下文生成全身人体运动；②以人体运动为条件预测少量驱动物体的轨迹；③物理仿真（接触/碰撞）导出响应物体运动，自然扩展物体数量与构型。数据方面融合人单独运动、全身HOI、手部操作（补全合理体动）三类异质监督以缓解配对数据稀缺。另提出结果条件化交互编辑：由期望的响应物体行为反推驱动物体轨迹的局部修改，无需显式目标驱动轨迹。

Result: 在单物体与多物体HOI基准上均表现良好；定性结果显示可处理物体间相互关系及交互编辑的多样场景，验证统一公式的优越性（摘要未给出量化指标）。

Conclusion: 将HOI生成拆为“意图轨迹学习 + 物理后果仿真”是处理多物体交互的有效范式，并配合异质数据监督与结果条件化编辑实现可控生成。

Abstract: Generating human-object interactions (HOIs) with multiple objects is challenging because the resulting motions arise from different underlying causes. Human motion reflects semantic intent, intentionally manipulated objects must remain coordinated with the human, while surrounding objects move primarily as physical consequences of contact and collision. We present BUMP, a text-driven framework for multi-object HOI generation based on a simple principle: learn intentional motion and simulate physical consequences. Our method first generates full-body human motion from text and scene context, then predicts trajectories for a small number of intentionally manipulated driver objects conditioned on the human motion. We then obtain responder object motions through physics simulation, allowing the framework to handle varying numbers and configurations of surrounding objects without expanding the learned trajectory output space. To address the scarcity of paired full-body multi-object HOI data, we combine heterogeneous supervision from human-only motion, full-body HOI, and hand-centric manipulation data augmented with plausible body motion. We further introduce outcome-conditioned interaction editing, which infers a local modification of the causal driver object trajectory from a desired responder object behavior without requiring an explicit target driver object trajectory. Experiments demonstrate strong performance on both single-object and multi-object HOI benchmarks. Qualitative results showcase diverse scenarios involving inter-object relationships and edits, highlighting the advantage of our unified model formulation.

</details>


### [17] [SciFigBench: A Benchmark for Scientific Method Figure Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27087)
*Lijia Wu,Zheng Li,Yuxuan Liu,Jingwen Zhang,Penghai Zhao,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SciFigBench基准（4,352张方法图、40,670条QA），围绕密集内容感知、视觉关系理解、信息流追踪三项能力评估VLM对科学方法图的理解，并提出无需训练的视觉提示模块SciFigCue。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学方法图是论文方法论的可视化入口，但布局密集、信息流复杂，VLM难以解读；现有基准多为粗粒度任务（检索、整体理解），忽略了方法图内部的细粒度内容与模块间关系。

Method: 从真实论文中筛选4,352张方法图，由专用流水线生成并以人工校验的40,670条QA对将三项能力操作化为问答任务；同时提出SciFigCue——一种无需训练、参数更新的视觉提示模块，将箭头指示的依赖关系外化为视觉关系证据以辅助推理。

Result: 开源与闭源VLM实验显示，即便强模型在视觉关系理解上仍显著受限；SciFigCue在无参数更新情况下提升了多个任务的性能。

Conclusion: 细粒度方法图理解是当前VLM的薄弱环节，显式外化箭头的视觉关系证据可在零训练成本下带来增益；该方向值得在更复杂的真实方法图上继续深化。

Abstract: Scientific method figures often provide researchers with a clear visual entry point into a paper's methodology and core contributions, yet their dense layouts and complex information flows are difficult for Vision Language Models (VLMs) to interpret. Existing benchmarks predominantly focus on coarse-grained tasks, such as context-based retrieval or holistic comprehension, overlooking the fine-grained content and inter-module relations within complex methodological diagrams. To address these overlooked aspects, we introduce Scientific Figure Benchmark (SciFigBench), a benchmark for evaluating core capabilities required for scientific method figure understanding. Specifically, it targets three capabilities: dense content perception, visual relation comprehension, and information-flow tracing. For this purpose, we curate 4,352 scientific method figures from real-world research papers and construct 40,670 QA pairs to operationalize these capabilities as measurable question-answering tasks. All annotations are generated by dedicated pipelines and then manually verified. We further propose SciFigCue, a training-free visual prompting module that externalizes arrow-induced dependencies as visual relational evidence for method-figure reasoning. Experiments across open-source and proprietary VLMs show that even strong models remain substantially limited in visual relation comprehension, while SciFigCue improves performance across several tasks without parameter updates. The code and related resources are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [18] [LVMem: Image-Augmented Memory for Long-Horizon Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27104)
*Victoria Khoruzhaya,Maksim Kalmykov,Tuong Do Minh,Tatiana Zemskova,Yixin Zhu,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LVMem 在 ReMEmbR 风格视点记忆基础上加入图像路径链接、光流选帧(PSFR)与物体中心场景描述，并配自适应 VLM 检索工具，实现长时程时空问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需在长观测历史中对物体、事件及其位置进行语言引导感知，而现有视点记忆系统仅存储文本描述或视觉嵌入，缺乏可回溯的视觉证据与信息量充足的帧选择。

Method: 扩展 ReMEmbR 式记忆，为每条存储描述附带原始图像路径；用基于光流的帧选择策略 PSFR 替代均匀采样；存储物体中心描述性场景表示替代普通字幕；为 VLM 智能体配备自适应检索工具，返回最相关字幕对应图像，由智能体自主决定何时进行视觉检查。

Result: 在 OC-NaVQA 基准上，两个骨干模型下 LVMem 在二分类与位置指标上均优于 ReMEmbR，二分类准确率与 4D 场景图 DAAAM 基线持平或更优，且仅依赖轻量视点记忆、无需显式建图。

Conclusion: 图像路径链接、光流关键帧选择与物体中心描述的组合可在不构建图结构的轻量视点记忆中显著提升长时程时空问答，表明记忆表示与检索设计比记忆图结构更关键。

Abstract: Reliable language-guided perception is essential for embodied agents, where questions about objects, events, and their locations must be answered from long observation histories. Unlike conventional visual question answering, realistic robotic settings require reasoning over extended temporal horizons and persistent scene memory. We build upon the view-based memory paradigm and propose an efficient framework for long-horizon spatio-temporal question answering. While existing view-based systems typically store either text captions or visual embeddings, we extend the ReMEmbR-style memory with image paths that link each stored description to its original visual content. To make the stored annotations more informative, we replace uniform sampling during memory construction with an optical-flow-based frame selection strategy (PSFR) and store an object-centric descriptive scene representation instead of plain captions. We further equip a VLM-based agent with an adaptive retrieval tool that returns the image associated with the most relevant caption, allowing the agent to decide when visual inspection is needed. On the OC-NaVQA benchmark, LVMem outperforms ReMEmbR on binary and positional metrics with both backbones and matches or exceeds a 4D scene graph DAAAM baseline on binary accuracy, while operating on a lightweight view-based memory that requires no explicit graph construction.

</details>


### [19] [Counting Contributions, Not Views: Occlusion-Aware Densification for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27108)
*Marcel Rogge,René Schuster,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3DGS自适应密度控制中视锥过滤忽略遮挡的问题，提出遮挡感知过滤与平均贡献归一化，减少冗余高斯、提升遮挡场景画质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS的ADC依赖渲染时统计的每高斯梯度信息，但现有视锥可见性过滤不考虑遮挡，完全被遮挡的高斯仍被计为"已观测"，稀释平均梯度并抑制被遮挡区域的致密化。

Method: (1) 遮挡感知过滤：仅在高斯真正对某视角渲染有贡献时，才把该视角计入其致密化统计；(2) 将准则中的原始视角计数归一化项替换为平均贡献归一化项；(3) 集成到StopThePop框架中。

Result: 在遮挡严重场景上所有图像质量指标均优于遮挡盲基线（LPIPS最多降低6%），高斯数量不足基线的一半；长训练计划下数量保持有界，而多数准则会发散；小规模基准上以远低于对比方法的高斯数保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 遮挡感知的密度控制统计是提升3DGS效率与遮挡区域重建质量的有效且轻量手段，配合平均贡献归一化可显著抑制高斯膨胀。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting grows a dense scene from a sparse initialization through adaptive density control (ADC), driven by per-Gaussian statistics gathered during rendering. These statistics pass a frustum-based visibility filter blind to occlusion: fully occluded Gaussians still count as observed, diluting the averaged gradient signal and suppressing densification precisely where geometry is occluded. We correct this with an occlusion-aware filter that restricts densification statistics to views in which a Gaussian actually contributes, and replace the criterion's raw view-count normalizer with an average-contribution one. Integrated into a StopThePop-based framework, the two together improve every image-quality metric over the occlusion-blind baseline on occlusion-heavy scenes (up to 6% lower LPIPS), at less than half the Gaussians a view-count normalizer requires. Under the longer schedules these large scenes require, the count stays bounded where most criteria diverge, and on smaller benchmarks our method stays competitive at a fraction of the competitors' count.

</details>


### [20] [MatchCityLoc: Aerial Localization in LoD City Models via Surface Normal Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27110)
*Oussema Dhaouadi,Zuria Bauer,Johannes Michael Meier,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Cremers,Olaf Wysocki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatchCityLoc：从噪声6-DoF先验出发，提取图像表面法向（零样本基础模型）与渲染LoD法向做显式3D-2D匹配，PnP求解+渲染-匹配迭代精化，实现城市场景对无纹理LoD模型的轻量、隐私友好定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市GNSS不可靠，视觉定位需依赖城市模型；带纹理三维网格笨重且涉隐私，无纹理LoD模型轻量隐私友好，但现有方法基于2D轮廓重叠打分，几何歧义严重。

Method: 用零样本基础模型从查询图像预测表面法向图，与渲染LoD几何的法向图建立显式3D-2D对应，进而用PnP解算位姿，并通过render-and-match循环迭代精化；整个流程由嘈杂6-DoF先验驱动，渲染次数极少。

Result: 在同类即插即用LoD基方法中取得较高位姿召回率与准确平移/旋转精度，且渲染开销很小。

Conclusion: 通过从轮廓匹配范式转向法向特征匹配，弥合航拍影像与无纹理几何间的模态差距，使轻量隐私保护城市模型可用于精确定位。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) navigation in urban environments requires precise pose estimation. Because Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals are often unreliable in cities, visual localization serves as a crucial complement. Within this domain, textureless Level-of-Detail (LoD) city models provide a lightweight and privacy-preserving substitute for heavy 3D textured meshes. However, localizing against abstract geometry is challenging. Existing pipelines typically score 2D silhouette overlaps, which can suffer from severe geometric ambiguities. We present MatchCityLoc, a framework that shifts this paradigm to direct feature matching from a noisy 6-DoF pose prior. By extracting surface normals from the query image using a zero-shot foundation model and matching them against rendered LoD normals, we establish explicit 3D-to-2D correspondences. This enables a Perspective-n-Point (PnP) pose solve, which we iteratively refine through a render-and-match loop. Experiments demonstrate strong pose recall and accurate translation and rotation among the evaluated off-the-shelf LoD-based methods with very few renderings. By bridging the modality gap between aerial imagery and texture-free geometry, MatchCityLoc enables accurate localization using lightweight, privacy-preserving city models.

</details>


### [21] [PRIMFLOW: Learning Trajectory Primitives for Few-Step Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27146)
*Hongyi Fang,Benjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种少步轨迹蒸馏方法，直接学习表示教师采样轨迹上累积位移的连续"轨迹基元"，以其有限差分匹配局部教师速度，再用端点差分获得全局更新，并给出去中心化局部到全局误差界与"端点吸收"以保持单次网络评估推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步轨迹蒸馏依赖特定轨迹参数化或离散区间预测来构造步内动力学，将学习到的更新绑定在预设时间结构上；作者希望摆脱固定时间基和查询网格，直接从局部监督中学到累积位移的时间演化。

Method: 用连续轨迹基元参数化累积位移；以基元有限差分与教师局部速度的回归作为监督；端点差分给出全局更新；理论上建立局部到全局误差界刻画局部回归误差如何传播到全区间位移精度；提出端点吸收，将辅助基元分支吸收进主干，去掉辅助分支同时保留一次网络评估（1 NFE）推理。

Result: 大规模文生图实验中，1 NFE 下取得 T-FID 20.92、HPSv2 28.35，两项指标均优于轨迹蒸馏基线；经端点吸收后性能基本保持，T-FID 20.86、HPSv2 28.33。

Conclusion: 以连续累积位移基元替代离散/固定时间基参数化，可在保持 1 NFE 推理的前提下改进少步蒸馏的生成质量，且局部误差界为局部监督的有效性提供理论支撑；属于扩散/流匹配加速的通用方法论，不含机器人、3D 重建或具身实体验证。

Abstract: Few-step trajectory distillation aims to learn accurate global updates from dense local supervision along a teacher's sampling trajectory. Existing approaches construct within-step dynamics using specific trajectory parameterizations or discrete interval predictions, tying the learned update to a prescribed temporal structure. We propose \method, which directly learns a continuous trajectory primitive representing cumulative displacement along the teacher trajectory. By supervising finite differences of the primitive with local teacher velocities, \method learns the accumulated motion directly, while obtaining global updates through endpoint differences. This formulation avoids prescribing a temporal basis or fixed query grid and instead learns the temporal evolution of cumulative displacement from local supervision. We further establish a local-to-global error bound to characterize how local regression errors affect full-interval displacement accuracy, and introduce endpoint absorption to eliminate the auxiliary primitive branch while preserving one-network-evaluation inference. Experiments on large-scale text-to-image generation show that \method achieves a T-FID of 20.92 and an HPSv2 score of 28.35 at one NFE, improving both metrics over a trajectory-distillation baseline. After endpoint absorption, it retains comparable performance, achieving a T-FID of 20.86 and an HPSv2 score of 28.33.

</details>


### [22] [CARLAR Influence: A Structured Hallucination Risk in Multimodal Cheque Extraction and an Exploratory Compressed-Image Countermeasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27192)
*Joong Ho,Choi,Aditya Paul,Nidhi Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究多模态大模型在金融票据视觉歧义下的"金额串扰"（CARLAR）现象：模型对支票的礼貌金额与法定金额返回相同数值；并在10种视觉提示条件下评测GPT-4.1与Claude 4.5 Sonnet，提出压缩图像少样本表格干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM越来越多用于金融文档结构化抽取，但其在视觉歧义（字段遮挡/空白标注被编码为0）下的行为缺乏系统刻画，字段间依赖导致的错误可能被高置信度掩盖。

Method: 定义CARLAR指标（两字段预测一致的比率），在243张主支票（124张存在两个可观测且不等的金额）及来自两个额外数据集的598个严格不匹配样本上，跨10种视觉提示条件评测GPT-4.1与Claude 4.5 Sonnet；报告bootstrap区间与显著性检验、按模型自报置信度计算ECE；并提出免微调的"压缩图像+单张视觉表格少样本示例"干预。

Result: 礼貌金额涂黑条件下GPT-4.1的CARLAR达73.7%（95%CI 68.3–79.0，p<0.001）；预测一致时自报置信度仍很高，CARLAR样本中ECE最高达0.93。干预在243例队列上使Claude的成对联合抽取准确率提升11.5个百分点（95%CI 6.2–16.9，校正p=0.00054），但在严格124例子集增益较小且校正后不显著；对字段一致性的影响依模型而异（Claude下降、GPT-4.1上升）。

Conclusion: 多模态LLM在结构化抽取中会因视觉上下文产生字段间串扰，且置信度校准失效；需发展考虑视觉上下文与字段依赖关系的验证/评测方法。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for structured extraction from financial documents, but their behavior under visual ambiguity is poorly understood. We study CARLAR (Courtesy Amount Recognition and Legal Amount Recognition) influence: a model returns the same amount for both cheque fields even though their annotations differ, including cases with a blank annotation encoded as zero. Across ten visual prompting conditions, we evaluate GPT-4.1 and Claude 4.5 Sonnet on 243 primary cheques (124 with two observed unequal amounts) and 598 strict mismatches from two additional datasets. Under courtesy redaction, GPT-4.1 CARLAR reaches 73.7 percent (95 percent bootstrap interval: 68.3 to 79.0; p below 0.001 versus baseline). Model-reported confidence can remain high when predictions agree; expected calibration error reaches 0.93 among CARLAR cases. We also test a compressed-image few-shot intervention that presents examples as one visual table without model fine-tuning. On the 243-case cohort, Claude's Control condition increases paired joint extraction accuracy by 11.5 percentage points (95 percent interval: 6.2 to 16.9; adjusted p = 0.00054), but its strict-124 gain is smaller and not significant after correction. The intervention's effect on predicted-field agreement is model-dependent: it decreases for Claude and increases for GPT-4.1. These results motivate validation methods that account for visual context and dependencies between extracted fields.

</details>


### [23] [Language Guidance for Supervised Vision Training: An Empirical Study of Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27208)
*Shruthi Gowda,Nikita Jain,Elahe Arani,Bahram Zonooz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估预训练语言模型作为视觉训练轻量辅助监督（显式ExLG / 隐式ImLG），无需图文配对数据、提示工程或对比目标，在六类泛化场景下比较两种语言引导机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督视觉训练依赖one-hot标签，语义结构信息有限，导致表征对分布偏移、虚假相关、纹理偏置、对抗扰动和序贯遗忘脆弱；作者希望验证是否可低成本复用现成预训练语言模型提供辅助监督。

Method: 提出并对比两种语言引导形式：显式语言引导（ExLG）与隐式语言引导（ImLG），不依赖图像-文本配对、prompt工程或对比学习目标；在六类泛化设置（ID、OOD泛化、捷径与虚假相关抵抗、纹理/形状偏置、对抗鲁棒性、持续学习）上系统评测。

Result: 两种机制优势互补：显式引导稳定提升ID性能、低数据表现与持续学习保持率；隐式引导在捷径敏感场景和更强对抗扰动下更有效。二者均参数与训练开销极小。

Conclusion: 语言派生语义结构可作为视觉监督的有效补充，为跨模态复用现成预训练模型作为辅助监督提供了实用路线图。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have achieved remarkable success on vision benchmarks, yet they continue to struggle with many generalization challenges. Supervised vision training relies on one-hot labels, which provide limited information about semantic structure and shared attributes between classes. This limited supervision can leave visual representations vulnerable to distribution shifts, spurious correlations, texture bias, adversarial perturbations, and forgetting in sequential learning settings. We study whether pretrained language models can serve as lightweight auxiliary supervision for vision training without requiring paired image-text data, prompt engineering, or contrastive objectives. Specifically, we evaluate two forms of language guidance, Explicit Language Guidance (ExLG) and Implicit Language Guidance (ImLG). We conduct a comprehensive evaluation across six generalization regimes, including in-distribution, out-of-distribution generalization, shortcut and spurious correlation resistance, texture and shape bias, adversarial robustness, and continual learning. Our analyses show that the two mechanisms have complementary strengths, with explicit guidance consistently benefiting in-distribution, low-data performance, and continual learning retention, while implicit guidance is often more useful in shortcut-sensitive settings and under stronger adversarial perturbations. Importantly, both are lightweight and add minimal parameters and training overhead. The analyses characterize when language-derived structure helps supervised vision training and provides a practical roadmap for using off-the-shelf pretrained models from another modality as auxiliary supervision.

</details>


### [24] [Generalizing Multimodal Foundational Models in Context-Driven Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27298)
*Avi Gupta,Nilotpal Sinha,Vishnu Raj,Sambuddha Saha,Pratik Joshi,Koteswar Rao Jerripothula,Tammam Tillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将冻结的多模态基础模型（MFM）泛化到音视频类增量学习（AV-CIL）的框架：以密集音频表示为视觉表示提供任务稳定的上下文引导，并结合特征级与logit级蒸馏抑制灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频交叉模态下的类增量学习研究不足；作者初步实验发现，MFM表示虽蕴含丰富静态先验，但在增量场景中表现欠佳，直接使用难以奏效。

Method: 保留预训练MFM编码器冻结，提取稠密音频与视觉特征；设计基于注意力的上下文引导模块，将音频特征作为任务稳定的上下文注入视觉分支以增强新类识别；同时采用特征级与logit级双重蒸馏约束跨任务稳定性。

Result: 在多个音视频CIL基准上一致优于现有SOTA方法；消融显示蒸馏（特征级+logit级）是遗忘降低的主要贡献来源。

Conclusion: 冻结多模态基础模型配合跨模态上下文引导与蒸馏，可有效实现音视频类增量学习，为多模态增量学习提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) aims to learn new classes continuously without forgetting the previously learned ones. Recent works in CIL have significantly advanced across various modalities; however, at the intersection of audio-visual modality, this problem remains underexplored. While representations from multimodal foundational models (MFMs) encapsulate rich static priors, our preliminary analysis shows that these representations struggle in incremental settings. Motivated by this, we propose a framework that generalizes frozen MFMs to audio-visual CIL. Specifically, we leverage their dense audio and visual representations and employ an attention-based contextual guidance module, in which the audio representations serve as task-stable contextual guidance for the visual representations, enabling effective class recognition in the incremental setting. To further mitigate catastrophic forgetting and preserve stability across tasks, we employ feature- and logit-level distillation, which are primarily responsible for the reduced forgetting. Extensive evaluations across various audio-visual CIL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [What Not to Erase: Saliency-Constrained Augmentation for Low-Resource Handwritten Character Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27309)
*Paraskevi Platanou,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究低资源历史手写字符识别中的显著性引导数据增强，提出可解释性一致性指标ECI，发现显著性约束增强可提升识别率与解释稳定性，但最优策略随架构与语料而变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源历史材料OCR/HCR的数据增强通常仅以预测精度评价，无法判断模型性能提升是否伴随显著性（解释）稳定性；历史字形多变且退化，预测性能相近的模型在扰动下敏感性可能差异显著。

Method: 将显著性图从后验解释工具转为增强设计的依据：对字符图像中视觉信息区域分别做扰动、保留、约束，比较经典文档风格变换、随机缺损（lacunae）及多种显著性约束缺损变体；同时提出可解释性一致性指数（ECI）量化受控扰动下显著性模式的稳定性。

Result: 显著性约束增强在识别精度上可优于经典增强，但没有单一显著性策略始终最优；偏好约束形式随模型架构与语料变化；部分设置中预测性能相近时，显著性约束训练仍提升解释一致性。

Conclusion: 显著性宜作为控制增强的机制而非固定增强策略；仅用预测指标会掩盖扰动下敏感性差异，解释一致性应作为补充评价维度。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) and handwritten character recognition (HCR) in low-resource historical material collections often rely on data augmentation, yet augmentation strategies are typically evaluated almost exclusively through predictive accuracy. This leaves open whether a model's improved performance is accompanied by saliency stability. This question is important in low-resource historical HCR, where recognition is performed on variable and partially degraded letterforms and two models with similar predictive performance may differ in how consistently their saliency patterns respond to such variation. We therefore study saliency-guided augmentation as an explanation-aware strategy for low-resource HCR. Rather than treating saliency maps only as post-hoc interpretations, we use them to define augmentation variants that perturb, preserve, or constrain visually informative regions of character images. We compare classical document-style transformations, random lacunae, and several saliency-constrained lacuna variants. To evaluate the effect of augmentation beyond accuracy, we introduce the Explainability Consistency Index (ECI), a metric that quantifies the stability of saliency patterns under controlled perturbations. We find that saliency-constrained augmentation can improve recognition over classical augmentation, but no single saliency strategy is consistently best. The preferred form of the constraint varies with both architecture and corpus, indicating that saliency is better treated as a mechanism for controlling augmentation than as a fixed augmentation strategy. In some settings, saliency-constrained training improves explanation consistency even when predictive performance is similar. This shows that predictive metrics alone can obscure differences in model sensitivity under perturbation, motivating explanation consistency as a complementary evaluation dimension.

</details>


### [26] [Steering Vision-Language Models for Visual-Contextual Privacy in Location Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27323)
*Wenyue Chen,Yuhan Cheng,Ruixin Yang,Ethan Mendes,Alan Ritter,Jingwei Sun,Hai Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究VLM的地理位置推断隐私，提出"按视觉上下文匹配披露粒度"的框架：从生成前隐状态中恢复有序的披露级别方向，用序数路由+路由条件隐状态干预在解码时控制披露粒度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM可从细微视觉线索推断地理位置等敏感信息，回复常比图像上下文所允许的粒度更细，构成视觉语境隐私风险；需在推理时实现"上下文明智"的披露粒度控制。

Method: 先实证表明上下文合适的粒度可从生成前隐状态恢复（即便输出不匹配），且披露等级沿共同隐状态方向呈有序结构；据此提出推理时分流-引导框架：序数粒度路由器将视觉语境隐私表征映射到披露路径，披露级别条件的引导模块在解码中施加路径特定的隐状态干预。

Result: 在四个VLM骨干上提升视觉语境感知的披露对齐，更好平衡过度与不足披露，通用能力基准上仅有适度变化；并可迁移到数据分布和隐私风险分类体系不同的另一数据集。

Conclusion: 隐状态中存在可读的有序粒度信号，支持无需重训练的推理时激活引导来实现语境感知的隐私披露控制，且具跨域迁移性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can infer sensitive information such as geolocation from subtle visual cues, creating privacy risks when responses disclose more detail than the visual context warrants. We study visual-contextual privacy in geolocation disclosure, where a VLM should reveal location information only at the granularity appropriate to the visual context depicted in the image. We first show that this contextually appropriate granularity is recoverable from pre-generation hidden states, even when generated responses are misaligned, with disclosure levels exhibiting an ordered structure along a common hidden-state direction. Motivated by this, we propose an inference-time route-and-steer framework: an ordinal granularity router maps visual-contextual privacy representations to a disclosure route, and a disclosure-level-conditioned steering module applies route-specific hidden-state interventions during decoding. Across four VLM backbones, our method improves visual-context-aware disclosure alignment, better balances over- and under-disclosure, and incurs only modest changes on general capability benchmarks. It further transfers to another dataset with a distinct data distribution and privacy-risk taxonomy.

</details>


### [27] [Look Before You Reason: Implicit Visual Thinking for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27341)
*Zerui Chen,Changrui Chen,Fei Ni,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoRe 提出隐式视觉思维框架：单轮推理内预测最相关图像区域，将区域与图像 patch 特征对齐并增强对应 KV cache，配合强化学习引导模型定位并利用信息区域，无需多轮工具调用；同时发布 COCO-ObjQA 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"用图像思考"的方法依赖缩放、裁剪或代码执行等多轮交互，带来冗余 token 计算与推理延迟，并把区域检查与依赖它的推理步骤割裂开来。

Method: 单轮交互框架：先预测与问题最相关的图像区域，映射到对应 patch 特征并强化相关 KV cache 条目，使后续解码直接利用选定视觉线索；再用强化学习训练模型自主发现并利用关键区域。另构建对象中心的多模态推理基准 COCO-ObjQA。

Result: 在三个具挑战性的多模态基准上，LoRe 稳定提升 VLM 推理性能，且额外推理开销低。

Conclusion: 通过在单轮内完成区域选择与 KV cache 引导，可在保持高效的同时获得接近或优于多轮视觉工具方法的细粒度视觉推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly in multimodal reasoning, yet they remain limited on tasks requiring fine-grained understanding of small or easily overlooked image regions. Recent thinking with images approaches mitigate this limitation by allowing models to inspect images through operations such as zooming, cropping, or code execution. While effective, their multi-turn interactions with VLMs introduce redundant token computation and slow inference, and separate region inspection from the reasoning step that depends on it. To address these issues, we propose Look Before You Reason (LoRe), an implicit visual thinking framework for efficient visual reasoning within a single-turn VLM interaction. Given an image and a question, LoRe predicts the most relevant image region, aligns it with the corresponding image patch features, and strengthens the associated key-value (KV) cache entries, enabling subsequent decoding to directly exploit the selected visual cues without additional visual inputs or explicit tool calls. We further employ reinforcement learning to guide the VLM toward identifying informative image regions and leveraging them during reasoning. To support fine-grained evaluation of multimodal reasoning, we introduce COCO-ObjQA, a large-scale benchmark for object-centric multimodal reasoning. Experiments on three challenging multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that LoRe consistently improves VLM reasoning performance with low additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [28] [On the Limits of Scale: Inverse Scaling in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27360)
*Rohit Saxena,Alessandro Suglia,Marek Rei,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统发现视觉语言模型（VLM）在子任务层面存在"逆缩放"现象：同一模型族内更大模型在特定问题类别上反而比小模型表现更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律通常认为模型越大越好，但作者想检验这种单调性在VLM的子任务/问题类别层面是否成立，并评估其是否是任务驱动而非模型特异的。

Method: 评估4个模型族的19个模型、8个多模态基准（9个评测split），每个模型回答29,386题，共558,334次模型-问题评测；对族内所有模型对做逐题配对分析，统计逆缩放的子任务-模型对组合，并检验跨架构、跨族的复现性。

Result: 识别出375个逆缩放的子任务-模型对组合，覆盖4,342个唯一查询（小模型答对、大模型答错）；13个子任务-数据集对在多个模型族中复现。受影响最严重的是多图空间推理、视觉感知、大学水平STEM知识，精度差距部分超过20个百分点；图表理解相对稳健。

Conclusion: 增大模型规模并不能一致提升多模态能力，逆缩放是任务驱动且跨架构可复现的现象，模型选择应当任务感知。

Abstract: Scaling laws suggest that increasing model size, training data, and compute generally yields monotonic performance improvements. While this often holds at the aggregate level, we present systematic evidence that vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit inverse scaling at the subtask level: specific question categories on which larger models perform worse than smaller counterparts within the same model family. We evaluate 19 models from four distinct model families across eight multimodal benchmarks (nine evaluation splits), answering 29,386 questions per model, 558,334 model-question evaluations in total. Using per-question pairwise analysis across all model pairs in each family, we identify 375 subtask-model-pair combinations exhibiting inverse scaling, spanning 4,342 unique queries where a smaller model answers correctly but a larger model fails, and we find that these patterns replicate across independently developed architectures. We identify 13 subtask-dataset pairs that show inverse scaling across multiple families, consistent with the phenomenon being task-driven rather than model-specific. The most affected domains in our study include multi-image spatial reasoning, visual perception, and university-level STEM knowledge, with accuracy gaps exceeding 20 percentage points in several cases, while diagram understanding appears comparatively resistant. These findings challenge the assumption that increasing model size uniformly improves multimodal capabilities and suggest that model selection should be task-aware.

</details>


### [29] [PerQ: Inverse Generative Modeling for Neural Image Compression via Quantization Error Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27374)
*Tianhao Peng,Ho Man Kwan,Fan Zhang,Shan Liu,Dave Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerQ 将感知重建重构为“有界支撑的逆生成建模”：把量化单元视为产生同一传输符号的隐变量集合，并推导传输界证明该单元内重建与编解码输出的距离受码率约束，从而在码率诱导的量化单元内做生成式重建，而非在无约束图像先验上采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经图像编解码器存在像素保真与感知质量的固有权衡：失真优化码流低码率下重建模糊，生成式码流感知质量好但高码率 PSNR 饱和，且不同码率需各自训练模型。作者希望在保持骨干编码性能的前提下实现感知增强，并用单一模型覆盖宽码率范围。

Method: 理论层面定义量化单元（映射到同一传输符号的隐变量集合），推导传输界（transport bound）说明单元内重建被限制在由码率决定的半径内。实现上在冻结的多码率失真优化骨干上附加码率条件的 flow-matching 补偿器，在该量化单元内执行生成式重建。

Result: 在 Kodak 与 CLIC2020 上，LPIPS、FID 与 MS-ILLM 等生成式码流相当，PSNR 与 ELIC 等失真优化码流保持竞争力；单一 checkpoint 覆盖宽码率范围且解码快；补偿器在三种不同骨干上均带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 把生成式重建约束在编解码器诱导的量化单元内，是兼顾像素保真与感知质量、并实现单模型多码率的有效范式；模块化补偿器设计可即插即用于现有失真优化骨干。

Abstract: Neural image codecs face a fundamental trade-off between pixel fidelity and perceptual quality. Distortion-optimized codecs preserve pixel-wise fidelity but produce blurred reconstructions at low bitrates, whereas generative codecs leverage learned priors to improve perceptual quality but tend to exhibit saturating PSNR at higher bitrates and require separate models for different bitrate targets. We propose PerQ, which reframes perceptual reconstruction as inverse generative modeling with bounded support. Specifically, we define the quantization cell as the set of latents that yield the same transmitted symbols and derive a transport bound showing that reconstructions from this cell remain within a rate-determined radius of the codec output. This motivates performing generative reconstruction within the codec-induced quantization cell, rather than sampling from an unconstrained image prior. PerQ implements this principle by attaching a rate-conditioned flow-matching compensator to a frozen, multi-rate distortion-optimized backbone, enabling perceptual enhancement while preserving the backbone's coding performance. Experiments on Kodak and CLIC2020 show that PerQ achieves perceptual performance comparable to generative codecs such as MS-ILLM in LPIPS and FID, while maintaining competitive PSNR relative to distortion-optimized codecs such as ELIC. A single model checkpoint supports a wide bitrate range with fast decoding, and the compensator provides consistent gains across three different backbones. The implementation is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [30] [Test-time Adaptation for Point Cloud Denoising under Unseen Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27410)
*Kun Jiang,Shida Fu,Fanhao Yi,Weiqing Li,Zhiyong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出测试时自适应点云去噪框架TTAD：用残差驱动噪声建模(RDNM)估计样本级噪声分布，再用噪声感知对比自适应(NACA)构造"噪声-更强噪声"自监督对并在测试时做少步微调，另扩展出面向同分布流的持续版本CTTAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习点云去噪方法在真实场景中因测试噪声分布偏离训练分布而性能骤降；且无法获得干净真值、也不便于从源数据重训。

Method: 两阶段测试时自适应：RDNM通过噪声输入与初始去噪伪干净版本之间的残差估计样本特定噪声分布；NACA基于去噪结果合成"噪声—更强噪声"测试时训练对，以对比学习在每个样本上执行少量参数更新；并自然扩展到连续测试样本流下的CTTAD，实现渐进式精炼并降低单样本计算开销。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著提升现有去噪模型在未见噪声下的泛化能力，多项评价指标上取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 无需额外监督或源数据重训的测试时自适应可有效缓解点云去噪的噪声分布偏移问题，并可低成本地扩展到持续测试场景。

Abstract: Although deep learning-based point cloud denoising methods have demonstrated impressive results in recent years, their effectiveness usually degrades substantially in real-world scenarios where the noise distribution of test point clouds deviates from that of the training set. To address this challenge, we propose a test-time adaptive denoising (TTAD) framework that enables existing denoising approaches to adapt to unknown noise patterns at test time without relying on additional supervision or retraining from source data. Taking a pre-trained denoising model and a test noisy point cloud as input, TTAD first employs a residual-driven noise modeling (RDNM) module to estimate the sample-specific noise distribution through analyzing the deviation between the noisy input and its pseudo-clean version denoised by the initial model. Then, a noise-aware contrastive adaptation (NACA) module is designed to build sample-specific noisy-noisier test-time training pair from the denoised point cloud, and update the model parameters over a few adaptation steps per sample at test time through contrastive learning. Furthermore, TTAD naturally extends to a continual TTAD (CTTAD) setting, facilitating efficient progressive refinement across sequential test samples with consistent noise distributions, while reducing the computational cost per sample. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly improves the generalization ability of existing denoising models under unseen noise, achieving superior performance across multiple evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [31] [TokenFlow: Latent Token Rectified Flow Matching for 3D Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27418)
*Linhao Wang,Dongjin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenFlow用M≪N个潜在token表示形状，联合rectified flow（token）与条件flow（点），以O(NM)代价实现分层点云生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜变量生成模型将整形状压成单一向量，丢失部件级结构；同时需保持点集置换等变性。

Method: 层次化flow matching：形状编码为潜在token集合，经交叉注意力O(NM)聚合全局上下文；对token施加rectified flow，对点施加以token为条件的flow。

Result: ShapeNet上airplane/car/chair取得最佳MMD与coverage，1-NNA与最强基线相当；ModelNet10线性评估准确率最佳。

Conclusion: 以潜在token集合、O(NM)聚合的形状表示是有效的3D点云生成表征。

Abstract: 3D point clouds generation requires capturing both part-level layout and surface detail while preserving permutation equivariance. Existing latent generative models often encode an entire shape into a single vector, which limits their ability to retain part-level structure. We propose TokenFlow, a hierarchical flow matching framework that represents each shape as a set of latent tokens and couples a rectified flow over these tokens with a conditional flow over points. A bottleneck of $M \ll N$ learnable tokens aggregates global context through cross-attention at $O(NM)$ cost. On ShapeNet, TokenFlow achieves the best MMD and coverage across airplane, car, and chair, while obtaining 1-NNA comparable to the strongest baselines. On ModelNet, its learned latent set reaches the best reported linear-evaluation accuracy on ModelNet10. These results show that representing a shape as a set of latent tokens, aggregated at $O(NM)$ cost, provides an effective representation for 3D point cloud generation.

</details>


### [32] [LoRASpace: A Pool-Wide Shared Substrate for Static and Dynamic Multi-LoRA Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27444)
*Thamirawaran Sathiyalogeswaran,Navindu De Silva,Chamikara Siriwardane,W. K. Alwis,Jason Mars,Udaya Sampath K. Perera Miriya Thanthrige,Ranga Rodrigo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoRA池的堆叠低秩因子存在大量方向重叠（SVD保留99%能量仅需46%-57%的秩），据此提出免训练LoRASpace，将子空间每池计算一次并复用于静态合并与动态路由两种推理模式，在保持composite质量的同时实现合并级成本，动态模式比composite快6倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多LoRA组合存在两难：权重合并（merge）只需一次前向但不同概念的LoRA互相干扰导致质量下降；逐LoRA独立前向（composite）保质量但推理时间随活跃LoRA数线性增长。作者想利用LoRA池中被忽视的结构性冗余来打破该权衡。

Method: 先对LoRA池的堆叠低秩因子做SVD，刻画保留99%因子能量所需的最小公共子空间维度占秩和的比例；再提出免训练方法LoRASpace，每池一次性计算该子空间基，并提供静态模式（等价合并成本）与动态模式（输入相关路由）。在三个扩散模型LoRA池、LoRA-Hub(FLAN-T5)子池和LoRA-Retriever基准上评估，并做同秩静态变体消融。

Result: 三个扩散池仅需秩和的46%-57%，子空间随池规模亚线性增长；LoRA-Hub上48个LoRA子池重叠为62%，而多样性策展的LoRA-Retriever接近无重叠基线(96%)，说明池组成而非模态决定压缩空间。静态LoRASpace以合并成本达到composite质量；在17-LoRA池上激活6个LoRA时动态模式比composite快6倍以上（单次骨干前向替代六次）。消融显示同秩静态变体质量相当，增益不依赖池级基；近正交基准上压缩很小，收益取决于外部检索耦合。

Conclusion: LoRA池的低秩因子高度重叠是可被系统利用的压缩结构，但压缩程度由池的组成而非模态决定；LoRASpace提供了训练无关、可落地的多LoRA推理加速方案，对多样性/近正交池收益有限。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) attaches small low-rank matrices to a frozen backbone, and public repositories host many LoRAs per backbone, each for a different concept or task. Combining several LoRAs in one generation forces a trade-off. Merging their weights into one update needs a single forward pass, but LoRAs trained for different concepts interfere and quality drops. Running each LoRA in its own forward pass (composite) preserves quality, but inference time grows linearly with the number of active LoRAs. We study a property of LoRA pools overlooked by prior multi-LoRA work. In real LoRA pools, the stacked low-rank factors need far fewer directions than their summed ranks. Using singular value decomposition, we find the minimal subspace that keeps 99% of the factor energy. On three diffusion pools, only 46% to 57% of the summed LoRA ranks is needed, and the subspace grows sub-linearly with pool size. A 48-LoRA sub-pool of LoRA-Hub on FLAN-T5 tasks shows comparable overlap (62%), while the diversity-curated LoRA-Retriever benchmark sits near the no-overlap baseline (96%), so pool composition rather than modality predicts where compression is large. We build on this overlap with LoRASpace, a training-free method that computes the subspace once per pool and reuses it in two inference modes, a static mode at merge cost and a dynamic mode for input-dependent routing. On the diffusion pools, static LoRASpace matches composite quality at merge cost, and dynamic LoRASpace is more than 6x faster than composite with six active LoRAs on a 17-LoRA pool, mainly because it runs one backbone pass instead of six. In an ablation, static variants at the same rank match LoRASpace, so the quality gain does not depend on the pool-wide basis. On the near-orthogonal LoRA-Retriever benchmark, compression is small and gains come from coupling with external retrieval.

</details>


### [33] [FocusOVD: Geometric and Contextual Feature Refinement for Open-Vocabulary Small-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27479)
*Hanlin Kou,Beiqi Bai,Li Chen,Youjia Jing,Shupan Li,Mingyuan Jiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FocusOVD: 不改动冻结VLM主干，通过重加权跨尺度融合、残差风车门控卷积、残差卷积门控线性单元三个模块强化开放词汇小目标检测表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇检测方法在小目标上表现差，原因在于高分辨率细节保留不足、跨层信息聚合低效、RoI区域级判别力有限。

Method: 在冻结的VLM主干上添加三个互补模块：(1) Reweighted Cross-scale Feature Fusion，学习归一化层级权重自适应平衡浅层外观细节与深层语义；(2) Residual Pinwheel-shaped Gated Convolution，增强高分辨率特征中的局部几何响应；(3) Residual Convolutional Gated Linear Unit，在预测前抑制背景主导的激活并提升RoI判别力。

Result: COCO-OVD上新类AP 36.3%，分别超CORA、UniDetector、SAMP 1.3/1.1/1.5个点；LVIS-OVD总体AP 33.7%，超最强对比方法0.6个点。

Conclusion: 三个轻量模块的组合有效提升了开放词汇小目标检测性能，且无需微调VLM主干，具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection aims to detect both seen and unseen categories by leveraging the semantic knowledge of vision-language models. However, existing methods often struggle with small objects due to insufficient preservation of high-resolution details, ineffective information aggregation across feature levels, and limited region-level discriminability. To address these challenges, we propose FocusOVD, an open-vocabulary object detector that enhances small-object representations without modifying the frozen VLM backbone. FocusOVD introduces three complementary components: a Reweighted Cross-scale Feature Fusion module, which learns normalized level-wise weights to adaptively balance shallow appearance details and deep semantic cues during feature pyramid aggregation; a Residual Pinwheel-shaped Gated Convolution module, which strengthens local geometric responses in high-resolution features; and a Residual Convolutional Gated Linear Unit module, which suppresses background-dominated activations and enhances RoI discriminability before prediction. Extensive experiments on COCO-OVD and LVIS-OVD demonstrate the effectiveness of FocusOVD. On COCO-OVD, FocusOVD achieves a novel-category AP of 36.3%, outperforming CORA, UniDetector, and SAMP by 1.3%, 1.1%, and 1.5%, respectively; on LVIS-OVD, it achieves an overall AP of 33.7%, surpassing the strongest competing method by 0.6%. These results demonstrate the strong generalization ability of FocusOVD for open-vocabulary small-object detection.

</details>


### [34] [Cross-Visibility-Gap Identity Persistence for 4D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27505)
*Jiahui Hu,Li Gao,Yu Liu,Pengzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVG-SGM is a perception-frontend-compatible transfer–maintain–read memory module for persistent object identity across visibility gaps (object hidden inside an opaque carrier and later re-detected).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D场景图中，当物体被不透明载体遮挡消失、随后以全新检测重现时，需要维持持久身份；此时外观与目标中心运动线索不可靠，只能依赖关系历史，而该问题此前缺乏形式化定义与专用方法。

Method: 将跨可见性间隙的身份持续问题形式化为"通过记忆中的目标–载体绑定进行历史节点恢复"；提出CVG-SGM记忆模块：几何加权的跨节点交互把目标特定证据转移进载体状态，共享的逐节点GRU在传输期间维持该信息，两时刻读出层结合存储的载体–身份兼容性与当前检测–载体几何来恢复历史身份；与感知前端解耦。

Result: 受控单身份/单载体设置下重链接准确率1.000，无记忆与参数量匹配的"不写入"对照均为0.570；可泛化到场景不重叠、更长的未见可见性间隙；真实RGB-D/SAM2特征级episodes中36/36正确重链接（最强固定载体–身份对照为28/36）；反事实载体记忆交换按交换后的绑定改变预测，佐证存储关系信息的作用。

Conclusion: 关系记忆可有效支撑跨可见性间隙的身份恢复，但方法仍受共享载体下身份歧义限制，且未实现端到端原始视频重识别。

Abstract: 4D scene graphs require persistent object identity when an object disappears inside an opaque carrier and later reappears as a fresh detection. When appearance and target-centric motion are unavailable, identity recovery must rely on relational history. We formulate cross-visibility-gap identity persistence as historical-node recovery through a remembered target--carrier binding. We introduce CVG-SGM, a perception-frontend-compatible transfer--maintain--read memory. Geometry-weighted cross-node interaction transfers target-specific evidence into carrier states, a shared per-node GRU maintains this information during transport, and a two-time readout combines stored carrier--identity compatibility with current detection--carrier geometry to recover the historical identity. In the controlled one-identity-per-carrier setting, CVG-SGM achieves 1.000 re-link accuracy, compared with 0.570 for both no-memory and parameter-count-matched no-write controls. It also generalizes to scene-disjoint, unseen longer visibility gaps. On real RGB-D/SAM2 feature-level episodes, it correctly re-links 36/36 held-out detections, compared with 28/36 for the strongest fixed carrier--identity control. Counterfactual carrier-memory swaps redirect predictions according to the swapped bindings, supporting the role of stored relational information in identity recovery. The current approach remains limited by shared-carrier identity ambiguity and does not provide end-to-end raw-video re-identification.

</details>


### [35] [Progressive Subset Selection and Cascaded Consistency for Unsupervised Vision-Language Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27515)
*Hao Yan,Guanghui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需标注的PVLM适配框架：用基于预测间隔的渐进式子集选择构建类平衡伪标签集并逐步扩展，结合教师-学生级联一致性学习与置信度过滤，仅更新视觉编码器的归一化参数和文本提示初始化的类原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型在目标域偏移下零样本性能下降，而无监督适配面临伪标签不可靠、且缺少有标签验证集用于模型选择和早停的难题。

Method: 1) 渐进式间隔子集选择：按预测间隔挑选、类别平衡的伪标签子集，从可靠锚点开始训练并逐渐扩大；2) 级联一致性学习：对弱增强视图做时间平滑的教师预测，监督强增强视图的学生预测，并用置信度过滤剔除不确定伪标签；3) 仅微调视觉编码器的归一化参数与文本提示初始化的类原型，以保持预训练嵌入结构。

Result: 在多个识别基准上，使用CLIP与SigLIP骨干均相对零样本基线取得一致提升，并与近期无监督PVLM适配方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 渐进伪标签选择结合教师引导一致性是一种轻量有效的无标签目标域PVLM分类器适配策略。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language Models (PVLMs) achieve strong zero-shot recognition but often suffer performance degradation under target-domain shift. Unsupervised adaptation provides a practical way to improve target-domain performance without labeled data, but it remains challenging because pseudo-labels generated by the model can be unreliable and no labeled validation set is available for model selection or early stopping. We propose a simple framework for unsupervised PVLM adaptation that combines progressive margin-based subset selection with cascaded consistency learning.
