Abstract: Token merging is an effective approach to reducing KV-cache memory consumption in autoregressive video generation by clustering temporally redundant tokens and retaining only a small set of representatives. Existing methods, however, typically apply a uniform merging ratio across all attention layers and heads. We observe that different layers and heads exhibit substantially different yet jointly correlated sensitivities to token merging, and that these sensitivity patterns remain consistent across prompts. Based on this observation, we introduce CALM, a Calibrated Allocation for Layer-Head Merging that uses offline calibration to improve online cache compression. Exhaustively evaluating all possible layer-head allocations is prohibitively expensive. To enable efficient calibration, we show that attention-output error strongly correlates with end-to-end video quality and can therefore serve as a lightweight proxy for allocation risk assessment. We evaluate different allocation strategies using this proxy and construct a fixed layer-head risk map offline. Given a cache budget at inference time, CALM uses dynamic programming to determine the best allocation of merging ratios. Experiments across three different autoregressive video models demonstrate that CALM consistently outperforms uniform token merging under the same memory budget, by reducing the LPIPS to original videos by around 21% with higher video-quality scores.

</details>


### [104] [Attractive Faces Skew Text-to-Image Reward Models and Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28195)
*Harvey Mannering,Zhiwu Huang,Adam Prügel-Bennett*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究揭示人类偏好评判中人脸"吸引力"压倒图像质量：控制实验、Elo模拟与人偏好模型分析均显示吸引力是分数最强相关因素，并导致偏好对齐生成偏移及可覆盖明确提示，最后探讨数据收集与后训练层面的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好已成为文生图模型评估、引导、微调与排序的核心，但人类偏好对生成图像中偏见的敏感性尚缺乏系统研究，可能损害评估可靠性与用户可控性。

Method: 控制性用户研究（真实照片同保真度对比、真实不吸引人脸 vs AI生成吸引人脸）；基于用户研究数据模拟Elo排名；在40个已标注人脸属性上单独编辑，分析5个人偏好模型（PickScore、MPS、HPSv2.1、HPSv3等）的分数相关性及回归分析；检验偏好对齐生成的分布偏移与显式提示覆盖；提出数据收集与后训练缓解策略。

Result: 吸引力可使排行榜排序反转，将合成吸引人脸置于真实不吸引人脸上方；吸引力为5个偏好模型中4个的最强分数相关因素，并在回归中为最强预测因子；偏好对齐生成使人脸偏向更年轻、更女性化，并可覆盖对不吸引人脸子的明确提示。

Conclusion: 需要能够将图像质量与人类认知偏见解耦的偏好模型，以实现更可靠的文生图评估；缓解需在数据收集与偏好模型后训练两端同时着手。

Abstract: Human preference is now central to the evaluation, guidance, finetuning, and ranking of text-to-image models. However, the susceptibility of human preferences to bias in image generation remains underexplored. We identify a key observation: for human faces, perceived attractiveness overrides image quality in preference judgments, even when comparing real photographs of identical fidelity. In a controlled user study, annotators rank more attractive faces as higher quality, even when unattractive real faces are compared with attractive AI-generated ones. We examine the downstream consequences of this bias. Firstly, simulating Elo rankings from user study data shows attractiveness can invert a leaderboard's ranking, elevating synthetic attractive faces over real, unattractive ones. Secondly, the same bias reappears in the human preference models. Editing 40 annotated facial attributes in isolation, we find that attractiveness is the strongest correlate of score for four of five human preference models (PickScore, MPS, HPSv2.1, HPSv3), and the strongest predictor of score in regression analysis. We further show that preference aligned generation shifts faces toward younger, more feminine faces, and can override explicit prompts for unattractive faces, thereby limiting user control. Finally, we explore mitigation strategies, both for data collection and post training on human preference models. These findings highlight the need for preference models that disentangle image quality from human cognitive bias, enabling more reliable evaluation.

</details>


### [105] [Learn to Modify: Self-evolving Multi-agent Refinement Framework for Conversational Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28210)
*Jianan Li,Jiawei Zhao,Yangtao Zhou,Zhifu Zhao,Shaoxin Wu,Yutao Wang,Dezhen Wang,Hua Chu,Fu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SMART：用于对话式动作生成的自演化多智能体精修框架，通过语义引导的掩码动作编辑将语言反馈定位到时空区域做局部修改，并用记忆驱动的自演化策略复用历史编辑经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成多为单次生成范式，用户对局部细节不满时只能整段重生成，计算低效且会破坏已建立的运动学上下文；因此需要支持迭代反馈、仅精修受影响时空区域并保留其余部分的对话式生成系统，需协调指令理解、生成、局部编辑与质量评估等多种能力。

Method: 提出自演化多智能体精修框架SMART：(1) 语义引导的掩码动作编辑机制，将自然语言反馈精确grounding到时空动作区域，实现局部精修且不改动未编辑区域以保证语义一致；(2) 基于记忆的自演化策略，从交互历史中动态累积动作编辑知识并复用过往成功经验，减少冗余探索；整体采用LLM驱动的角色分工多智能体协同。

Result: 大量实验表明SMART能有效支持多轮对话式动作生成，并实现高效的动作精修。

Conclusion: 对话式、局部化的迭代精修是文本到动作生成更实用的范式；多智能体分工结合掩码局部编辑与经验记忆可兼顾语义一致性与编辑效率。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in text-to-motion generation, existing methods are commonly designed for single-shot generation. When users are dissatisfied with specific motion details, they are often forced to regenerate the entire sequence from scratch, which is computationally inefficient and potentially destroys previously established kinematic context. A practical generation system should instead support iterative user feedback and refine only the affected spatio-temporal regions while preserving the remaining motion. Therefore, we introduce a conversational motion generation paradigm that requires the system to coordinate instruction understanding, motion generation, localized editing, and quality assessment. Recent advances in LLM-driven multi-agent systems provide a promising foundation for such role-specialized coordination. To tackle these challenges, a Self-evolving Multi-Agent RefinemenT Framework (SMART) is proposed for conversational motion generation. Within SMART, to preserve semantic consistency, we design a semantic-guided masked motion editing mechanism, which precisely grounds natural language feedback into spatiotemporal motion regions to enable localized refinements without altering unedited regions. To reduce redundant exploration, we propose a memory-based self-evolving strategy that dynamically accumulates motion editing knowledge from interaction history and reuses past successful experience. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SMART effectively supports multi-turn conversational motion generation and enables efficient motion refinement.

</details>


### [106] [Region-Guided Food-Package Evidence Extraction and Abstention-Aware Draft R3337 Screening with Selective VLM Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28211)
*Mayimunah Nagayi,Alice Scaria Khan,Tamryn Frank,Rina Swart,Clement Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向南非R3337草案的食品包装图像筛查流程：三分类检测定位营养标签/配料表/产品识别区域，PaddleOCR+结构化抽取回收证据，用确定性规则判定，证据不足时显式弃权（Review/Insufficient evidence），并对未决案例选择性调用Qwen3-VL 8B复评后重新应用同一规则；442产品/3929图像，65产品人工验证集上覆盖率31/60→65.0%，选择性准确率96.8%→97.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接把R3337警示标签判据套用到包装照片上，需要营养、配料与产品形态三类证据，而单张照片常缺失、模糊或互相冲突；此前工作多强行输出二元结论，缺乏对"证据不足"的显式建模与可审计的弃权机制。

Method: 区域引导式流水线：三分类检测器定位营养信息面板、配料表、产品识别区域；PaddleOCR加结构化抽取恢复营养值、配料证据与产品形态；证据聚合后用确定性R3337规则评估，证据不完整/不确定/冲突时输出Review或Insufficient evidence而非强制判定；对合格未决案例选择性调用VLM（Qwen3-VL 8B Instruct）复评，接受其证据并与已有证据合并后重跑同一确定性规则，形成"规则为主、模型为辅、弃权可控"的架构。

Result: 442产品/3929图像；65产品人工验证。选择性VLM复评前：60个可判定产品中31个获自动结论、30个正确（覆盖率51.7%，选择性准确率96.8%）。复评后额外解决8个产品，覆盖率升至65.0%；39个自动结论中38个正确（选择性准确率97.4%）。唯一误报继承自纯规则流程，复评后无误警漏报；但证据层评估发现VLM恢复的营养、配料与产品形态证据存在错误。

Conclusion: 选择性VLM复评能有效回收未决证据并提升覆盖率，且不引入不安全漏警；但确定性筛查与显式弃权仍是可靠图像筛查的必要组件，VLM更适合作为受控的证据补全器而非直接决策者。"证据层评估+决策层评估"分离的评测设计对高风险合规筛查有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Applying South Africa's draft R3337 warning criteria to food-package photographs requires nutrition, ingredient and product-form evidence. This study presents a region-guided evidence extraction and abstention-aware screening workflow for draft R3337. A three-class detector localises nutrition information panels, ingredient lists and product-identity regions, after which PaddleOCR and structured extraction recover nutrient values, ingredient evidence and product-form information. Recovered evidence is aggregated and assessed using deterministic draft R3337 rules. When evidence is incomplete, uncertain or conflicting, the workflow abstains through Review or Insufficient evidence rather than forcing an automatic outcome. For qualifying unresolved cases, a selective vision-language model (VLM) review stage using Qwen3-VL 8B Instruct reassesses unresolved evidence. Accepted evidence is merged and the same deterministic rules are reapplied. The workflow was applied to 442 products represented by 3,929 images. Evaluation used a manual validation set of 65 products. Before selective VLM review, 31 of 60 decidable products received automatic outcomes and 30 were correct, giving 51.7% coverage and 96.8% accuracy among automatic outcomes (selective accuracy). Selective VLM review resolved eight additional products, increasing coverage to 65.0%. Of 39 automatic outcomes, 38 were correct, giving 97.4% selective accuracy. The single false warning was retained from the rules-only workflow, while no unsafe warning omissions occurred after selective review. Evidence-level evaluation nevertheless identified incorrect VLM-recovered nutrition, ingredient and product-form evidence. These findings show that selective VLM review can recover unresolved evidence, while deterministic screening and explicit abstention remain necessary for reliable image-based draft R3337 screening.

</details>


### [107] [Convergent Performance, Divergent Mechanisms in LoRA-Adapted Vision Foundation Models for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28255)
*Cecilia Diana-Albelda,Fiona Kekwick,Raghav Mehta,Aleksandra Kulbaka,Paul M. Matthews,Jesus Bescos,Álvaro García-Martín,Wenjia Bai,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Four ViT-B/16 VFMs (DINOv3, SigLIP2, MerMED-FM, BiomedCLIP) adapted to brain tumour segmentation via LoRA reach similar Dice (78–81%) but through very different internal mechanisms; only early-to-mid layers are causally sufficient, and restricting LoRA to them improves cross-task robustness at lower parameter cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Practitioners select LoRA backbones purely by downstream metric equality and treat similarly performing models as interchangeable. The paper asks whether equal performance implies equal internal representational shift and equal cross-task transfer behaviour.

Method: Linear probing-style comparative analysis of four backbones pre-trained with different objectives (self-distillation vs. contrastive vision-language) and domains (natural vs. medical). Tools: attention divergence, weight-change analysis, and activation patching to localize causally relevant layers. Evaluated on brain tumour segmentation plus six datasets spanning segmentation and Alzheimer's classification; then layer-restricted LoRA (early-to-mid only) is proposed as a mitigation.

Result: Segmentation performance converges (mean Dice 78–81%) despite markedly different internal dynamics. Cross-task transfer diverges: segmentation adaptation helps DINOv3 for Alzheimer's classification but hurts the other three. Modification magnitude dissociates from causal importance — most heavily updated layers are not causally key; early-to-mid layers suffice to recover segmentation. Layer-restricted LoRA matches or approaches full-layer Dice while reducing classification degradation in the three affected backbones.

Conclusion: Metric-equal backbones are not mechanistically interchangeable; activation patching reveals a layer-wise causal structure that can be exploited to design more parameter-efficient and cross-task-robust LoRA adaptation. The analysis is confined to medical imaging (segmentation/classification), with no evidence for generative, 3D, or embodied settings.

Abstract: Adapting Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) to medical imaging via LoRA has become standard practice, yet model selection is guided almost entirely by downstream task metrics, treating backbones with similar performance as interchangeable. We investigate whether equal performance implies equal internal representational shift. We analyse four ViT-B/16 backbones, DINOv3, SigLIP2, MerMED-FM, and BiomedCLIP, pre-trained with different objectives (self-distillation vs. contrastive vision-language) and on different data domains (natural vs. medical images). We adapt each to brain tumour segmentation and evaluate on six datasets covering both segmentation and Alzheimer's disease classification. Using attention divergence, weight-change analysis, and activation patching, we find that the four models converge to similar segmentation performance (mean Dice 78-81%) through markedly different internal dynamics, yet diverge in cross-task transfer: segmentation adaptation improves Alzheimer's disease classification for DINOv3 but degrades it for the other three backbones. We reveal a dissociation between modification magnitude and causal importance: the most heavily modified layers are not the causally relevant ones, with early-to-mid layers identified as sufficient for segmentation recovery. Restricting LoRA to these layers matches or approaches full-layer segmentation while reducing classification degradation in the three affected backbones, providing a mechanistically grounded strategy that is more parameter-efficient and more robust than standard full-layer adaptation.

</details>


### [108] [SplatFlux: Feed-Forward 4D Gaussian Reconstruction and Generation from Video Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28257)
*Runmin Zhang,Binglin Ren,Zhu Yu,Fudong Wang,Jiangke Lin,Yichun Shentu,Si-Yuan Cao,Yi Yuan,Hui-Liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatFlux：统一视频4D重建与条件4D生成的前馈 latent-to-4DGS 框架，通过静态-动态解耦高斯表示与蒸馏式伪标注实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测视频与条件生成内容可共享同一预训练视频 latent 空间，因此两任务可统一为 latent→4DGS 解码；同时需要紧凑、时序一致的4D表示，并避免昂贵的动态掩码/光流标注。

Method: 1) 将重建与生成统一为在预训练视频 latent 空间上的前馈解码；2) 提出静态-动态解耦的4D高斯表示，显式为每个高斯基元预测动态概率，静态部分做静态聚合、动态部分做运动插值以完整构建4D场景；3) 设计辅助蒸馏策略，用伪动态掩码与光流线索监督静态-动态分解与动态高斯运动，无需真值标注。

Result: 在3D/4D重建与生成任务上的大量实验均达到SOTA（摘要未给出具体数值与数据集）。

Conclusion: 预训练视频 latent 空间可作为重建与生成的共同接口，静态-动态解耦+蒸馏伪监督是构建紧凑动态4D高斯场景的有效前馈范式；开放问题包括真实场景/野外泛化、实时性与代码开源情况未知。

Abstract: This work presents SplatFlux, a feed-forward latent-to-4DGS framework for both video-based 4D reconstruction and conditional 4D generation. Our key observation is that observed videos and conditionally generated content can be represented in the same pretrained video latent space, allowing both tasks to be formulated as latent-to-4DGS decoding. To construct compact and temporally coherent 4D scenes, SplatFlux introduces a static-dynamic decoupled 4D Gaussian representation that explicitly predicts dynamic probabilities for Gaussian primitives. This representation separates static scene structures from moving regions, enabling complete 4D scene construction in a feed-forward manner through static aggregation and dynamic motion interpolation. To learn static-dynamic decomposition and dynamic Gaussian motion without costly annotations, we further develop an auxiliary distillation strategy that provides pseudo dynamic masks and optical-flow cues. Extensive experiments across 3D and 4D reconstruction and generation demonstrate that SplatFlux achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [109] [Reliability-Aware Reconstruction of V-Shaped Surfaces with a Low-Cost Multi-Zone SPAD Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28258)
*Yohanssen Pratama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用两个TMF8821低成本多区SPAD配置，学习逐点可靠性（置信度、瞬态峰值幅度、峰锐度）并嵌入图优化，重建V形表面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本多区SPAD传感器采样稀疏、几何不连续处深度不可靠，难以稳定重建V形折面（如室内墙角、箱体棱边）。

Method: 两种互补采集配置（多区SPAD）融合；由传感器置信度、瞬态直方图峰值幅度与峰锐度构造逐点可靠性权重；将该权重引入图结构深度优化；用鲁棒双平面拟合与垂直截面评估V角误差及点到面RMSE。

Result: 60°、90°、120°目标上平均V角误差由11.21°降至9.66°（相对降低13.8%）；代表性90°目标联合点到面RMSE由14.56 mm降至13.65 mm。

Conclusion: 在极低成本稀疏SPAD硬件上，可靠性加权图优化可有限但可测地改善折面几何重建；增益幅度较小、缺乏大规模与动态场景验证，属增量式传感/重建改进。

Abstract: Low-cost multi-zone single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) sensors provide compact time-of-flight measurements but are limited by sparse sampling and unreliable depth estimates near geometric discontinuities. This letter presents a reliability-aware reconstruction framework for V-shaped surfaces using two complementary TMF8821 acquisition configurations. Point-wise reliability is learned from sensor confidence, transient peak amplitude, and peak sharpness, and is incorporated into a graph-based depth optimization. The reconstructed geometry is evaluated by robust two-plane fitting and a perpendicular cross-section. Experiments on 60-degree, 90-degree, and 120-degree targets reduce the mean V-angle error from 11.21 degrees to 9.66 degrees (13.8%). For the representative 90-degree target, the combined point-to-plane RMSE decreases from 14.56 mm to 13.65 mm.

</details>


### [110] [PI-MLLM: Psychophysics-Inspired Multimodal Large Language Model for Oddly Satisfying Video Emotion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28264)
*Yuheng Zhang,Jie Zhang,Yuecong Min,Yunwei Zhao,Meichen Liu,Qiankun Li,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建12,000条5秒标注的Oddly Satisfying Video数据集（OSVD，含视频/音频/视听三条件情绪标签与感官证据解释），并提出心理物理学启发的多模态大模型PI-MLLM：通过模态特异动态对齐（MSDA）与基于联盟的视听归因（CAVA，Shapley归因）实现情绪预测与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oddly Satisfying Videos（OSV）依靠重复运动、视觉触感与类ASMR声音引发强烈观感反应，但缺乏显式语义情绪线索；现有视频情绪分析（VEA）方法偏重语义内容，对塑造观者反应的物理感官动态建模不足，且缺少相应数据集。

Method: 1) OSVD数据集：12,000条5秒片段，每条提供video-only、audio-only、audio-visual三种条件下的观者情绪标签，以及基于视觉/声学感官证据的解释。2) PI-MLLM：MSDA用辅助预测目标将模态表征与视觉运动、声学能量起始（energy-onset）动态对齐；CAVA在模态联盟上评估情绪分数并用Shapley归因确定主导模态，判断视听证据是协同还是冲突。

Result: 实验表明PI-MLLM在观者侧情绪预测与感官证据解释质量两方面均有提升，说明物理感官线索对视频情绪分析具有价值。

Conclusion: OSVEA作为新任务被提出，配套数据集与心理物理学启发的MLLM方法证明：显式建模非语义的物理感官动态（运动、能量起始、视听协同/冲突）可显著改善视频情绪理解与解释生成。

Abstract: Oddly Satisfying Videos (OSVs) can evoke strong viewer responses through repetitive motion, visual tactility, and ASMR-like sounds despite containing few explicit semantic emotion cues. This makes OSVs a challenging setting for Video Emotion Analysis (VEA), since most existing methods focus on semantic video content and under-model the physical sensory dynamics that shape viewer responses. To support Oddly Satisfying Video Emotion Analysis (OSVEA), we construct the Oddly Satisfying Video Dataset (OSVD), comprising 12,000 annotated 5-second clips. Each clip provides viewer-emotion labels under video-only, audio-only, and audio-visual conditions, together with explanations grounded in visual and acoustic sensory evidence. Building on OSVD, we propose PI-MLLM, a psychophysics-inspired multimodal large language model that integrates Modality-Specific Dynamics Alignment (MSDA) and Coalition-Based Audio-Visual Attribution (CAVA). MSDA uses auxiliary prediction objectives to align modality representations with visual motion and acoustic energy-onset dynamics. CAVA evaluates emotion scores across modality coalitions and applies Shapley attribution to identify the dominant modality and determine whether visual and auditory evidence is synergistic or conflicting. Experiments show that PI-MLLM improves both viewer-side emotion prediction and sensory-grounded explanation quality, suggesting the value of physical sensory cues to video emotion analysis.

</details>


### [111] [VGL-stGen: Learning a shared multimodal model for lung histology and spatial transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28266)
*Haenara Shin,Dongjoo Lee,Sungwoo Bae,Kwon Joong Na,Daeseung Lee,Hongyoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGL-stGen：以配对肺H&E与空间转录组（单细胞参考）微调医学视觉-语言模型，统一支持组织/细胞类型分类、基因预测和条件表达补全等13项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合组织学形态与分子状态之间的鸿沟，使生物医学多模态骨干网络能从H&E图像推断转录组层面的细胞类型与基因表达。

Method: 在预训练医学视觉-语言模型基础上适配：训练数据整合图像-表达、表达-细胞类型配对；用基因条件伪标签把转录组细胞类型监督迁移到组织学图像；任务特定解码输出类别标签、基因列表与结构化表达水平；条件补全时固定查询基因身份并自回归预测其表达值；对照实验显示仅对所需表达水平token施加监督可进一步提升图像条件补全；图像置换实验检验配对有效性。

Result: 单一checkpoint在13项评估任务上可用，覆盖组织/细胞类型分类、基因预测与条件表达补全；受限监督可改善图像条件补全；图像置换表明正确配对的H&E提供观测表达之外的信息；外部分类部分依赖共享注释谱系的计算参考，且部分H&E在癌症训练或模型开发中已暴露。

Conclusion: 在所述限制条件下，展示医学视觉-语言骨干网络可通过共享生成建模从空间转录组与组织学中学习，连接组织形态与分子状态；泛化性与外部验证仍受数据暴露与注释谱系偏差制约。

Abstract: VGL-stGen is a multimodal large language model adapted from a pretrained medical vision-language model using paired lung H&E and spatial transcriptomics with single-cell references. Training integrates image-expression and expression-cell-type pairs, and gene-conditioned pseudo-labels transfer transcriptomic cell-type supervision to histology images. One deployed checkpoint supports thirteen evaluated tasks spanning tissue and cell-type classification, gene prediction and conditional expression completion from combinations of histology, cell identity and observed gene expression. Task-specific decoding produces categorical labels, gene lists and structured expression levels. For conditional completion, fixing queried gene identities and predicting their levels autoregressively improves structured prediction. Controlled adaptation shows that restricting supervision to requested expression-level tokens can further improve image-conditioned completion. Image-permutation experiments indicate that correctly paired histology adds information beyond observed expression. External classification relies partly on computational references with shared annotation lineage, and some H&E sources were exposed during cancer training or model development. Within these conditions, VGL-stGen demonstrates how a medical vision-language backbone can learn from spatial transcriptomics and histology to connect tissue morphology and molecular states through shared generative modeling.

</details>


### [112] [HRoPE: Homography Relative Positional Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28284)
*Huachen Gao,Yang Yang,Xiaoqian Liang,Xingyu Chen,Youjia Zhang,Qi Zhang,Baorui Ma,Anpei Chen,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HRoPE：以每个patch的斜平面（slanted plane）表示局部几何，将平面诱导的单应变换作为相对位置编码注入多视角Transformer注意力，无需真值几何即可端到端学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何相对位置编码（GRoPE）基于相机视锥或射线行进深度，属于"传感器中心"表述，只描述token从何处被观测，未刻画token所对应场景区域的三维表面几何。

Method: 借鉴经典多视角立体视觉的斜支撑窗假设，将每个patch的局部表面表示为倾斜3D平面；由其诱导的token级单应性作为注意力中的相对变换，提供校正透视畸变、跨视角对齐patch特征的几何先验。平面参数可仅凭视图合成目标端到端预测，或从基础模型导出。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、CO3Dv2、DL3DV上，最佳HRoPE变体在新视角合成任务上持续优于所评估的几何位置编码；消融与分析验证了编码的有效性。

Conclusion: 将位置编码从传感器中心转向表面感知是一条有效且实用的方向，可即插即用地增强多视角Transformer的几何归纳偏置，且不依赖真值深度。

Abstract: Geometric Relative Positional Encoding has become the primary mechanism for injecting spatial inductive biases into multi-view transformers. Existing designs condition attention on camera-level frustums or ray-marched depth, associating cross-view tokens through the sensor geometry. Such sensor-centric formulations, however, describe only where a token is observed from, leaving the rich surface geometry of the scene region depicted by the token. In this paper, we advocate a shift toward surface-aware positional encoding that explicitly grounds patch tokens in the geometry of physical surfaces. We introduce HRoPE, which represents the local geometry of each patch as a slanted 3D plane, following the slanted support-window assumption of classical multi-view stereo. We inject token-level, plane-induced homographies into attention as relative transformations. This equips the transformer with a geometric prior for rectifying perspective distortions and aligning patch features across viewpoints. Our encoding requires no ground-truth geometry: the plane parameters can be predicted end-to-end under a view-synthesis objective alone, or derived from foundation models. Extensive experiments on RealEstate10K, CO3Dv2, and DL3DV show that our best variant of HRoPE consistently outperforms the evaluated geometric positional encodings for novel view synthesis. Extensive ablations and analysis show the effectiveness of the proposed encoding. Please refer to our project page at: this https URL

</details>


### [113] [CPVB-RG: Cross-Prior Visual Bridging for Prior-Aware Chest X-Ray Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28289)
*Sike Xiang,Shuang Chen,Julie Cox,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CPVB-RG：利用"进展引导焦点注意力"(PGFA)对比进展差注意与共享空间注意，并用两个交叉注意力单元将纵向视觉证据与临床文本对齐，两阶段训练（先验引导图文对齐+自回归报告生成）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随访胸片报告生成系统要么忽略纵向上下文（不利用旧片），要么直接融合旧片特征而不区分"间隔期变化"与"稳定解剖结构"，导致不变响应干扰差异判别。

Method: Cross-Prior Visual Bridge (CPVB)：PGFA 显式对比进展-差异注意力与共享-空间注意力以抑制不变响应、保留粗粒度空间对应；两个 Cross-Attention 单元将纵向视觉证据与临床语义连接；两阶段训练：先验引导的图像-文本对齐 + 轻量语言解码器自回归生成。

Result: 在 MIMIC-CXR 与 IU X-Ray 上语言与临床指标一致提升；MIMIC-CXR 上 F1RadGraph 0.2847、VLScore 0.6692，相较最接近的、解码器匹配的先验感知基线具统计显著增益。

Conclusion: 差异感知 + 空间引导的融合设计对先验感知胸片报告生成有效；属于医学影像报告生成方法学工作，与物理AI/具身/3D重建主题无关。

Abstract: Radiologists interpret follow-up chest X-rays by comparing current and prior examinations to identify new, resolved or progressing findings. Most report generation systems either omit this longitudinal context or fuse prior features without distinguishing interval changes from anatomy that remains stable over time. We propose CPVB-RG, a prior-aware framework that combines current and prior radiographs with available clinical text. Its Cross-Prior Visual Bridge (CPVB) contains Progression-Guided Focus Attention (PGFA) and two Cross-Attention units. PGFA is designed to contrast progression-difference attention with shared-spatial attention, aiming to reduce invariant responses whilst retaining coarse spatial correspondence between examinations. The Cross-Attention units then connect the resulting longitudinal visual evidence with clinical semantics. CPVB-RG is trained in two stages, using prior-guided image-text alignment followed by autoregressive report generation with a lightweight language decoder. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR and IU X-Ray show consistent improvements across language and clinical metrics. On MIMIC-CXR, CPVB-RG achieves 0.2847 F1RadGraph and 0.6692 VLScore, with statistically significant gains over the closest decoder-matched prior-aware baseline. These results support the effectiveness of the proposed difference-aware and spatially guided fusion design for prior-aware chest X-ray report generation.

</details>


### [114] [AGGR-Mamba: Mamba-based Network with Attention-Guided Gated Residual Refinement for Undersampled MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28291)
*Yue Zhuo,Wenbin Deng,Lingjun Liu,Xuhang Chen,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGGR-Mamba 是一种轻量级输出级残差精炼方法，在 Mamba 主干重建之后，用通道/空间注意力头与 GDFN 式门控残差分支进一步校正欠采样脑部 MRI 的局部误差与高频伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠采样 MRI 重建因缺失频域测量而产生残余混叠伪影、边缘模糊与局部结构细节丢失；作者希望在不大幅改动已有主干重建的前提下，针对性地修正输出端的局部错误与高频残留。

Method: 以 Mamba 作为主干先产生初始重建；随后接两个精炼模块：(1) Attention Refine Head，通过通道与空间注意力重新校准局部解剖结构、边缘纹理和残差误差区域；(2) GDFN 风格的门控残差精炼分支，选择性地保留有信息量的残差特征、抑制冗余或不稳定响应。二者以受控残差方式叠加回主干输出。

Result: 在 IXI 脑部 MRI 数据集（T1、T2 加权）上，加速因子 R=4 与 R=8 各设置下均取得有竞争力的 PSNR 与 SSIM，作者据此论证输出级精炼策略的有效性（摘要未给出具体数值与参数量对比）。

Conclusion: 输出级残差精炼是一种低成本、可与 Mamba 主干组合的提升方案；但验证仅为医学 MRI 基准上的定量指标，缺乏临床/真实扫描场景证据与消融细节，方法洞见对其他领域迁移性有限。

Abstract: Undersampled magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reconstruction aims to recover high-quality images from partially acquired k-space data, thereby reducing scan time. However, missing frequency-domain measurements often lead to residual aliasing artifacts, blurred edges, and loss of local structural details. In this paper, we propose AGGR-Mamba, a lightweight output-stage residual refinement method built upon a Mamba backbone for undersampled brain MRI reconstruction. The Mamba backbone first produces an initial reconstruction, and the proposed refinement modules then correct the remaining local errors and high-frequency artifacts in a controlled residual manner. Specifically, an Attention Refine Head is introduced to recalibrate local anatomical structures, edge textures, and residual-error regions through channel and spatial attention. In addition, a GDFN-style gated residual refinement branch is designed to selectively preserve informative residual features while suppressing redundant or unstable responses. By combining attention-based recalibration with gated residual selection, AGGR-Mamba improves local structure recovery without substantially disturbing the backbone reconstruction. Experiments are conducted on the IXI brain MRI dataset with T1- and T2-weighted images under acceleration factors of R=4 and R=8. The results show that AGGR-Mamba achieves competitive PSNR and SSIM performance across different reconstruction settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed output-stage refinement strategy.

</details>


### [115] [Towards Consistent Geospatial Representations: Aligning One Encoder to Many Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28313)
*Arthur Ouaknine,David Rolnick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pangaea-Latents 提供 5 个地理空间基础模型在 11 个数据集上的冻结嵌入与复现基线，发现 GFM 表示既非冗余也非完全不相交；进而提出 GeoMixture，用多表示对齐目标（mREPA）将单个 ViT 对齐到多个 GFM，在九个 Pangaea 数据集平均排名上追平最优 GFM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFM 在预训练目标、模态与分辨率上差异大，且无单一模型在所有基准上领先；作者要验证这些模型的表示是否互补，以及单个编码器能否继承这种互补性。

Method: 构建 Pangaea-Latents：在 Pangaea 基准的 11 个数据集上提取 5 个 GFM 的冻结嵌入，并复现基线；分析表示的关系。随后提出 GeoMixture 框架，以 mREPA（multi-representation alignment）为逐数据集目标，把 ViT 与多个 GFM 表示对齐。

Result: GFM 嵌入既非冗余也非不相交；GeoMixture 单编码器在 9 个 Pangaea 数据集上的平均排名追平最佳 GFM，牺牲峰值性能换取跨任务一致性。

Conclusion: GFM 表示具有可利用的互补性，可通过多表示对齐蒸馏进单一编码器；作者将 GeoMixture 定位为初步框架，强调跨任务稳健性而非单任务峰值。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GFMs) differ widely in pretraining objective, modality and resolution, and none leads across benchmarks. We ask whether their representations are complementary, and whether a single encoder can inherit that complementarity. We introduce Pangaea-Latents, an extension of the Pangaea benchmark providing frozen embeddings of $5$ GFMs over its $11$ datasets, along with reproduced baselines, and find GFM embeddings to be neither redundant nor disjoint. We then propose GeoMixture, a preliminary framework to align a ViT to several GFMs per dataset through a multi-representation alignment (mREPA) objective. Our encoder ties the best GFM in average rank across nine Pangaea datasets, trading peak performance for cross-task consistency.

</details>


### [116] [ABACUS: A Benchmark for Image Editing Models to Assess Object Category and Counting Under Scene Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28316)
*Aymen Lassoued,Angelique Loesch,Romaric Audigier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ABACUS——面向指令式图像编辑“数值正确性”的基准、评测协议与专用打分指标：采用无需 VLM 判官（judge-free）的实例级验证，借助全视景真值与检测器共识核验“精确计数达成 + 场景其余部分保持”，覆盖增/删/改三类操作，并揭示现有编辑器在计数上的系统性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令式图像编辑模型在增删指定数量对象或改变类别时经常产出错误数量，且难度随场景复杂度与目标数量增加而急剧上升。现有基准以整体相似度指标或计数能力不足的 VLM 判官打分，导致数值错误被忽略或误评，当前编辑器真实的计数能力从未被准确测量。

Method: 构建 ABACUS 基准与评测协议：完全 judge-free，不使用 VLM/MLLM 对编辑或对象计数打分（VLM 仅用于确认罕见模糊裁剪的类别）；将每次编辑与经检测器共识确认的全视景（panoptic）真值做实例级比对，检查是否达到精确请求计数，同时检查场景其余区域是否保持，覆盖 add / remove / change 三类操作。

Result: 对主流图像编辑器的评测揭示出大量数值编辑失败，且随场景复杂度与对象数量上升而恶化；这类失败在以往基准中常被忽略或误判。

Conclusion: 数值正确性是当前指令式图像编辑被系统性忽视的关键失效维度；judge-free、实例级、结合精确计数与场景保持的评测协议能更可靠地度量该能力，为后续模型改进提供可信度量基线。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing models can now add, remove a specified number of objects or change their category while preserving the scene. Yet these models often fail to produce the correct number, and these failures grow sharply as scenes become more complex and the requested count increases. Current benchmarks largely miss this problem: they score edits with holistic similarity metrics or with vision-language model (VLM) judges that are themselves poor at counting, so numerical errors go unnoticed or are graded incorrectly. As a result, the true numerical ability of today's editing models has never been accurately measured. We address this issue with ABACUS, a new benchmark, evaluation protocol and dedicated scoring metric for numerical image editing. Our protocol is judge-free: no VLM or MLLM scores an edit or counts objects (a VLM only confirms the category of rare ambiguous crops). Instead, it verifies each edit at the instance level against panoptic ground truth confirmed by detector consensus, checking both that the exact requested count was achieved and that the rest of the scene was preserved, across the add, remove and change operations. By evaluating leading image editors, our method reveals substantial numerical editing failures that worsen with increasing scene complexity and object count; failures that prior benchmarks often overlooked or misjudged.

</details>


### [117] [PACE: Plan, Attend, Check, and Emit for Training-Free Response Timing in Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28323)
*Siqi Guo,Yuxia Chen,Wenbo Zhu,Yangguang Ji,Zhenxiang Jiang,Teng Zhong,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE 是一个免训练框架，通过"计划—观察—校验—输出"闭环解决流式视频理解中的过早回答问题，把错误拆解为不稳定、无证据支撑、事件未完成三类并分别校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解要求模型不仅决定答什么，还要决定何时答。证据是逐步到达的，已有基准指出"在事件结束前抢答"是常见失败模式；现有方法要么需要额外训练学习回答时机，要么仅用标量门控信号，无法显式刻画"何时答案已成定稿"。

Method: 将块级流式错误归纳为三种模式（采样导致的答案不稳定、答案稳定但当前观测视频缺乏支撑、答案有支撑但事件尚未结束）。据此提出 PACE：给定问题先起草计划，明确需观察什么、何种信号代表完成；流展开过程中在信息量高的时刻调用现成视频 LLM，对每份草稿分别评估稳定性、引用帧上的接地性、完成度，并将通过校验的观测写入 event ledger（事件账本）。全程免训练。

Result: 在 ProactiveVideoQA 与 OVO-Bench Forward 上，跨闭源与开源骨干模型平均 PAUC 提升最多 5.9 点、平均准确率提升最多 6.2 点，优于官方评测协议，取得 SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式建模"何时回答"并通过多重校验机制替代标量门控，是免训练条件下提升流式/主动视频理解时序可靠性的有效路径；错误模式分解为模块设计提供了直接依据。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires a model to decide not only what to answer but also when to answer. Evidence arrives progressively, and recent benchmarks identify premature responses, issued before the queried event has concluded, as a recurring failure mode. Existing approaches often learn response timing through additional training, while training-free alternatives typically rely on scalar gating signals that do not explicitly represent when an answer becomes final. We organize chunk-wise streaming errors into three patterns: sampling variation can make an answer unstable, a stable answer may lack support in the observed video, or an answer may be supported by the current evidence while the event remains unfinished. We propose PACE (Plan, Attend, Check, and Emit), a training-free framework designed around these three failure modes. Given a question, PACE drafts a plan specifying what to observe and what will signal completion. As the stream unfolds, it invokes an off-the-shelf video LLM at informative moments, evaluates each draft for stability, grounding in its cited frames, and completion, and records verified observations in an event ledger. Experiments on ProactiveVideoQA and OVO-Bench Forward show that PACE improves over the official protocols by up to 5.9 average PAUC points and 6.2 average accuracy points across proprietary and open-source backbones, yielding state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [118] [HistoLoop: Iterative Contextual Inference with a Looped Transformer for Histology-Based Spatial Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28339)
*Ziang Liu,Jie Liu,Shuai Li,Yigui Feng,Qinglin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HistoLoop：用循环Transformer把"从H&E预测空间基因表达"建模为迭代上下文推断，用更新的spot状态逐级重构更细分辨率上下文，在HEST-Bench HVG50上取得最高宏平均基因级Pearson相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组实验成本高，从H&E形态学图像预测空间基因表达是可扩展替代方案。现有方法虽已引入空间上下文建模，但"如何由早期上下文交互更新后的spot状态构造后续上下文"尚未被充分探索，多为单向、单次或固定层级的上下文聚合。

Method: 提出HistoLoop，一种looped Transformer，将预测表述为迭代上下文推断。每个精化步骤：(1)把当前spot状态按对应分辨率聚合为context token；(2)用共享空间Transformer处理；(3)通过cross-attention为每个spot检索特定上下文信息；(4)通过memory-mediated update将其写入持久spot状态，供下一更细分辨率的上下文构造使用。共享权重逐级循环，实现由粗到细的递归状态精化。

Result: 在HEST-Bench HVG50评测协议下，覆盖HEST-1K中五个器官、六个癌种任务，HistoLoop在对比方法中取得最高宏平均基因级Pearson相关。消融实验验证了递归状态精化、以及用更新后spot状态在渐进更细分辨率上重构上下文的重要性；基于表达导出的聚类分析表明其更好地捕获了空间组织的多基因模式。

Conclusion: 将上下文构造与状态更新耦合成循环式、由粗到细的迭代推断，是提升H&E到空间基因表达预测的有效路径。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from H&E images offers a scalable complement to experimental spatial transcriptomics. However, despite progress in spatial context modeling, how to construct subsequent context from spot states updated by earlier contextual interactions remains underexplored. We introduce HistoLoop, a looped Transformer that formulates prediction as iterative contextual inference. At each refinement step, HistoLoop aggregates the current spot states into context tokens at the corresponding resolution, processes them with a shared spatial Transformer, and retrieves spot-specific contextual information through cross-attention. A memory-mediated update integrates this information into persistent spot states, which are then used to construct context at the next, finer resolution. Under the HEST-Bench HVG50 evaluation protocol, HistoLoop achieves the highest macro-averaged gene-wise Pearson correlation among the compared methods across six cancer-specific tasks spanning five organs in HEST-1K. Ablation studies support the importance of recurrent state refinement and reconstructing context from updated spot states at progressively finer resolutions. Expression-derived clustering further suggests that HistoLoop better captures spatially organized multigene patterns.

</details>


### [119] [Auto-Director: Leveraging Multi-Agent Collaboration for Autonomous Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28346)
*Yuxia Chen,Wenbo Zhu,Yangguang Ji,Zhenxiang Jiang,Teng Zhong,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Auto-Director: a tool-augmented multi-agent video editing framework (Planning/Material/Editor/Critic agents) plus VEdit-Bench, a 100-topic expert benchmark with 5-dim metric suite.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Retrieval-based editing lacks creative flexibility; generative models suffer long-horizon incoherence and intent drift. Neither mirrors the structured multi-stage reasoning of professional post-production.

Method: Four-agent pipeline: retrieval-augmented planning to decompose topics into fact-grounded scripts; shot segmentation + visual tagging index for open-world footage; MLLM-driven spatial adaptation to assemble semantically aligned clips; a Critic agent driving an iterative Reflect-and-Revise loop for scene alignment and pacing.

Result: Significant gains over baselines on automated metrics and human evaluation; benchmark shows bridging gap between amateur and professional-grade production.

Conclusion: Multi-agent, tool-augmented decomposition is an effective paradigm for open-world autonomous video editing; benchmark and metric suite enable systematic evaluation.

Abstract: Autonomous video editing from open-world resources remains a challenging problem in multimedia content creation. Retrieval-based methods are constrained by fixed source material and lack creative flexibility, while generative models struggle with long-horizon coherence and often diverge from user intent. Both paradigms fail to replicate the structured, multi-stage reasoning that underlies professional post-production. To address these limitations, we present Auto-Director, a tool-augmented multi-agent framework that mirrors professional editorial workflows: a Planning Agent decomposes abstract topics into fact-grounded scripts via retrieval-augmented planning; a Material Agent indexes open-world footage through shot segmentation and visual tagging; an Editor Agent assembles semantically aligned clips with MLLM-driven spatial adaptation; and a Critic Agent governs an iterative Reflect-and-Revise loop that checks scene-level alignment and pacing. To rigorously evaluate this new paradigm, we introduce VEdit-Bench, a benchmark of 100 expert-curated topics with human-annotated Golden Scripts and a five-dimensional metric suite covering Narrative Logic, Factual Accuracy, Semantic Alignment, Compositional Quality, and Stylistic Cohesion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Auto-Director significantly outperforms baseline methods in both automated metrics and human evaluation, effectively bridging the gap between amateurs and professional-grade video production.

</details>


### [120] [Gated Prototype Transfer for Zero-Support Medical Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28350)
*Sumaiya Fazal,Rizwan Qureshi,Laleh Behjat,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无梯度预排斥模块 GProtoT，用 VLM 相似度与本体临床距离把 supported 类的视觉残差迁移到零支持（missing）类，并以无验证集的证据门控限制外推；在 MESSIDOR 50 次配对 one-shot 试验中提升缺失类召回 5.23 点，负迁移率由 46–48% 降至 2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实少样本设定下低患病率类别可能完全缺少有标注支持样本，训练无关的 SS-Text+ 仍可运行，但其零支持原型不做任何视觉自适应，退化为纯文本原型，导致缺失类性能差。

Method: GProtoT：1) 计算 supported 类的视觉残差（视觉相对文本原型偏移）；2) 依据 VLM 语义相似度与基于本体(ontology)的临床距离，将残差加权迁移到 missing 类原型；3) 无验证集证据门控(evidence gate)，按语义相关性与残差一致性缩放迁移强度，抑制无依据外推；无需梯度、验证数据或额外推理。

Result: MESSIDOR + FLAIR 特征，50 次 one-shot 配对试验中 severe 与 proliferative DR 均不在支持集内：缺失类召回 +5.23pt，平均类准确率 +1.37pt，缺失/支持召回调和均值 +1.83pt，负迁移频率由无门控的 46–48% 降至 2%；代价为支持类召回 −1.20pt、top-1 准确率 −0.45pt。

Conclusion: 证据门控为知识迁移提供了安全机制，实现可问责的低算力自适应；但仅在单一医疗数据集、单一特征与 one-shot 设定下评估，缺少跨数据集与真实临床部署验证，且存在正类性能的轻微权衡。

Abstract: Realistic few-shot adaptation of medical vision-language models (VLMs) can leave low-prevalence classes without any labeled support. The training-free SS- Text+ solver remains operational in this setting, but its zero-support prototypes receive no visual adaptation and fall back to a purely textual class prototype. We in- troduce Gated Knowledge-Guided Prototype Transfer (GProtoT), a pre-repulsion module that transfers supported-class visual residuals to missing classes using VLM similarity and an ontology-based clinical distance. A validation-free evidence gate scales transfer based on semantic relevance and residual agreement, thereby limiting unsupported extrapolation. In 50 paired realistic one-shot trials on MESSIDOR with FLAIR features, severe and proliferative diabetic retinopathy were absent from every support set. Relative to SS-Text+, gated ProtoT improved missing-class recall by 5.23 points, average class accuracy by 1.37 points, and the missing/supported recall harmonic mean by 1.83 points. It reduced negative- transfer frequency from 46-48% for ungated transfer to 2%, while at the cost of reductions of 1.20 percentage points in supported-class recall and 0.45 percentage points in top-1 accuracy. These results indicate that evidence-based gating provides a safety mechanism for the knowledge source. GProtoT is based on SS- Text+ and requires no gradients, validation data, or additional model inference, of- fering a low-compute path toward accountable adaptation under incomplete clini- cal supervision. Code is available: this https URL

</details>


### [121] [RayCo4D: Ray-Conditioned Continuous Motion Model for Monocular 4D Dynamic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28351)
*Yuanxiang Ni,Xianliang Huang,Ruihan Hou,Feng Xie,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayCo4D提出一种射线条件连续运动模型，将长程点轨迹的稀疏观测与共享隐式3D运动状态解耦，用SE(3)位姿+体坐标速度节点与白噪声加速度先验构建连续时间运动，并通过对偶四元数混合迁移到高斯图元，实现单目4D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频动态重建中观测噪声大、可见性间歇，长程轨迹虽提供几何约束但时间覆盖碎片化，难以整合进一段连续的共享运动表示。

Method: 每条轨迹绑定受限于其相机射线的参考时刻3D查询；图像与深度观测通过局部多对多关联约束共享的隐式运动节点。每个节点在稀疏支撑时刻携带SE(3)位姿与体坐标速度，采用加速度白噪声（WNOA）过程先验耦合相邻状态并定义连续时间插值，使不同可见区间的观测共同约束同一隐式状态。推断出的共享运动经对偶四元数混合传递到高斯图元，并用参考帧锚定的局部SE(3)残差刻画共享骨架之外的细尺度非刚性形变。

Result: 在多样化单目动态场景上，新视角合成质量具竞争力；在快速运动与轨迹不完整条件下改善结构与时序一致性；支持连续时间渲染。

Conclusion: 显式分离观测与隐式运动状态的射线条件连续运动表示，可有效融合碎片化长程轨迹，为单目4D重建提供连续、可插值的运动骨架并兼容高斯泼溅渲染。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from monocular video requires inferring motion from noisy and intermittently visible observations. Long-range tracks provide valuable geometric constraints, but integrating their fragmented temporal coverage into shared, continuous motion remains challenging. We introduce \textbf{RayCo4D}, a ray-conditioned continuous motion model for monocular 4D reconstruction that explicitly separates visual measurements from latent 3D motion states. Each track is associated with a reference-time 3D query constrained to its camera ray, and its image and depth observations constrain shared latent motion nodes through local many-to-many associations. Each node maintains an $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ pose and body-frame velocity at sparse support times. A white-noise-on-acceleration process prior couples neighboring states and defines continuous-time interpolation, allowing observations across visibility intervals to jointly constrain the same latent state this http URL inferred shared motion is transferred to Gaussian primitives through dual-quaternion blending, while reference-anchored local $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ residuals capture fine-scale non-rigid deformation beyond the shared scaffold. Experiments on diverse monocular dynamic scenes show that RayCo4D achieves competitive novel-view synthesis quality, improves structural and temporal consistency under rapid motion and incomplete tracks, and supports continuous-time rendering.

</details>


### [122] [CountVideoBench: A Benchmark to Evaluate Prediction Biases on the Object Counting task by Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28356)
*Marc Chapus,Carlos Crispim-Junior,Véronique Eglin,Atilla Baskurt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CountVideoBench 基准，用 2600 个合成视频覆盖 [5,50] 的 26 个基数，揭示现有视频计数问答基准的真值分布严重偏斜（中位数≤5），并发现16个视频-语言模型零样本 Exact Match 均不超过 27.6%，且准确率随基数的变化模式各异、预测集中于各自偏好整数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中的物体计数是 MLLM 能力中表征不足的一环。现有 VideoQA 基准虽含计数题，但真值分布严重偏向小数值（9 个基准中位数≤5，前三高频值占 45%–93%），导致平均准确率被高频计数主导，稀有计数上的依赖偏差被淹没而不可见，无法可靠诊断模型的计数能力。

Method: 构建 CountVideoBench：2600 个合成视频，覆盖 [5,50] 区间内 26 个基数（每个 100 视频），按六个连续计数簇组织以支持近邻比较；每个视频仅含单一类别静态物体，配合受控相机运动，排除物体运动与识别因素干扰。在此基准上零样本评测来自 9 个架构家族的 16 个视频-语言模型。

Result: 无模型 Exact Match 超过 27.6%。准确率随基数的变化因模型而异，从单调下降至非单调的"峰值—崩塌"模式，说明"难度随基数递增"无法解释计数失败。各模型预测均集中于其自身偏好整数集合，准确率峰值与这些值重合而在相邻计数处骤降。

Conclusion: 现有计数评估因数据分布偏斜而失真，计数失败并非单纯随基数增大而加剧，而是表现为模型特有的整数偏好偏差。需要构建均衡、诊断性的计数基准以可靠评估这一基础视觉能力。

Abstract: Object counting in videos remains a poorly characterized capability of multimodal large language models. Counting questions are included in many existing VideoQA benchmarks, but the ground-truth count distributions in these benchmarks are heavily skewed toward small values. Across the nine benchmarks we survey, the median ground-truth count is at most five, and the three most frequent values together account for between 45% and 93% of the videos. The resulting mean accuracy is then dominated by the most frequent counts; a value-dependent bias at a rare count contributes negligibly to the average and remains invisible. We introduce CountVideoBench, a benchmark of 2,600 synthetic videos covering 26 cardinalities in the interval [5, 50] (100 videos each), grouped into six clusters of consecutive counts that span the range while enabling near-neighbor comparisons. Each video contains a single class of static objects under a controlled camera motion, removing object motion and reducing recognition as confounds. We evaluate sixteen video-language models from nine architecture families in a zero-shot setting. No model exceeds 27.6% Exact Match. The dependence of accuracy on cardinality varies across models, ranging from monotonic decline to a non-monotonic peak-and-collapse pattern, which rules out increasing difficulty with cardinality as the dominant explanation of counting failure. Every model further concentrates its predictions on its own set of preferred integers, with accuracy peaks coinciding with these values and collapsing at adjacent counts. These findings highlight the need for balanced, diagnostic counting benchmarks to reliably assess this fundamental visual capability.

</details>


### [123] [RACE: Learning Multimodal Search through Referential Abstraction and Compositional Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28382)
*Eric Tao,Minhao Sun,Liyan Liu,Zhijing Wu,Xiaohan Yu,Lang Mei,Li Huang,Chao Feng,Jinchao Ma,Sichen Kang,Siyu Mo,Chong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RACE 提出图像条件化任务合成框架，通过"指代抽象(RA)"和"组合证据(CE)"消除合成视觉搜索题中的文本身份捷径，迫使多模态搜索智能体真正依赖视觉搜索与多跳证据整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练多模态搜索智能体需要"必须依赖视觉定位才能确定答案"的任务。现有合成问题中常残留揭示身份的文字线索与未被使用的目标证据，使智能体可走捷径，从而削弱对视觉搜索与证据整合的监督信号。

Method: RACE 从图像到目标、从目标专属证据到答案构建依赖链：1) Referential Abstraction (RA) 用具体视觉指代或需在图中候选间检索消歧的模糊关系指代替换暴露身份的文本；2) Compositional Evidence (CE) 将同一图像内多实体或跨图像实体的检索事实链接为联合问题，答案必须由每个指定贡献共同决定；两种操作同样作用于搜索过程中新发现的图像，使新的视觉观察决定后续搜索目标或提供答案证据。用 Qwen3.5-27B 仅在 12K 条教师生成轨迹上微调，与匹配的基座智能体对比。

Result: 在十个多模态基准上平均准确率由 53.5 提升至 56.0，相对基座智能体约 4.7% 提升；仅需 12K 合成教师轨迹。代码、数据与模型将公开。

Conclusion: 通过消除合成任务中的身份捷径并强制组合证据依赖，可低成本（12K 轨迹）显著提升多模态搜索智能体的视觉搜索与证据整合能力；该任务合成范式可推广至搜索中发现的新图像。

Abstract: Training multimodal search agents requires tasks that make visually grounded search necessary for determining the answer. Textual identity clues and unused target evidence allow shortcuts in synthetic questions, weakening supervision for visual search and evidence integration. We propose RACE, an image-conditioned task synthesis framework that constructs dependencies from images to targets and from target-specific evidence to answers. Referential Abstraction (RA) replaces identity-revealing text with specific visual references or ambiguous relational references resolved by searching among depicted candidates. Compositional Evidence (CE) links retrieved facts about multiple entities in one image or entities from different images into joint questions whose answers require every designated contribution. Both operations also apply to images found during search, allowing new visual observations to determine subsequent search targets or supply answer evidence. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-27B on only 12K teacher-generated trajectories from RACE raises average accuracy across ten multimodal benchmarks from 53.5 to 56.0, a relative improvement of approximately 4.7% over the matched base agent. Our code, data, and model will be publicly available.

</details>


### [124] [VisualEvolve: A Skill Self-Evolution Framework for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28386)
*Jiawei Wang,Yucun Li,Xueqi Li,Shenghao Wang,Xinquan Gong,Dan Zhang,Zheqi Lv,Yuquan Le,Shaozu Yuan,An Zhang,Da Cao,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisualEvolve：通过"选择-执行-进化-评估"循环，用技能库快照选择与渐进式证据访问（PEA）来自进化VLM的可执行视觉操作与工具使用策略，避免参数更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM的视觉工具使用受限于预定义视觉操作与训练期习得的工具策略；将其表征为可复用的外部技能可通过自进化改进，但技能精炼会引入性能回退，且累积轨迹+进化历史的证据量导致"进化证据过载"。

Method: 自进化框架：Select–Execute–Evolve–Evaluate循环。Skill-Library Snapshot Selection 用与执行任务不相交的固定验证集选择历史快照以抑制回退传播；Progressive Evidence Access（PEA）以粗到细层级（Skill-Performance Report → Evolution Log → Trajectory Summaries → 相关原始轨迹片段）指导能力缺口诊断。

Result: 4个VLM骨干、3个benchmark共6个评测集上，24个设置中23个优于初始技能库，Qwen3.5-Plus上VisualProbe Hard最大提升15.1个百分点；消融支持Snapshot Selection与PEA贡献；技能可跨数据设置迁移、跨骨干复用；相比代表性RL方法所需解题轨迹少一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 无需更新参数的外部技能自进化可有效提升VLM视觉工具使用，配合快照选择与层级证据访问可稳定收益并显著降低样本成本；但面向通用视觉问答基准，证据为benchmark精度而非机器人/3D场景部署。

Abstract: Visual tool use enables vision-language models (VLMs) to acquire task-relevant evidence, but its effectiveness can be limited by predefined visual operations and the tool-use strategies learned during training. Representing these operations and strategies as reusable external skills allows them to be refined through self-evolution without updating model parameters. However, skill refinement can introduce performance regressions. Meanwhile, inspecting accumulated task execution trajectories together with skill evolution history may lead to evolution evidence overload. To address these challenges, we propose VisualEvolve, a self-evolving framework that evolves executable visual operations and tool-use strategies for VLMs through a Select--Execute--Evolve--Evaluate cycle. Skill-Library Snapshot Selection uses performance on a fixed validation set disjoint from execution tasks to select an effective historical snapshot for subsequent evolution, limiting the propagation of performance regressions. Progressive Evidence Access (PEA) guides capability-gap diagnosis through a coarse-to-fine hierarchy comprising the Skill-Performance Report, Evolution Log, Trajectory Summaries, and relevant raw trajectory segments. Across four VLM backbones and six evaluation sets from three benchmarks, VisualEvolve improves over the Initial Skill Library in 23 of 24 settings, with a maximum gain of 15.1 percentage points on VisualProbe Hard using Qwen3.5-Plus. Ablation studies support the contributions of Snapshot Selection and PEA, while further experiments show that skills can be improved using experience from different data settings and directly reused across backbones. Moreover, VisualEvolve enables effective skill self-evolution with over an order of magnitude fewer task-solving trajectories than estimated for representative RL-based methods.

</details>


### [125] [StreamEval: Evaluating Interactive Streaming Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28402)
*StreamEval Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StreamEval，一个面向交互式流式视频生成的统一基准，涵盖数字人对话、数字人表演、世界生成、指令编辑、参考编辑五类任务，包含 300 个 15–300 秒的脚本化测试用例，并从任务执行、状态一致性、视觉保真度、视听协调四个维度用 10 项任务相关指标（0–100 分）进行评估；对 37 个模型的评测显示视觉质量与请求执行能力并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成技术快速进步，使"与生成视频实时交互"成为关键能力，但现有评测缺乏对增量到达的多模态请求、时序约束与内容保持能力的统一衡量手段，因此需要一个覆盖多任务的交互式流式生成基准。

Method: 构建 StreamEval：300 个脚本化测试用例（时长 15–300 秒），每个用例给定初始素材、按时间增量到达的多模态请求，以及明确的状态变化与内容保持要求；评测沿任务执行、状态一致性、视觉保真度、视听协调四个维度展开，实例化为 10 个任务特定指标并归一到 0–100 分；在全部 300 个用例上评测 37 个不同模型并构建排行榜。

Result: 高视觉质量与身份保持并不必然伴随可靠的请求执行或持续的状态一致性；请求执行尤为困难：数字人表演的 7 个模型中 Following 最高仅 36.90 分，文本条件世界生成的 Following 最高 25.93 分，3 个参考编辑模型的 Edit Success 最高仅 15.00 分。

Conclusion: 在时序约束与内容保持条件下可靠地执行请求，是推进全模态实时交互系统面临的核心挑战；StreamEval 为该方向提供了统一、可复现的评测基线与公开排行榜。

Abstract: As streaming video generation advances, enabling real-time interaction with generated video is becoming increasingly important. We introduce StreamEval, a unified benchmark for interactive streaming video generation spanning five tasks: avatar dialogue, avatar performance, world generation, instruction editing, and reference editing. StreamEval comprises 300 scripted test cases lasting 15-300s, each specifying initial assets, incrementally arriving multimodal requests, and explicit requirements for state changes and content preservation. Performance is assessed along four evaluation dimensions: task execution, state consistency, visual fidelity, and audiovisual coordination, instantiated through ten task-specific metrics on a 0-100 scale. Evaluating 37 distinct models across 300 test cases, we find that high visual quality and identity retention do not necessarily coincide with reliable request execution or sustained state consistency. In particular, request execution remains challenging: the highest task-specific scores are 36.90 for Following among seven avatar-performance models, 25.93 for Following in text-conditioned world generation, and 15.00 for Edit Success among three reported reference-editing models. These findings identify reliable request execution under timing and preservation constraints as a central challenge for advancing omni-modal real-time interactive systems. The leaderboard is available at this https URL

</details>


### [126] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Machine Learning with Evidence-based Planning for Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28410)
*Yiqing Shen,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedicalMLOmni：面向多模态医学任务的多智能体 AutoML 框架，通过自然语言接口、基于文献证据的规划与并行 HPO，在 20 个基准中 19 个取得最优成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像深度学习仍依赖人工选择架构与超参数（"更像艺术而非科学"）；通用 AutoML 缺少医学专用架构与预处理，医学专用 AutoML 又需工程知识且局限于 3D 分割等窄任务。

Method: 多智能体 AutoML 框架：自然语言接口驱动；基于证据的规划，检索已发表结果以指导模型选择与配置；多个智能体并行探索不同超参数调整策略；支持单模态与多模态多种医学任务。并用骨干网络匹配的对照实验做消融。

Result: 在 20 个基准中 19 个取得最佳分数；仅 PMC-VQA 例外，由预训练更广的视觉语言模型领先。骨干匹配对照表明增益来自框架而非骨干网络。

Conclusion: 降低编码门槛，使数据丰富但工程能力有限的领域专家也能自动完成模型训练；AutoML 在医学影像的通用化仍受限于通用 VLM 的预训练规模。

Abstract: Deep learning has become the default tool for medical image analysis, driven by the open-source movement. However, achieving the best performance on a given task remains difficult, with architecture selection and hyperparameter optimization (HPO) still considered "more of an art than a science". Automated machine learning (AutoML) automates parts of model training, yet helps little in medical imaging: general-purpose approaches lack medical architectures and preprocessing, and medical-focused ones demand engineering expertise and remain confined to narrow tasks such as 3D segmentation. We introduce MedicalMLOmni, a multi-agent AutoML framework that automates model training for diverse single- and multi-modal medical tasks through a natural language interface. Specifically, MedicalMLOmni performs evidence-based planning by retrieving published results to guide model selection and configuration, and employs parallel HPO where multiple agents explore adjustment strategies concurrently. MedicalMLOmni achieves the best score on 19 of 20 benchmarks; PMC-VQA is the exception, where a vision-language model with broader pre-training leads. Backbone-matched controls attribute the gains to the framework, not the backbone. By removing coding requirements, MedicalMLOmni lowers the barrier for domain experts with rich datasets but limited engineering expertise.

</details>


### [127] [Brain2Vec: A Self-Supervised 3D Foundation Model for Structural Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28411)
*Spiros Maggioros,Guray Erus,Gareth Harman,George Aidinis,Alexander Getka,Aristeidis Sotiras,Christos Davatzikos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Brain2Vec：基于74,425例T1加权MRI预训练的脑影像基础模型，可作微调骨干或冻结特征提取器，在下游预测、收敛速度与嵌入质量上均优于随机初始化与体素体积特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像深度学习面临标注数据稀缺与算力受限，端到端训练多样任务困难；需通用预训练模型以缓解数据与计算瓶颈。

Method: 汇总多研究共74,425例T1w MRI，自监督预训练得到Brain2Vec基础模型；评估三重维度：下游任务预测性能（脑龄回归微调）、收敛速度、冻结嵌入质量（5项分类任务对比解剖体积特征），并做区域归因与潜变量操控实验验证表征的神经解剖可解释性。

Result: 微调后最终精度更高、收敛更快；冻结嵌入在5个分类任务中4个优于体积特征，与数据量更大的SOTA基础模型性能相当；模型编码集中于空间局部且具神经解剖意义的脑区，操控潜特征产生单调、区域特异的解剖效应。

Conclusion: Brain2Vec提供可复用、可解释的脑MRI表征，开源并提供云端NiChart平台接入，降低基础设施门槛。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become essential tools in medical imaging, yet training them end-to-end for diverse tasks remains challenging due to limited labeled data and computational constraints. To address this, we introduce Brain2Vec, a foundation model pre-trained on 74,425 T1-weighted Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) scans pooled from multiple studies. Brain2Vec can serve both as a weight initialization backbone for fine-tuning downstream tasks and as a standalone feature extractor. We evaluated Brain2Vec in three tasks: downstream predictive performance, convergence speed, and embedding quality. Using Brain2Vec's pretrained weights as initialization and fine-tuning on labeled data for a brain age prediction task, results demonstrate improved final accuracy and faster convergence compared to random initialization. Additionally, its frozen embeddings outperform anatomically defined volumetric features on four of five downstream classification tasks and perform competitively with a state-of-the-art foundation model pre-trained on substantially more data. Lastly, we show that Brain2Vec encodes information primarily from a spatially localized and neuroanatomically meaningful set of brain regions, and that manipulating a subset of latent features produces monotonic, region-specific effects on the reconstructed anatomy. Brain2Vec code and trained models are available as an open-source package on this https URL . Users can apply Brain2Vec locally by installing the package, or directly through the NiChart cloud platform this https URL , where Brain2Vec features can be derived without requiring local installation or specialized infrastructure.

</details>


### [128] [Code2Video-Compass: A Comprehensive Benchmark for General-Purpose Code-Based Video Generation across T2V, I2V, and V2V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28416)
*Zhenghan Chen,Zekai Shao,Xin Lin,Leting Cheng,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Code2Video-Compass：面向代码式视频生成的通用基准，涵盖T2V/I2V/V2V，2104个任务、10类24子类、三维复杂度，双层评测（12客观+5主观指标），评测15个MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码式视频生成可实现细粒度控制、定向编辑与组件复用，但现有基准多局限于专门任务，缺乏对多创意类别、多输入模态下完整视频创作的评估。

Method: 构建包含2,104个任务的基准，覆盖10类别、24子类及构建/动态动画/状态依赖三个复杂度维度；任务来源兼有文本指令与视觉参考，以及由专业与用户视频反推的T2V/I2V/V2V任务。提出双层评测：客观12项指标（运行性能、视频质量、文视对齐、参考保真）加主观5项指标（MLLM评审判断指令遵循与整体质量）。

Result: 对9个家族、15个近期闭源与开源MLLM的评测显示，即使领先模型也难以稳定生成符合文本与视觉要求的高质量完整视频，且性能随构建、动态动画、状态依赖复杂度上升而普遍下降。

Conclusion: 该基准有望推动可靠、连贯、可控的代码式视频生成，并促进程序化视觉创作的发展。

Abstract: Code-based video generation offers fine-grained control, targeted editing, and component reuse compared with direct video generation. This approach requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to translate visual and temporal requirements into executable programs that produce complete videos. However, existing code-based benchmarks primarily target specialized tasks, leaving complete video creation across diverse creative categories and input modalities insufficiently evaluated. We introduce Code2Video-Compass, a general-purpose benchmark for code-based video generation across text-to-video (T2V), image-to-video (I2V), and video-to-video (V2V) settings. Code2Video-Compass comprises 2,104 tasks spanning 10 categories, 24 subtypes, and three complexity dimensions covering construction, dynamic animation, and state dependency. It combines tasks curated from textual instructions and visual references with corresponding T2V, I2V, and V2V tasks derived from complete professional and user-created videos. We propose a dual-level evaluation framework combining 12 objective metrics and five subjective metrics. Objective evaluation covers operational performance, video quality, text-video alignment, and reference fidelity, while subjective evaluation uses an MLLM judge to evaluate instruction following and holistic video quality. Extensive evaluation of 15 recent proprietary and open-weight MLLMs from nine families reveals that even leading models face challenges in reliably generating high-quality complete videos aligned with textual and visual requirements, with performance generally declining as construction, dynamic animation, and state-dependency complexity increases. We anticipate that Code2Video-Compass will advance reliable, coherent, and controllable code-based video generation and inspire broader progress in programmatic visual creation.

</details>


### [129] [DISTRACT: Degradation-Indexed Study of Textual Reference Across Competing Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28419)
*Dan Zlotnikov,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DISTRACT 匹配干预基准：固定视频/目标/干扰物，交叉 4 类文本先验与 4 级图像退化，用行为对比、逐层表征探针与激活补丁评估 5 个视觉语言跟踪器（VLT）是否真正利用文本语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLT 接受文本描述并不等于其内容真的影响跟踪决策，尤其在视觉证据退化时文本作用是否被放大或失效尚不清楚，缺乏受控的因果式评测手段。

Method: 构建匹配干预基准 DISTRACT：保持视频、目标、干扰物不变，将 4 种文本先验与 4 种图像退化沿统一严重度轴交叉；在 4 个数据集上评估 5 个 VLT，融合行为对比、逐层表征探针与激活补丁（activation patching）三类分析，并采用多重比较/FDR 校正。

Result: Molmo2 与 JointNLT 表现出有方向的文本利用，部分效应在退化下收缩、部分经 FDR 校正后端点变化不显著；UVLTrack/MMTrack/DUTrack 对文本语义变化响应微弱。决策近状态可解码正确与冲突文本（仅前两者），可解码性峰值出现在替换几乎不改变行为的后期层，替换较早状态可把冲突轨迹引向目标；但匹配正确供体未显著优于无关供体。

Conclusion: DISTRACT 揭示了在视觉退化条件下文本何时、何处影响跟踪，并区分了"可解码"与"因果敏感"状态，同时表明干预效应尚不足以证明目标特定的语义救助。

Abstract: Vision-language trackers (VLTs) accept descriptions intended to assist tracking, but accepting text does not establish that its content changes tracking decisions, particularly as visual evidence deteriorates. We introduce DISTRACT, a matched-intervention benchmark that holds video, target, and distractor fixed while crossing four text priors with four image degradations along a controlled severity axis. We evaluate five VLTs on four datasets using behavioral contrasts, layerwise representation probes, and activation patching. Molmo2 and JointNLT exhibit directed text use. Some effects contract under degradation, whereas others show no significant endpoint change after multiplicity correction. UVLTrack, MMTrack, and DUTrack respond only weakly to changes in text semantics, with no directed behavioral effect surviving FDR correction in the tested settings. Model-internal analyses reveal that at decision-near states, correct-versus-conflicting text is decodable in Molmo2 and JointNLT but not in the other three systems. In the responsive models, decodability peaks at late states where replacement produces little behavioral change, whereas replacing selected earlier states redirects conflict-associated trajectories toward the target. Because matched-correct donors do not significantly outperform unrelated donors, these interventions identify earlier intervention-sensitive states without establishing target-specific semantic rescue. DISTRACT thus exposes when and where text affects tracking under degraded visual evidence.

</details>


### [130] [WorldSR: Harnessing World Knowledge Search for Grounded Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28423)
*Junyuan Deng,Xinyi Wu,Zhenyao Wu,Song Wang,Yu Wang,Congchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldSR 用显式、实体对齐的外部视觉世界知识增强超分：将输入分解为语义实体，从大规模外部语料中逐实体检索结构与语义对齐的参考图，经记忆复用与多步过滤后，由多参考超分模型融合，为细节合成与结构恢复提供细粒度约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成先验天然随机、面向多样生成，与超分所需的确定性高保真重建相冲突；且先验绑定基座模型、更新成本高，随真实世界数据演化易产生幻觉。作者希望用可更新、外部的显式世界知识作为补充。

Method: 框架包含：（1）自动语义实体分解；（2）实体级检索，从大规模外部图像语料中取回结构/语义对齐的参考；（3）效率与可靠性机制——缓存并复用历史检索结果的记忆模块，以及多步候选过滤；（4）新提出的多参考超分模型，将选定参考作为世界知识注入，提供细粒度约束。

Result: 在真实世界超分基准上，WorldSR 在结构保真度与视觉质量上均稳定提升，优于现有生成式超分方法。

Conclusion: 实体级的外部视觉世界知识 grounding 是模型内置生成先验的简单有效补充；将“检索式知识”与生成式先验结合可缓解随机性与幻觉问题。摘要未给出定量指标、代码或部署验证。

Abstract: Generative-prior-based super-resolution (SR) methods leverage implicit knowledge learned from large-scale models to enhance low-resolution inputs. However, such priors are inherently stochastic and optimized for diverse generation, conflicting with the deterministic, high-fidelity reconstruction required for SR. It is also tied to the base model, costly to update, and prone to hallucinations as real-world data evolves. To address these limitations, we introduce WorldSR, a novel framework that augments SR with explicit, entity-aligned visual world knowledge. WorldSR automatically decomposes the input into semantic entities and performs entity-wise retrieval of structurally and semantically aligned images from a large-scale external corpus. For efficiency and reliability, we incorporate a memory mechanism to reuse previously retrieved results and a multi-step filtering process to refine candidate references. The selected references as world knowledge are then integrated through a novel multi-reference super-resolution model, which provides fine-grained constraints for detail synthesis and structural recovery. Experiments on real-world SR benchmarks show that WorldSR consistently improves structural fidelity and visual quality, outperforming existing generative super-resolution methods. Our results suggest that entity-level grounding in external visual world knowledge provides a simple and effective complement to model-bound generative priors.

</details>


### [131] [EvaOmni: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Audio-Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28444)
*Xiaoxing Hu,Wenxi Yao,Chaoyuan Shi,Ziqi Ye,Xiaorong Guo,Peixuan Li,Zhuoran Sun,Ziyang Gong,Peiyuan Zhang,Mingxin Liu,Shuran Ma,Hongbo Liu,Xiaomin Yu,Yu Tian,Wentao Wang,Zhenjie Yang,Jian Hu,Yunhang Shen,Haoyu Cao,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvaOmni：一个要求模型同时输出答案与音视频证据时间区间的基准（392视频、1027人工标注问答、15能力3层级），并提出C.G.与B.G.两项定位评分，揭示顶尖Omni模型在时序证据定位上的显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频推理基准多为选择题、仅按答案正确率评分，无法判断模型是否真正建立了"证据→答案"的推理链；且文本或单模态捷径会让模型在未分析必要音视频证据时也答对，削弱评估可信度。

Method: 构建EvaOmni：392段视频、1027条纯人工标注QA，覆盖15项能力、三层次（检索与定位、基础理解、复杂推理）。标注流程包括专家标注音视频证据、构造视频内干扰项、人工审核严格标准、并用模型过滤文本与单模态捷径。评测时要求模型同时返回答案与支撑证据区间，提出Coverage Grounded Score（C.G.，衡量对标注证据的覆盖）与Bottleneck Grounded Score（B.G.，惩罚任一模态的时序定位过差），并纳入答案正确性考量。

Result: 在7个开源权重与17个专有Omni大模型上评测，Gemini3.7-Flash综合最优：准确率79.4%、C.G. 70.8%、B.G. 35.1%；Level 3准确率79.1%但B.G.仅12.3%。

Conclusion: 模型普遍"答对但定位不到证据"，最强模型在复杂推理层级仍有巨大提升空间；仅看答案正确率的高估问题被证实，grounded 音视频推理仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Omni LLMs can naturally locate key audio and visual evidence and integrate information across modalities for audiovisual reasoning. Existing audio-visual reasoning benchmarks that mainly rely on multiple-choice questions usually score models solely based on answer correctness, making it difficult to assess whether a model truly forms a reasoning chain from evidence to the correct answer. In addition, textual or unimodal shortcuts may allow models to answer correctly without analyzing the necessary audio-visual evidence, further weakening the reliability of evaluation benchmarks. To address these issues, we introduce EvaOmni, an evidence-grounded benchmark containing 392 videos and 1,027 purely human-annotated question-answer pairs, covering 15 capabilities and three levels: retrieval and localization, basic understanding, and complex reasoning. We design a series of rigorous annotation procedures in which experts annotate audio and visual evidence and construct video-based distractors; humans review whether questions meet strict inspection criteria, and finally models filter textual and unimodal shortcuts. Beyond high-quality question-answer pairs, we require models to simultaneously return the answer and supporting evidence intervals in order to evaluate the complete reasoning chain. We propose the Coverage Grounded Score (C.G.) to measure how much annotated evidence a model identifies, and the Bottleneck Grounded Score (B.G.) to penalize poor temporal localization in either modality; both are incorporated into answer correctness. Across 7 open-weight and 17 proprietary Omni LLMs, Gemini3.7-Flash achieved the highest overall accuracy (79.4%), C.G. (70.8%), and B.G. (35.1%). At Level 3, it reached 79.1% accuracy, but its B.G. was only 12.3%. These results show that even the most advanced models still have room for development in grounded audio-visual reasoning.

</details>


### [132] [Answering in the User's Variety: Calibrated Accommodation Pairs for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28474)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Heming Li,Jian Yang,Junyu Luo,Jingshu Peng,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"校准迎合配对"(CAP)偏好数据,并引入对更新本身(而非仅输出)的审计方法,揭示后训练(SFT/DPO/RL)会把模型回复标准化,且DPO更新在控制先验偏好后仍偏好匹配英式/澳式拼写而非AAE与Singlish;CAP在完全开源模型上提升了未训练特征的收敛度,且不损害标准英语用户与指令遵循。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天模型倾向以标准英语回复,忽略用户方言。统一的输出会掩盖训练是否对不同类型的用户区别对待,且仅做输出审计也无法察觉这种差异。因此需要审计"更新"本身,弄清各后训练阶段如何改变模型对人方言的迎合倾向。

Method: 追踪SFT、DPO与强化学习各阶段对模型方言迎合倾向的影响;用内容匹配的两条回复(一条迎合用户方言、一条不迎合)读取每次更新所隐含的偏好,并对每条回复的先验概率作校正。在10个开源模型系列上对比。随后构造CAP偏好数据,奖励匹配用户方言密度同时保留标准英语用户的规范回复,并在完全开源模型上验证;同时与单侧配对(unilateral pairs)对比。

Result: 后训练会把回复对各类非正式线索标准化;在概率校正后,DPO更新在10个模型系列上仍更偏好英式/澳式拼写而非AAE与Singlish,但模型在被直接要求时仍能产出AAE特征。CAP提升了模型对未训练特征的收敛,且未检测到对标准英语用户的方言漂移或指令遵循损失;单侧配对只能通过过度迎合、强加方言来获得类似增益。

Conclusion: 统一化回复会掩盖选择性更新,审计必须同时读取"更新"与"输出";基于匹配用户方言密度的偏好数据可在不牺牲规范性的前提下改善对多样方言的迎合能力。

Abstract: People adapt how they talk to the person in front of them. However, chat models tend to respond in Standard English regardless of the dialect used by the user. A uniform answer can hide whether training treats these users alike, and output audits cannot tell as well. We trace how each post-training stage, from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to preference optimization (DPO) and reinforcement learning, changes a model's tendency to match the user, and read what each update prefers from two content-matched replies, one matching the user and one not. Across 10 open model lineages, post-training standardizes replies to every informal cue, yet after adjusting for how likely each reply already was, the DPO update still favours matching British and Australian spelling over AAE and Singlish on 10 lineages, and models still produce AAE features when asked. We then introduce calibrated accommodation pairs (CAP), preference data that reward matching users' dialect density while preserving Standard English for Standard-English users. On a fully open model, CAP raises convergence on features it was not trained on without a detectable increase in dialect for Standard-English users or a cost in instruction following. In contrast, one-sided pairs reach a similar gain only by overshooting the user and imposing dialect. Our findings show that uniform replies can hide selective updates and audits should read the update as well as the output. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [133] [CLOSER: Evidence-Aware Active Visual Verification for Embodied Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28476)
*Pengying Wu,Mingye Jiang,Yunze Hu,Zhejia Wen,Kangjie Zhou,Zongru Yang,Zhe Peng,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLOSER是一个证据感知的主动验证框架，将"停止决策"重构为闭环证据采集：用微调的开源VLM把候选物体在各视角标注为目标/非目标/不确定，用嵌入多样性门控挑选互补视角并轻量网络融合概率；若仍不确定，则用扩散策略仅凭跟踪mask与深度主动靠近候选以获取确证视角。无需重训即可接入现有ObjectNav系统，在Gibson与HM3D上SR提升3.6、SPL提升2.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ObjectNav依赖检测器，但检测器常在相似类别上高置信误判（沙发当床），且仅凭有限第一视角视频甚至单帧在线判断；现有系统贪心地径直靠近候选，导致遮挡或观察不全，停在假正例上即任务失败。作者希望把"是否停止"从一次性判定改为可主动获取证据的闭环决策。

Method: (1) 对跨帧跟踪的候选，微调开源VLM逐视角输出target/non-target/uncertain三分类概率；(2) 基于嵌入的多样性门控选择互补视角；(3) 轻量融合网络聚合逐视角概率；(4) 若融合后仍不确定，用扩散策略仅以跟踪mask与深度为输入驱动机器人靠近候选，直至判定明确。

Result: 在Gibson与Habitat-Matterport 3D上，平均成功率提升3.6个百分点，SPL提升2.0个百分点；方法本地运行，可作为即插即用模块接入现有ObjectNav系统而无需重训。

Conclusion: 在提交停止决策前先主动采集并验证证据，能有效抑制相似类别的假正例与贪心靠近造成的遮挡问题，提升具身导航的成功率与路径效率，说明"验证—再承诺"的闭环范式对ObjectNav有效。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) requires an embodied agent to find an instance of a specified category in an unseen environment and stop near it. The agent typically relies on an object detector but must judge online from a limited egocentric video stream, sometimes a single frame, whether a detected candidate is the target. Detectors often confidently mistake similar objects, such as a sofa for a bed, and stopping at a false positive fails the episode. Prevailing systems also approach candidates greedily, leaving them occluded or partially observed. We present CLOSER, an evidence-aware active verification framework that treats stopping as closed-loop evidence acquisition, coupling vision-language model (VLM) verification with active perception. CLOSER fine-tunes an open-source VLM to label each view of a candidate tracked across frames as target, non-target, or uncertain, selects complementary views with an embedding-based diversity gate, and fuses their per-view probabilities with a lightweight network. If the fused judgment remains uncertain, a diffusion policy drives the agent toward the candidate using only the tracked mask and depth until the decision becomes decisive. CLOSER runs locally and plugs into existing ObjectNav systems without retraining them. On Gibson and Habitat-Matterport 3D, it raises average success rate by 3.6 points and success weighted by path length by 2.0 points, showing that verifying evidence before committing improves embodied navigation.

</details>


### [134] [Learning from Automatic 3D Gaussian Splatting Edits for Online Bayesian Scene Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28490)
*Changrui Zhu,Simon Julier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用3DGS参考模型自动编辑生成标注，离线学习证据模型与像素先验，在线以广义贝叶斯证据融合更新累积3D高斯场，实现在线场景变化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线变化检测需融合新观测判断相对参考场景的变化区域，但变化先验与证据模型未知且数值贝叶斯推断开销大。

Method: 对参考3DGS模型做自动编辑，生成无需人工标注的带标签训练视图；结合未变化参考观测，离线学习场景特定证据模型并估计有效像素先验与响应统计；在线融合当前证据与历史支持，用由响应统计参数化的闭式局部贝叶斯因子更新累积3D高斯场，再经物体引导细化得到变化掩码。

Result: PASLCD与CCS3D上mIoU达67.64%和75.82%，均SOTA；PASLCD完整流程在整段查询序列上17.2 FPS，为评估的在线方法中最快。

Conclusion: 将数据合成式自监督与闭式贝叶斯证据融合结合，可在线、高效地完成场景级变化检测，兼顾精度与实时性。

Abstract: Online scene change detection requires integrating incoming observations to determine which regions have changed relative to a reference scene. We update change beliefs as new images arrive using generalised Bayesian evidence fusion. However, the prior probability of change and evidence models are unknown beforehand, and numerical Bayesian inference can be costly. We address these challenges by learning offline from automatic edits to a reference 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model. These edits generate labelled training views without manual annotations. Together with unchanged reference observations, they provide supervision for learning scene-specific evidence models and estimating the effective pixel prior and response statistics. Online prediction combines current evidence with historical support, while closed-form local Bayes factors, parameterized by the learned response statistics, update an accumulated 3D Gaussian field. Object-guided refinement produces the final change masks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on PASLCD and CCS3D, with $67.64\%$ and $75.82\%$ mIoU, respectively. On PASLCD, the complete pipeline runs at $17.2$\,FPS over complete query sequences, the fastest among the evaluated online methods.

</details>


### [135] [GazeLens for Efficient Egocentric Video Question Answering with Simple Gaze-Driven Adaptive Token Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28502)
*Trong-Thang Pham,Anh Nguyen,Hien Van Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GazeLens: 面向智能眼镜小VLM的高效视频问答方法，先用HOG转移+清晰度选帧，再用视线与问题引导的高斯池化把每帧压缩为2个token（聚焦+全局场景），在StreamGaze上以更低FLOPs取得更高宏/微准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜上小规模视觉语言模型算力受限：冗余帧浪费视觉预算，激进空间压缩又会丢失关键细节；仅依赖视线聚焦不足以覆盖问题所需的场景上下文证据。因此需要在帧选择与token压缩两个层面同时做证据保留。

Method: 1) 编码前帧选择：利用HOG帧间转移量与图像清晰度打分挑选信息量高的帧；2) 对选中帧做高斯池化，生成以视线为中心的聚焦摘要与互补摘要；3) 用轻量门控根据问题相关性将两者融合为一个主token，并与一个全局场景token结合，实现每帧仅2个token的极紧凑表示；4) 与传统全网格与token缩减基线对比评估。

Result: 在StreamGaze数据集上，GazeLens的宏平均与微平均准确率均高于所评估的全网格基线和token-reduction基线，且输入前向FLOPs低于共享骨干的token-reduction基线。

Conclusion: 将帧级选择与"视线+问题"引导的自适应token压缩结合，可在智能眼镜等受限硬件上以极低视觉token预算保留聚焦与上下文证据，是可行的端侧流式VQA方案；主要局限是验证仅限单一数据集与离线评估，缺少真实设备部署与延迟/功耗数据。

Abstract: Smart glasses can help users understand their activities and surroundings, but small vision-language models must preserve useful evidence within limited computation. This is difficult because redundant frames consume the visual budget, while aggressive spatial compression can discard relevant details. Although gaze identifies the wearer's focus, the question may require evidence from the surrounding scene. To address these constraints, we propose GazeLens, which combines frame selection with gaze- and question-guided token reduction. First, HOG transitions and image sharpness select frames before visual encoding. For each selected frame, Gaussian pooling then forms gaze-focused and complementary summaries. Because their relevance depends on the question, a lightweight gate blends them into a primary token. Together with a global scene token, this retains both focused and contextual summaries within two tokens per frame. On StreamGaze, GazeLens achieves higher macro and micro accuracy than the evaluated full-grid and token-reduction baselines, with lower input-forward FLOPs than the shared-backbone token-reduction baselines.

</details>


### [136] [Deep Learning-Based Road Lane Quality Assessment from Egocentric Dashcam Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28508)
*Shaif Chowdhury,Akhila Yeruvasu,Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy,Ayush Goyal,Dunren Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了南德州采集的63,839帧地理参考手机行车记录仪数据集，并提出三项无参考车道标线质量指标（CSI、LCR、RLCS）及基线模型，用于可扩展的车道标线质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车道标线的可见性、连续性与退化状况目前主要依赖人工巡检，而现有自动驾驶数据集侧重车道检测与场景理解，普遍缺乏面向养护的标线质量标注，导致质量评估难以规模化。

Method: 采集63,839帧路面前视视频（GPS手机行车记录仪），其中3,339帧与OpenStreetMap推得的车道数标签匹配作为弱监督；提出CSI（基于Laplacian响应的车道-背景对比度稳定性）、LCR（基于边缘与Hough直线的结构连续性比率）与RLCS（预测与地图车道数一致性的回归置信分），并训练回归基线模型。

Result: CSI基线MAE 0.039、R²=0.97，LCR MAE 0.093、R²=0.84；在50张人工排序图像的初步验证中，LCR与人工排序的一致性（Spearman ρ=0.7316，Kendall τ=0.5472）优于PSNR、SSIM、BRISQUE、NIQE、LPIPS等通用图像质量指标。

Conclusion: 数据集、无参考指标与基线为低成本、可规模化、位置感知的车道标线质量评估提供了起点；但人工验证规模小（50张），RLCS依赖弱监督地图标签的精度。

Abstract: Reliable lane markings are important for both human drivers and automated driving systems, but their condition is still commonly assessed through manual inspection.
