Existing autonomous-driving datasets primarily support lane detection and scene understanding and usually lack maintenance-oriented labels for lane-marking visibility, continuity, or degradation. In this study, we present a geo-referenced smartphone dashcam dataset and baseline framework for lane-marking quality assessment. The dataset contains 63,839 road-facing frames collected across South Texas using a GPS-enabled dashcam application. A subset of 3,339 frames is matched with OpenStreetMap-derived lane-count labels, which are used as weak supervision for lane-count regression and map-consistency evaluation. We propose three lane-quality measures: Contrast Stability Index (CSI), which captures lane-background contrast using Laplacian responses; Line Continuity Ratio (LCR), which estimates structural continuity using edge and Hough-line evidence; and Regression Lane Confidence Score (RLCS), which measures agreement between predicted and map-derived lane counts on matched frames. The baseline prediction models attained strong performance, with CSI (MAE $0.039$, $R^2=0.97$) and LCR (MAE $0.093$, $R^2=0.84$). Notably, CSI and LCR are no-reference, segmentation-derived quality metrics that can be computed without dense human labels. In a pilot validation using 50 human-ranked images, these proposed lane-specific metrics show stronger rank agreement with human lane-quality ordering (e.g., Spearman's $\rho=0.7316$ and Kendall's $\tau=0.5472$ for LCR) than general image-quality measures such as PSNR, SSIM, BRISQUE, NIQE, and LPIPS. We believe the dataset, metrics, and baseline provide an initial step toward scalable, low-cost, location-aware lane-marking quality assessment. Data and code: this https URL

</details>


### [137] [SenseAlign: Understanding Vision and Touch through Language at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28511)
*Chuyang Ye,Qinting Jiang,Jingyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseAlign: 冻结编码器的触觉-视觉-语言模型测试时自适应框架,用统一的类文本嵌入同时完成模态可靠性加权(Alignment Trust)与共享特征校正(Anchor Realignment),以无标签历史观测提升自然漂移与传感器退化下的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时照明变化、接触质量或传感器硬件更换会同时偏移视觉与触觉输入,导致识别退化;双模态融合时不可靠模态可能压倒另一模态的正确证据;部署阶段通常无标签,需用无标签观测做测试时自适应。作者观察到两点:累积观测与候选类文本嵌入的比对能揭示置信度无法反映的模态可靠性差异;对竞争类做特征精修比单纯几何对齐更有效。

Method: 以类文本嵌入作为公共语义参照。(1) Alignment Trust:根据各模态对共享预测类别的支持度计算模态权重,用于测试时加权融合。(2) Anchor Realignment:用同一类文本嵌入在无标签历史特征上拟合共享特征校正,使预测类与备选类分离。两模块共享同一历史缓冲区,全部编码器保持冻结,无反向传播更新。

Result: 在多个数据集上针对自然分布漂移与受控传感器/视觉损坏两类设置进行了广泛实验,识别性能均有显著提升,验证了模态加权与竞争类特征校正的有效性。

Conclusion: 测试时用文本语义空间同时承担"模态可信度评估"与"特征校正锚点"是恢复触视语言模型鲁棒性的有效途径;无需标签、无需训练编码器,适合实际部署。可作为触觉-视觉多模态融合的即插即用适配层。

Abstract: Tactile-visual-language models combine vision and touch through a shared language space to recognize objects and materials. In real-world deployment, changes in lighting, contact quality or sensor hardware can shift visual or tactile inputs and degrade recognition performance. When both modalities contribute to a prediction, an unreliable modality can override correct evidence from the other. Since labeled data are generally unavailable during deployment, test-time adaptation offers a way to recover recognition performance from unlabeled observations. In this setting, we find that comparing accumulated observations with the text embeddings of candidate classes reveals modality reliability differences that confidence can miss. Refining features against competing classes also improves recognition beyond geometric alignment alone. These findings motivate SenseAlign, which uses a common semantic reference for test-time modality weighting and feature correction. Alignment Trust weights each modality according to its support for shared predicted classes. Anchor Realignment uses the same class text embeddings to fit a shared feature correction that separates predicted classes from alternatives. Both modules use the same unlabeled history while keeping all encoders frozen. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate strong recognition performance under both natural shifts and controlled corruptions.

</details>


### [138] [Touch-JEPA: Tactile Representation Learning from Hand-Object Video with Limited Tactile Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28519)
*Minseong Kweon,Qiao Feng,Yiming Huang,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Touch-JEPA 用无触觉标注的手-物视频做掩码视觉特征预测自监督预训练，学到的表征可迁移到触觉接触判别与接触力大小估计任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉数据采集成本高、覆盖面窄，限制触觉表征学习；而手-物视频廉价且大规模，蕴含隐性接触动力学信息，因此希望以视频作为可扩展的自监督信号，并用少量触觉测量提供物理接地。

Method: 1) 纯自监督预训练：利用视频估计的手部几何，在对应手表面位置上对掩码视觉特征做时空上下文预测，迫使表征编码潜在接触动力学；2) 联合表征学习：所有视频都参与掩码预测，仅在带触觉标注的子集上加入触觉监督，使共享表征同时对齐测量到的接触与力大小。

Result: 仅自监督预训练 + 冻结特征上的简单监督读出，在三个人手基准的接触判别与触觉大小估计上优于监督基线，并在无机器人触觉训练数据的情况下迁移到机器人手；加入有限触觉监督的联合学习进一步提升人手基准性能。

Conclusion: 手-物视频可作为触觉表征学习的可扩展自监督来源，少量触觉测量提供互补的物理接地；为以视频为媒介的触觉/接触表征学习提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Understanding contact and tactile magnitude in hand-object interaction is a key step toward physically intelligent systems, yet learning such representations is limited by the cost and restricted coverage of tactile measurements. We present Touch-JEPA, a framework for learning tactile representations from hand-object videos with limited tactile supervision. We first introduce a self-supervised pretraining objective that learns from video alone without tactile supervision. Using video-derived hand geometry, Touch-JEPA predicts masked visual features at corresponding hand-surface locations from spatial and temporal context, encouraging the representation to capture latent contact dynamics. We then extend this formulation to joint representation learning, where masked prediction is applied to all videos while tactile supervision is available only for a labeled subset, grounding the shared representation in measured contact and magnitude. With self-supervised pretraining alone, simple supervised readouts on frozen Touch-JEPA features outperform supervised baselines in contact discrimination and tactile magnitude estimation across three human-hand benchmarks, and transfer to robotic hands without robot tactile training data. Joint learning with limited tactile supervision further improves contact and magnitude estimation on the human-hand benchmarks. Together, these results show that hand-object video can provide scalable self-supervision for tactile representation learning, while limited tactile measurements provide complementary physical grounding.

</details>


### [139] [Latent Bridge: From Video Latents to 3D Geometry via Feature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28521)
*Shuai Zhang,Zhanpeng Ouyang,Zhengqing Chen,Ruohong Mei,Shaoyu Chen,Weiqiang Ren,Hui Sun,Qian Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Bridge，在视频生成潜空间与几何特征空间之间训练桥梁，从视频潜变量直接预测相机、深度与点云，无需中间 RGB 解码/重编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成与前馈 3D 重建使用分离的表征，从生成潜变量恢复几何须经 RGB 解码再视觉编码，计算冗余且几何精度受损。目标是打通两个潜空间。

Method: 针对 Wan2.1 与 V-RAE 分别构建表征专用 bridge；在 RGB 视频上通过特征蒸馏训练，视频编码器与几何模型均冻结，不需要 GT 几何、也无需运行下游几何聚合器或预测头；推理时同一潜变量支持 RGB 解码与几何预测，同一 bridge 可复用于 I2V/T2V。

Result: 在 Co3Dv2 与 WildRGB-D 上取得有竞争力的几何重建质量，端到端生成-重建高效，潜变量到几何的解码速度更快。

Conclusion: 潜空间对齐可作为视频生成与 3D 重建之间实用、可复用的接口；但未涉及动态/交互场景与机器人实机验证。

Abstract: Video generation and feed-forward 3D reconstruction typically use separate representations, requiring RGB decoding and visual re-encoding to recover geometry from generated latents. We propose Latent Bridge, which connects video generative latent spaces with geometric feature spaces to directly predict 3D geometry from video latents. We develop representation-specific bridges for Wan2.1 and V-RAE, enabling feed-forward prediction of cameras, depth, and 3D point clouds without the intermediate RGB pathway. Each bridge is trained on RGB videos through feature distillation, while the video encoder and geometry model remain frozen. Training requires neither ground-truth geometry nor execution of the downstream geometric aggregator or prediction heads. At inference, the same video latents support both RGB decoding and geometry prediction. The same trained bridge can be reused for image-to-video and text-to-video generation within the same video latent space, without bridge retraining. Experiments on Co3Dv2 and WildRGB-D demonstrate competitive geometric reconstruction quality, efficient end-to-end generation and reconstruction, and faster latent-to-geometry decoding, highlighting latent alignment as a practical and reusable interface between video generation and 3D reconstruction. Our code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [Copyright Leakage Detection in AI Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28524)
*Shaif Chowdhury,Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向AI生成视频的版权泄露检测基准数据集（50个版权概念×5图，每概念5个视频）与基于对比检索的工具集，将检测建模为视频→图像检索，用单一相似度阈值判定并返回最相似参考图作为证据，检测准确率99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型能力提升引发对受版权保护视觉内容被复现的担忧，但生成视频中的版权泄露检测几乎未被研究，缺乏基准与可解释的检测手段。

Method: 构建基准数据集：50个版权概念、每概念5张参考图，并用图像+提示词与纯提示词两类模型各生成5个视频。开发AI工具集，采用对比检索框架：聚合视频帧嵌入，与版权图像嵌入库做视频到图像检索，单一相似度阈值判定泄露并检索最相似参考图作为支撑证据。

Result: 检索与检测性能较强：检测准确率99.6%，仅1例假阴性；数据集与源码公开。

Conclusion: 基于检索的方法可扩展且可解释，适合生成视频的版权泄露检测，但属数据集/基准与工具类工作，与物理AI、VLA、世界模型、3D重建等核心方向关联弱。

Abstract: Video generation models can create realistic-looking video footage from images and text prompts. Some of the recent advances have raised concerns about the reproduction of copyrighted visual content. However, copyright leakage detection in generated videos remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new benchmark data set for copyright leakage detection, comprising 50 copyrighted concepts, each with 5 images. For each concept, we have 5 videos created using the image+prompt video generation model and the prompt-only video generation model. Following that, we develop an AI toolset based on a contrastive retrieval framework that formulates copyright leakage detection as a video-to-image retrieval problem by matching aggregated video frame embeddings against a database of copyrighted image embeddings. Our method uses a single similarity threshold and retrieves the most similar copyrighted reference as supporting evidence. We conducted experiments showing strong retrieval and detection performance, achieving 99.6\% detection accuracy with only a single false negative. These results show the effectiveness of the retrieval-based method for scalable and explainable copyright leakage detection in AI-generated videos. Data can be accessed publicly from this https URL and the source code for this project is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [141] [CoGaussian: Open-Vocabulary Gaussian-based Collaborative 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28530)
*Om Shivam Verma,Shaya Farahmand,Lingjun Zhao,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoGaussian：基于基础模型对齐稀疏3D高斯的多智能体开放词汇协同语义占据预测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同语义占据方法依赖稠密3D体素标注，且只能识别预定义语义类别，泛化与扩展性受限。

Method: 将场景表示为与基础模型对齐的稀疏3D高斯集合，作为多智能体间通信与融合几何/语义信息的紧凑表征，实现开放词汇协同语义占据预测。

Result: 在Semantic-OPV2V基准上优于现有开放词汇单智能体方法。

Conclusion: 开放词汇协同场景理解有效，高斯紧凑表示可支撑多智能体开放词汇感知。

Abstract: Collaborative perception enables agents to retrieve the scene semantics and geometry beyond the field of view of any individual agent. This broader scene understanding enhances transportation safety and improves efficiency in multi-robot systems. However, existing collaborative semantic occupancy methods rely on dense 3D voxel annotations and are limited to recognizing objects within a predefined set of semantic classes. To overcome these limitations, we present CoGaussian, an open-vocabulary Gaussian-based collaborative semantic occupancy prediction framework. CoGaussian represents a scene as a set of foundation model-aligned sparse 3D Gaussians, which serve as a compact representation for communicating and fusing geometric and semantic information across multiple agents. On the Semantic-OPV2V benchmark, our method outperforms existing open-vocabulary single-agent approaches, highlighting the effectiveness of open-vocabulary collaborative scene understanding.

</details>


### [142] [Predictive Latents for Autoregressive Language Models: Possibility, Gauge, and Obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28540)
*Yuzhou Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该笔记在同一潜变量过程上区分三种风险（token 读出风险 R_tok、教师强制的单步潜风险 R_1、开环迭代风险 R_H），证明当 Bayes 后验可由满秩 k 维统计量表示时，宽度 k 潜变量 + 线性最小二乘转移 + 重拟合 unembedding 即可匹配 Bayes 预测器，且统计量仅在可逆 gauge 下可辨识，并指出 R_1 小、R_tok 小都不蕴含开环 rollout 可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期若干自回归系统在不输出 token 的情况下迭代隐状态（continuous thoughts、pause steps、recurrent depth），但现有训练目标只监督 next-token 交叉熵，缺少对“隐式迭代过程”本身风险的刻画，易把单步 teacher-forced 损失误当作闭环 rollout 可靠性的证明。

Method: 理论分析（笔记/position paper）：将 token 风险 R_tok、单步 teacher-forced 潜风险 R_1 与开环迭代风险 R_H 形式化到同一潜过程上；在 Bayes next-token 后验为满秩协方差 k 维统计量函数的假设下，构造宽度 k 潜变量、线性最小二乘转移与重拟合 unembedding，并检验可辨识性（gauge 变换）与失配障碍。

Result: 给出匹配结论：上述最小构造可复现 Bayes token 预测器；统计量仅可辨识至可逆变换，白化残差流在重拟合 unembedding 后不改变 token 分布。同时给出尖锐反例：常数潜在状态使 R_1→0 而 token 交叉熵退化为边际熵；完美 token 预测器仍可隐藏零空间漂移，使开环误差随 horizon 线性增长。

Conclusion: 小 next-latent 损失不能作为 next-token 预测的证书，小 next-token 损失也不能保证自由潜 rollout 的可靠性，除非同时给出秩或单射性条件；评估隐式迭代/连续思维系统时必须显式报告 R_H 与秩条件。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models are trained by next-token cross-entropy, yet several recent systems also iterate a hidden state without emitting a token: continuous thoughts, pause steps, and recurrent depth. This note separates three risks on one latent process. The token risk $R_{\mathrm{tok}}$ scores a readout. The teacher-forced latent risk $R_1$ scores a one-step map that sees the true hidden state. The open-loop risk $R_H$ scores the same map after it has been iterated on its own outputs. If the Bayes next-token posterior is a function of a $k$-dimensional statistic with full-rank covariance, a width-$k$ latent, a linear least-squares transition, and a refit unembedding match the Bayes token predictor. The statistic is identified only up to an invertible gauge: whitening the residual stream does not change the token law once the unembedding is refit. The matching obstructions are sharp. A constant latent drives $R_1$ to zero while token cross-entropy falls back to the marginal entropy. A perfect token predictor can still hide a null-space walk whose open-loop error grows linearly in the horizon. A small next-latent loss does not certificate next-token prediction, and a small next-token loss does not certificate free latent rollout, unless a rank or injectivity condition is stated with the number.

</details>


### [143] [OverSeer: Multi-Agent World Models Beyond What Agents See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28554)
*Runjia Li,Kam Woh Ng,Minghao Chen,Junlin Han,Jianyuan Wang,Zhaochong An,Juan-Manuel Perez-Rua,Andrea Vedaldi,Shikun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OverSeer 是一个多智能体视频世界模型，通过引入空间固定的辅助相机视角作为"世界中心"表示的代理，并配合 delta-VAE 压缩冗余信息，显著提升多智能体生成的时间一致性与智能体间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型多针对单智能体，扩展到多智能体需要同时生成多条智能体专属视频流，并保证时间与跨智能体一致性。难点在于自中心视角随智能体位置微小变化而发生全局性剧烈变化，难以作为世界状态的可控表示。

Method: 在自中心智能体视角之外，附加若干空间固定的辅助相机视角，作为世界中心表示代理：其变化直接对应局部世界状态变化。针对辅助视角冗余、半静态的特性，提出 delta-VAE，仅编码相对静态参考图像的差分，大幅减少 token 数量与计算开销。

Result: 在 Minecraft 与 Doom 中，世界一致性与智能体一致性均有提升；真实世界多相机采集上的定性结果说明方法不局限于游戏引擎；压缩分析显示 delta-VAE 显著降低辅助视角生成的算力开销。

Conclusion: 空间固定辅助视角是一种简单而高效的多智能体世界模型一致性增强手段，配合差分压缩可在低额外成本下获得稳定的世界中心表示。

Abstract: Despite the success of video world models, extending them to multiple agents is difficult because it requires generating several agent-specific video streams that are consistent in time and between agents. To address this gap, we introduce OverSeer, a multi-agent world model that augments egocentric agent views with auxiliary camera views. We suggest that, by fixing the auxiliary views in space, they act as a proxy for a world-centric representation: Unlike the agents' views, which can change entirely due to a simple change in agent position, changes in the auxiliary views directly map to localized changes in the world state. We show that these fixed views substantially help the model to maintain consistency. We also show that, due to their redundant and semi-static nature, the auxiliary views can be efficiently compressed with a delta-VAE that encodes only the changes relative to a static reference image, reducing the number of tokens required to process them. Experiments in Minecraft and Doom demonstrate improved world and agent consistency, with qualitative results on real-world multi-camera captures indicating that the approach is not tied to game engines. Our compression analysis further shows that delta-VAE substantially reduces the computational overhead of auxiliary-view generation. We conclude that these fixed views provide a simple and efficient way to improve multi-agent world models.

</details>


### [144] [Continual Concept Personalization in Diffusion Models via Residual-Space Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28567)
*Yuzhi Liu,Enis Simsar,Giulia Lanzillotta,Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将残差空间适配引入预训练扩散模型的随机适配过程，解决概念按序到达的持续概念个性化问题，并利用残差空间动力学定位适配容量集中的层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像个性化方法通常独立训练每个概念或假设所有概念同时可用；实际应用中概念常顺序到达，需在持续学习新主体/物体/风格时最小化对已有概念的干扰，即持续概念个性化问题。

Method: 把残差空间适配扩展到预训练扩散模型的随机适配过程中；分析所学习到的残差空间动力学以定位适配容量集中的层，并仅对这些层进行适配（层选择性适配）。

Result: 在持续个性化方法中取得最高概念保真度且几乎零遗忘；仅适配识别出的层可保持接近同等性能并将可训练参数减少最多72%。

Conclusion: 残差空间动力学可揭示扩散模型内适配容量的分布，并直接指导更参数高效的持续概念个性化适配。

Abstract: Text-to-image personalization aims to equip pretrained diffusion models with the ability to learn user-specific concepts from only a few reference images. Existing methods, however, typically train each concept independently or assume that all concepts are available simultaneously. In practical applications, concepts often arrive sequentially, requiring models to continually learn new subjects, objects, or styles while minimizing interference with previously acquired concepts. We address this continual concept personalization setting by extending residual-space adaptation to the stochastic adaptation process of pretrained diffusion models. Our work achieves the highest concept fidelity among competing continual personalization methods while maintaining near-zero forgetting. Beyond these empirical gains, the learned residual-space dynamics reveal where adaptation capacity is concentrated within the diffusion model. Restricting adaptation to the identified layers by our method preserves nearly the same performance while reducing the number of trainable parameters by up to 72\%, demonstrating that residual-space dynamics can identify the layers that contribute most to continual concept personalization and directly guide more parameter-efficient adaptation.

</details>


### [145] [Can a Display Be Used for Rapid LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28571)
*Zuhao Zou,Xiangcheng Hu,Jianhao Jiao,Jin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScreenCalib 利用普通可编程显示屏（手机、平板、笔电、显示器、电视）作为标定板，实现 LiDAR-相机外参标定，通过标签引导的屏幕提取、几何假设生成与精化、以及周围场景边缘消歧，并支持单姿态与联合多姿态精化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用标定靶标制作与摆放成本高；显示屏本身能同时提供已知视觉标记（AprilTag）与受控几何，但 LiDAR 与相机之间需间接跨模态关联，存在屏幕几何导致的关联歧义与室内杂波干扰。

Method: 三部分流程：(1) tag 引导的显示屏提取；(2) 几何假设生成与精化；(3) 利用周围场景边缘消歧。单姿态模式仅需一次静态放置；联合多姿态模式通过跨姿态一致性融合多个独立静态观测，联合优化共享外参与标签尺度，避免连续运动与姿态间轨迹估计，降低标签尺寸测量误差敏感性。仿真与三套传感器平台（含机械式与非重复式 LiDAR，干净与杂乱环境）。

Result: 在平移精度上优于基于标定板的对比方法；联合多姿态精化进一步降低三平台的平均旋转与平移误差，并改善渲染地图对齐；多类消费级显示屏上的评估验证了通用性。

Conclusion: 显示屏可替代专用标定靶作为实用的 LiDAR-相机标定方案，且多姿态联合精化能显著提升精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: ScreenCalib enables light detection and ranging (LiDAR)--camera extrinsic calibration using an ordinary programmable display, providing known visual markers and controlled geometry while reducing dedicated target preparation. The camera observes displayed AprilTags, whereas the LiDAR measures the physical screen surface and boundary, requiring indirect cross-modal association. Display geometry introduces association ambiguities, while indoor clutter complicates extraction. ScreenCalib addresses these challenges through tag-guided display extraction, geometric hypothesis generation and refinement, and surrounding scene edges for ambiguity resolution. The single-pose mode requires only one static placement. The joint multi-pose mode combines independent static observations through cross-pose consensus and jointly refines the shared extrinsics and tag scale, reducing sensitivity to tag-size measurement errors without requiring continuous motion or inter-pose trajectory estimation. Experiments in simulation and on three different sensor platforms cover mechanical and nonrepetitive LiDARs in clean and cluttered environments. Benchmark results show that ScreenCalib outperforms competitive calibration-board-based methods in translation accuracy. Joint multi-pose refinement further reduces mean rotation and translation errors across the three platforms and improves rendered-map alignment. Evaluation across readily available displays, including cell phones, tablets, laptops, computer monitors, and televisions, further examines ScreenCalib's versatility as a practical alternative to dedicated calibration targets. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [146] [WildMAE: Structure-Aware Masked Autoencoding for Wildlife Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28590)
*Yicheng Zhang,Yujie Jin,Ke Zhang,Jiaxin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildMAE 在 MAE 解码器端加入零门控卷积残差（局部结构先验）与掩码到可见的交叉注意力，并配合归一化像素、Sobel 边缘、感知的渐进监督，在野生动摄影（Snapshot Serengeti）上改善重建指标，但所报告数字为历史协议下结果，非统一复现比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱图像存在外观、光照、背景杂乱与视觉退化等显著变化，MAE 的无标签重建范式适合建模此类数据；但原始 MAE 解码器缺乏局部空间先验与显式的掩码-可见特征交互，重建质量受限。作者希望在保留 Transformer 解码器全局混合能力的前提下，补足空间细节与定向上下文检索。

Method: 解码器侧扩展：(1) 零门控卷积残差路径引入二维邻域先验，实现局部结构感知细化；(2) 门控交叉注意力使掩码位置从可见位置特征检索上下文，且不替换原 Transformer 解码器；(3) 训练配方渐进引入归一化像素损失、Sobel 边缘损失与感知损失。

Result: 在 Snapshot Serengeti 原始研究的 4,744 张测试图、75% 掩码率下，WildMAE 与 vanilla MAE 分别取得 27.90 vs 24.33 dB PSNR、0.840 vs 0.780 SSIM、0.160 vs 0.320 LPIPS；另有历史记录显示四类合成退化条件下平均差异更优，并给出组件变体描述。

Conclusion: WildMAE 为"全局 token 混合 + 空间与定向上下文细化"的组合提供了一个实用的架构案例；但所保留指标依赖历史协议，既非新近复现的统一对比，也不构成对真实缺失像素恢复能力的验证，结论适用范围需据此限定。

Abstract: Camera-trap imagery presents substantial variation in animal appearance, illumination, background clutter, and visual degradation. Masked autoencoders (MAEs) offer a label-free framework for modeling such data through reconstruction of randomly hidden image patches. We present WildMAE, a decoder-side extension of MAE that combines local structure-aware refinement with masked-to-visible cross-attention. A zero-gated convolutional residual path introduces a two-dimensional neighborhood prior, while gated cross-attention allows masked positions to retrieve contextual information from visible-position features without replacing the original Transformer decoder. The associated training recipe progressively introduces normalized-pixel, Sobel-edge, and perceptual objectives. In the original Snapshot Serengeti study, covering 4,744 test images at 75% masking, the reported reconstruction scores were 27.90 versus 24.33 dB PSNR, 0.840 versus 0.780 SSIM, and 0.160 versus 0.320 LPIPS for WildMAE and vanilla MAE, respectively. Additional historical records document favorable average differences under four synthetic degradation conditions and describe component variants. We present the method, experimental observations, and their scope together: the retained metrics are historical protocol-dependent results, rather than a newly reproduced, harmonized comparison or a validation of restoration from genuinely unavailable pixels. WildMAE provides a practical architectural case study for combining global token mixing with spatial and targeted contextual refinement in wildlife masked-image modeling.

</details>


### [147] [Learning the 4D World through Point Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28594)
*Byungwoo Jeon,Sunghwan Hong,Dohyeon Kim,Jonghoon Lee,Jungwoo Park,Inhee Lee,Doosan Baek,Jinil Kim,Taehyun Lim,Hanbyul Joo,Marc Pollefeys,Saining Xie,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Video-Point Model (VPM)，一个以多视角视频历史与语言指令为条件的世界模型，联合预测任意视角的未来视频与持久场景点的3D轨迹；点轨迹监督跨所有视角锚定同一物理点，使外观变化与共享3D运动描述对齐，并扩展到机器人动作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角预测要求不同相机视频描述同一个演化中的3D场景，但纯视频生成模型缺乏跨视角一致的3D运动约束，容易产生视角间不一致的外观与运动。作者希望用一种共享的3D运动表示来锚定各视角的预测。

Method: 在预训练世界模型上扩展相机条件化的多视角处理模块以融合互补观测；增加轨迹头预测持久3D点随时间的运动，每条轨迹跨所有视角跟踪同一点；轨迹监督与视频生成联合训练。构建 VPM-Data（同步多视角视频+持久点轨迹仿真数据）与 VP-WorldBench 评测基准。进一步将机器人动作作为token模态加入生成器，与未来视频联合去噪，共享点监督主干。

Result: 在 VP-WorldBench 与现有4D生成基准上，加入点监督相比纯视频对照模型提升了视频保真度和3D运动精度；在仿真与真实机器人实验中，动作使能模型的操控成功率也得到提升。

Conclusion: 点轨迹监督是一种有效的跨视角一致性正则，可同时改善视频生成质量与3D运动准确性，并能把策略接地到同一3D感知表示中服务于机器人操控。

Abstract: Reliable multi-view forecasting requires videos from different cameras to describe the same evolving 3D scene. To this end, we introduce Video-Point Model (VPM), a world model that conditions on multi-view video histories and a language instruction to jointly forecast future videos from arbitrary viewpoints and the 3D trajectories of persistent scene points. We extend a pretrained world model with camera-conditioned multi-view processing that integrates complementary observations across cameras, and add a trajectory head that predicts how physical points move through time. Each trajectory tracks a single point across all views, and supervising these trajectories alongside video generation therefore anchors appearance changes in every camera to a shared description of 3D motion. To support training and evaluation, we build VPM-Data, a simulation corpus pairing synchronized multi-view videos with persistent point trajectories, and VP-WorldBench, a forecasting benchmark spanning simulated and real environments. We further extend VPM to robot control by adding robot actions as a token modality in the generator. Action tokens share the point-supervised backbone and are denoised jointly with future videos, thereby grounding the policy in the same 3D-aware representation. On VP-WorldBench and existing 4D generation benchmarks, point supervision improves both video fidelity and 3D motion accuracy over a video-only counterpart, and in simulated and real-robot experiments, it also raises the manipulation success of the action-enabled model.

</details>


### [148] [Neural Implicit Energy Optimization for LiDAR Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28598)
*Hrishikesh Viswanath,Md Ashiqur Rahman,Chi Lin,Damon Conover,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将神经隐式 LiDAR 建图表述为在神经隐式参数空间上最小化能量泛函，用基于形状算子（曲率）的能量项正则化 SDF，实现大规模 LiDAR 场景平滑重建且无推理时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经隐式表示虽内存高效，但从稀疏、含噪 LiDAR 输入恢复的几何存在抖动与伪影；经典几何优化用表面能量泛函可获得光滑极小曲面，作者希望把这一经典几何先验迁移到百万点级神经隐式建图规模。

Method: 在神经隐式 SDF 参数空间中优化能量泛函：以 SDF 水平集的形状算子（shape operator，刻画表面曲率）构造正则项，鼓励沿表面方向的平滑性，并使其适配百万点级 LiDAR 地图的可扩展性；无需额外推理成本。

Result: 在大规模数据集上，重建结果平滑度提升且几何保真度保持，推理阶段无额外开销；代码开源。

Conclusion: 经典曲率型表面能量先验可有效适配神经隐式 LiDAR 建图，是提升几何质量的低成本、可扩展正则化手段。

Abstract: Neural implicit representations provide memory-efficient signed distance field (SDF) parameterizations for large-scale LiDAR scenes, but the geometry recovered from sparse and often noisy inputs carries jitter and artifacts. Classical geometric optimization techniques resolve such artifacts with surface energy functionals, whose minimizers correspond to smooth surfaces. Motivated by this principle, we formulate neural LiDAR mapping as the optimization of energy functionals over the parameter space of neural implicit representations. Specifically, we regularize the signed distance field with an energy functional defined on the shape operator of its level set, which represents the curvature of the surface, thus encouraging smoothness along the surface. This adapts classical geometric priors to the scale of million-point LiDAR maps. Experiments on large-scale datasets show reconstructions with improved smoothness at preserved geometric fidelity, and no additional inference-time cost. The code is publicly available at \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }

</details>


### [149] [KoalaGS: Complexity-Aware Continuous Level of Detail for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28615)
*Radu Beche,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KoalaGS利用优化前由输入视图计算的图像复杂度先验，在训练中引导高斯重定位、并给出基元重要性评分，实现3DGS场景在连续LoD下按预算裁剪仍保持质量与平滑降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算预算收缩时渲染3DGS场景，需要同一套高斯基元在小前缀（低容量）下依然有效；关键难题是决定随预算下降应保留哪些高斯。对3DGS-MCMC重建的初步研究发现：图像复杂度能预测重建误差在图像与3D空间的集中位置，且简单区域高斯过多、低预算下退化程度与复杂区域相当。

Method: 先以输入视图计算图像复杂度作为先验；训练时将重定位偏置到重建较差区域，同时保证跨LoD平滑退化；再用基元重要性评分支持低容量推理以保持场景结构。

Result: 在三个数据集上，KoalaGS在全容量下质量保持竞争力，跨预算区间质量显著提升，LoD切换更平滑，并支持更快的渐进式流式传输。

Conclusion: 复杂度先验与重要性评分结合可有效解决3DGS连续LoD下的容量分配问题，兼顾全容量质量与低预算可用性。

Abstract: Deploying a trained 3D Gaussian Splatting scene under shrinking compute budgets requires one primitive set to remain effective when rendered at smaller prefixes. In this continuous level of detail (LoD) setting, the challenge is to decide which Gaussians should retain capacity as the budget decreases. A pilot study on 3DGS-MCMC reconstructions shows that image complexity, computed from the input views before optimization, predicts where reconstruction error concentrates in image and 3D space. It also reveals that simple regions contain too many Gaussians and degrade as much as complex ones at lower budgets, despite being inherently cheaper to represent. Subsequently, in KoalaGS, during training we use this complexity prior to bias relocation toward poorly reconstructed areas while ensuring smooth degradation across LoDs. A primitive importance score then enables inference at low-capacity, while preserving the scene structure. Tested across three datasets, KoalaGS maintains competitive full-capacity quality while significantly improving quality across the budget spectrum, yielding smoother transitions across LoDs, and enabling faster progressive streaming.

</details>


### [150] [MindView: Benchmarking Generative Multi-View Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28622)
*Yanbo Wang,Siqi Luo,Siyu Zhang,Chenming Zhu,Wenzhao Zheng,Qian Yu,Juncheng Yan,Bin Fu,Tai Wang,Yu Qiao,Yihao Liu,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MindView 多视角空间智能生成基准（1500样本/513场景/7任务），并提出 BAGEL 微调与免训练证据选择器 MindCue。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导下从部分多视角观测生成空间一致视角的能力缺乏系统评测；现有生成模型在扩展上下文下表现下降。

Method: 构建 MindView 基准，任务分几何主导（指定相机位姿变化）与语义主导（依据指称物体、空间关系与参考系推断视角）；评测11个生成器；任务特定微调 BAGEL；提出基于目标视角假设引导的免训练空间证据选择器 MindCue。

Result: 11个生成器均有较大提升空间；微调后 BAGEL 总分提升24.3分，七项任务全增益，且在三个未训练的空间理解基准上迁移提升；MindCue 逆转扩展上下文下的性能下降并超过标准上下文基线。

Conclusion: MindView 为生成式多视角空间智能提供了可测试平台，生成空间一致视角可促进空间理解。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence has recently been assessed through generative tasks such as spatially grounded image editing, yet language-guided generation of spatially consistent views from partial multi-view observations remains underexplored. We introduce MindView, a benchmark for generative multi-view spatial intelligence with 1,500 samples from 513 scenes across three indoor datasets. Its seven tasks form two families: geometry-dominant tasks specify changes in camera position and orientation, while semantics-dominant tasks require inferring target viewpoints by grounding referred objects and interpreting spatial relations and reference-frame cues. Evaluation of eleven generators reveals substantial room for improvement on MindView. A paired context analysis further shows that all eleven generators obtain lower overall scores under the expanded context setting. These findings motivate us to investigate whether task-specific supervision can improve target-view generation and whether models can use additional multi-view evidence effectively. We therefore fine-tune BAGEL on the MindView training dataset, improving its overall score by 24.3 points and yielding gains across all seven MindView tasks. Beyond these generation gains, the fine-tuned model also yields higher scores on three spatial-understanding benchmarks without training on their annotations, suggesting that learning to generate spatially grounded views may benefit spatial understanding. To improve evidence utilization under expanded context, we propose MindCue, a training-free spatial evidence selector guided by a target-view hypothesis. MindCue reverses the drop in overall score under expanded context and outperforms the standard-context baseline. Overall, MindView provides a concrete testbed for generative multi-view spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [151] [PsycheMME: A Psychology-Informed Benchmark for Social Understanding and Interaction in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28625)
*Heran Hu,Yuhang Dong,Zhucun Xue,Kaiwei Chen,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PsycheMME：心理学启发的视频基准，用八项能力（grounding、states、needs、interaction decisions、memory、risk）将"理解人"与"选择回应"分离，并提出 Reasoning-Verified 协议按显式理由的支撑度与逻辑一致性加权计分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合准确率会掩盖"理解人类需求"与"选择回应"这两类不同判断的差异，现有视频基准缺乏心理学理论指导的、可分离理解与决策的评价框架。

Method: 基于 Social Information Processing 与 Perception-Action Cycle 组织八项能力；从 630 个片段构建 1000 道题，采用"独立作答、隐藏参考、专家审核"流程；提出 Reasoning-Verified 协议，依据理由对标注线索的支撑与逻辑一致性对答案给予加权分数；评测 17 个模型，并做输入控制与 SFT/GRPO 实验。

Result: Timing 维度的 Verified 分数与分数保留率最低，模型对正确 Timing 答案的支撑判断更弱；Need 分数相近的模型在开放题答案完整性上仍有差异；通过答案与理由联合评估定位了模型在理解、回应选择与论证三个环节的失败点。

Conclusion: 将理由纳入评分可区分表面正确与真正理解，为人类理解与交互决策能力提供可诊断的评测视角；属于基准类工作，未提供可迁移的方法洞见。

Abstract: Understanding human needs and selecting responses require distinct judgments that aggregate accuracy can obscure. We introduce PsycheMME, a video benchmark informed by psychology, with eight capabilities spanning grounding, states, needs, interaction decisions, memory, and risk. Drawing on Social Information Processing and the Perception-Action Cycle, this organization separates human understanding from response selection. We construct 1,000 questions from 630 episodes through independent answering with proposed references withheld and expert review. Our Reasoning-Verified protocol weights answer credit by the support and logical consistency of explicit rationales against annotated cues. Across 17 models, Timing has the lowest Verified scores and credit retention, with weaker support judgments for correct Timing answers. Models with similar Need scores still differ in answer completeness on open questions. Input controls and SFT/GRPO examine context dependence and adaptation. Evaluating answers alongside rationales locates where models fail across understanding, response selection, and justification.

</details>


### [152] [SolarRenderer: Few-Step Generative Reference Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28630)
*Guian Fang,Shengju Qian,Dongyue Lu,Junchao Huang,Lutao Jiang,Xin Wang,Yiren Song,Li Jiang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SolarRenderer：基于冻结的 MiniMax-H3 33B 视频骨干，用两个轻量 LoRA 做结构条件视频渲染；结构视频以纯文本描述的形式进入原生参考通路，4 步蒸馏生成（比 50 步快 12.3×），并有块因果变体实现 768p 实时流式渲染；构建 136K 帧对齐语料，在 16 种结构表示基准上取得 SOTA 并泛化到 11 种未见表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏引擎与编码智能体只能给出场景的布局、运动与相机等粗粒度结构，需渲染器既遵循结构又补全外观；现有渲染器受限于单一引擎表示、需数十步去噪或自蒸馏、且训练条件与目标视频不对齐。

Method: 适配冻结 33B 视频扩散骨干：结构视频不附加类型标识、仅以自然语言描述，放置于其所述帧位置进入 backbone 原生 reference 通路，使单一控制 LoRA 兼容任意结构表示；在预训练 4 步 LoRA 上做结构引导的分布匹配（distribution matching）蒸馏，保留已学控制能力；另设计块因果（block-causal）变体支持流式实时渲染。

Result: 4 步渲染较 50 步采样加速 12.3×；块因果变体实现 768p 实时流式输出；分布内测试集与 16 种结构表示的泛化基准上均达 SOTA，并泛化至 11 种未见表示（姿态骨架、手绘、点云等）。

Conclusion: 将结构条件统一为"帧位对齐 + 文本描述"的模态无关参考输入，是构建通用、少步、可流式结构条件视频渲染器的有效途径，且可借助大规模帧对齐语料获得强跨表示泛化能力。

Abstract: Game engines and coding agents can specify the layout, motion, and camera of a scene, yet turning such coarse structure into realistic video requires a renderer that follows the structure and completes the appearance it leaves open. Existing renderers are tied to the representation of one engine, need tens of denoising steps or a costly distillation of their own, and learn from conditions that are not aligned with their target videos. We present SolarRenderer, a structure-conditioned video renderer that adapts a frozen MiniMax-H3 33B backbone with two lightweight LoRAs. Structural videos enter the native reference pathway of the backbone at the positions of the frames they describe, without a type identifier and described only in plain text, so that a single control LoRA follows arbitrary structural representations. Starting from a pretrained four-step LoRA, structure-guided distribution matching renders in four steps, 12.3 times faster than 50-step sampling, while preserving the learned control. A block-causal variant extends the same design to streaming and renders 768p video in real time. To train the model, we build a frame-aligned corpus of 136K clips from game engines, simulators, and YouTube videos. Experiments on an in-distribution test set and a generalization benchmark of 16 structural representations show that SolarRenderer achieves state-of-the-art results across both, and generalizes to 11 unseen representations, including pose skeletons, drawings, and point clouds.

</details>


### [153] [AEON: Unifying Video and 3D World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28631)
*Weiqi Zhang,Wenyuan Zhang,Yu-Shen Liu,Junsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AEON 是一个统一的世界模型框架，在文本/图像条件化的视频潜空间中生成可渲染的 4D Gaussian Splatting 显式世界表示，从而兼得视频生成模型的真实感与 3D 生成模型的几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显式世界模拟分两派：视频生成模型（如 Sora）外观逼真但缺乏 3D 一致性；3D 生成模型（如 Marble）几何一致但静态、真实度不足。需要统一二者优势。

Method: 在条件视频潜空间内做生成，输出可渲染的 4DGS；先用单目视频训练 4D 重建模型（预测 4DGS 与相机位姿）作为初始化的 4D 解码器；再通过视频 VAE 上的蒸馏设计域适配器，把生成视频潜变量映射为重建式 4D 潜变量。

Result: AEON 可在预测的相机轨迹上渲染 4DGS 以支持视频式世界建模，并构建具场景动态的持久 4D 环境；在标准基准上取得 SOTA，覆盖多种世界建模应用。

Conclusion: 以 4DGS 为显式表示、以视频潜空间为生成先验的桥接式设计，为动态世界模拟提供了兼具真实感与几何一致性的统一范式。

Abstract: Current world models for explicit world simulation typically follow two distinct pathways: (1) video generative models (e.g., Sora), which simulate realistic appearances but fail to preserve 3D consistency; and (2) 3D generative models (e.g., Marble), which generate geometrically consistent scenes but often yield static, less realistic observations. We present AEON, a unified framework that bridges video-based and 3D-based world models and combines the benefits of both. The key insight of AEON lies in a generative model that operates within the text- or image-conditioned video latent space while producing renderable 4D graphics primitives---specifically, 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS)---as the explicit world representation. We train a 4D reconstruction model to predict both 4DGS and camera poses from monocular videos, serving as our initialized 4D decoder. Furthermore, we design a domain adapter that translates generative video latents into reconstructive 4D latents through a distillation process on the video VAE. AEON naturally supports video-based world modeling by rendering 4DGS at predicted camera trajectories, and constructs a persistent 4D environment with scene dynamics for realistic world simulation. Consequently, AEON empowers a diverse range of world modeling applications and achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [154] [Joints, Camera, Action! Co-Generating 3D Motion and Video for Arbitrary Rigs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28632)
*Noam Rotstein,Yoad Tewel,Chen Tessler,Haggai Maron,Gal Chechik,Or Litany,Yoni Kasten,Yuval Atzmon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将视频生成与3D运动解耦的联合生成模型：保留独立支持任意骨架的3D运动模型，通过少量cross-attention层与预训练视频模型耦合，同时预测关节在视频帧中的位置以恢复相机并将3D运动注册到场景，并用生成式视频编辑器构建约20万"视频-骨架运动"配对训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型能产生逼真运动，但输出仅为像素，无法直接把该运动转换为可重放的、置于原3D场景中的骨架动画（例如让3D猴子重放视频中的跳跃）。已有video-motion方法要么只支持人体骨架且需额外数十亿参数，要么通过生成2D pose map再三角化，泛化性与骨架通用性不足。

Method: 1) 架构解耦：单独的3D运动模型（支持任意rig），通过小规模cross-attention层接入预训练视频扩散模型实现视频与3D运动协同生成；2) 运动模型可先在易获取、可增广的motion-only数据上预训练骨架运动先验；3) 模型额外监督"关节–像素"对应，用于恢复相机位姿，把3D运动放回3D场景，实现可重放；4) 数据构建：用生成式视频编辑器把带动画的3D资产嵌入与其动作匹配的场景，并筛除破坏已知运动有效性的编辑，结合人与机器人视频，共约200K配对样本。

Result: 实验显示：相比video-first流水线，能从文本更忠实驱动多种骨架；生成的运动能响应周围场景；支持视频→rig运动、rig运动→视频、以及基于关键帧的3D运动控制四种应用。

Conclusion: 将"任意骨架3D运动建模"与"视频外观生成"解耦、再用轻量cross-attention桥接，是一条可扩展的路径：既避免巨量参数扩展，也无需2D三角化，且天然支持相机恢复与3D场景内的运动重放；生成式视频编辑可规模化产出骨骼多样化训练配对。

Abstract: Video generation models can now depict remarkably realistic motion. Yet it is unclear how to use this motion beyond pixels: to animate a specific 3D asset and replay the animation in its 3D scene. For instance, a video model can show a monkey jumping over a crossbar, but it cannot return that jump as replayable 3D motion placed in the 3D scene. Here we present a model that directly co-generates the video and 3D motion for any supplied rig, along with a camera that registers this motion to the scene. Prior video-motion models either handle only human skeletons, often adding billions of parameters, or triangulate 3D motion from generated 2D pose maps. We instead keep 3D motion in a separate model that supports arbitrary skeletons, and connect it to a pretrained video model through a small set of cross-attention layers. This separation lets the motion model first learn how skeletons move from motion-only data, which is easier to obtain and augment than paired video. Additionally, our model learns to locate each joint in the video frames, which lets us recover the camera and place the 3D motion in the scene. To train across many skeletons, we use a generative video editor to place animated 3D assets in scenes suited to their actions, curating the edits so their known motion remains valid. Together with human and robot video, this yields nearly 200K training pairs. Our evaluations demonstrate that our model animates diverse rigs from text more faithfully than video-first pipelines, and its motion responds to the surrounding scene. It also turns video into rig motion, rig motion into video, and enables keyframe-based control of the 3D motion. Videos and additional results are available on our project page: this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [SZED at ArGuard Shared Tasks: Multi-Task Label-Attention and Cross-Modal Fusion for Arabic Hateful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26972)
*Selma Khelili,Zohra Abdeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SZED团队在ArGuard 2026 Track A（阿拉伯语仇恨表情包检测）的评测方案：A1用MARBERTv2+CLIP ViT-L/14多模态融合并比较拼接/自注意力/集成三种头，A2用带类级query向量的多任务标签注意力架构并加辅助头做级联校准；得分A1 macro-F1=0.7597，A2 macro-F1=0.3142（10个活跃类平均）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统评测活动（ArGuard 2026 Track A）需要完成阿拉伯语仇恨表情包的二分类与细粒度多标签标注两个子任务，并需处理A2标签空间的长尾分布问题。

Method: A1：将MARBERTv2文本嵌入与CLIP ViT-L/14图像嵌入进行融合，对比拼接、self-attention融合头与集成（ensemble）三种方案。A2：多任务标签注意力架构，为每个类别设置可学习query向量实现标签-多模态特征交互，并引入辅助头进行级联校准（cascade calibration）以缓解长尾。

Result: A1 macro-F1 = 0.7597；A2 macro-F1 = 0.3142（10个活跃类平均）。报告多次运行的可复现性、逐类错误模式分析，以及级联校准对长尾标签空间的影响。

Conclusion: 双塔式文本-图像编码器融合在仇恨表情包二分类上可行，但细粒度多标签任务仍很困难（macro-F1仅约0.31），长尾标签分布是主要瓶颈，级联校准可部分缓解；属域内CV/多模态内容审核工作，对核心研究方向无方法迁移价值。

Abstract: We present SZED's submission to ArGuard 2026 Track A: Arabic hateful meme detection. The task has two parts: binary classification (A1) and fine-grained multi-label tagging (A2). For A1, we fuse MARBERTv2 and CLIP ViT-L/14 embeddings, comparing concatenation, self-attention, and ensemble heads. For A2, we use a multi-task label-attention architecture with per-class query vectors and an auxiliary head for cascade calibration. We reach macro-F1 = 0.7597 on A1 and 0.3142 on A2 (averaged over 10 active classes). We report reproducibility across runs, per-class error patterns, and the effect of cascade calibration on the long-tailed A2 label space.

</details>


### [156] [Testing Associative Creativity in Humans and Multimodal LLMs with Difficult-to-Name Visual Stimuli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27894)
*Manar Ali,Lisa Gottschalk,Simeon Junker,Lisa Hudalla,Alon Fishman,Joana Cholin,Stefan Kopp,Hendrik Buschmeier,Sina Zarrieß*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对比人类与多模态大模型在抽象视觉刺激联想创造性上的表现（原创性+有效性），发现人类描述更多样、语义距离更大、熵更高，且随难度升高的适应策略不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念联想是人类沟通核心，但当视觉刺激难以映射到熟悉语义概念时，人类与大模型如何调整尚不清楚；需刻画二者的联想创造性差异。

Method: 构建两个难度可控数据集：SilCC（逐步复杂的抽象形状组合控制命名难度）与PixCC（逐渐增加随机视觉噪声），评估多个SOTA MLLM与人类被试，在原创性与有效性两个创造性维度上打分对比。

Result: 人类生成的联想描述比MLLM更多样：相对参考词表语义距离更大、熵更高、表达更新颖；难度上升时SilCC中有效性下降，PixCC中人类从整体性联想转向低层视觉属性描述。

Conclusion: MLLM在抽象刺激的联想创造性上仍不及人类，且两者在难度增加时呈现不同适应模式；为评估多模态模型创造性提供了受控基准与量化维度。

Abstract: We investigate associative creativity in descriptions of abstract visual stimuli by humans and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). While conceptual association is central to human communication, it remains unclear how humans and models adapt when visual stimuli become increasingly difficult to map onto familiar semantic concepts. We evaluate several state-of-the-art MLLMs and human participants along two dimensions of creativity, originality and effectiveness, using two datasets with controlled manipulations of difficulty. SilCC varies naming difficulty through progressively more complex combinations of abstract shapes, and PixCC varies difficulty through increasing levels of random visual noise. Results show that humans produce more diverse associative descriptions than MLLMs, including greater semantic distance, higher entropy, and more novel expressions relative to reference lexica. As difficulty increases, effectiveness declines in SilCC, and humans increasingly shift from holistic associations to descriptions of low-level visual properties in PixCC.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [GALA: Geometry-Aware Language Model for Controllable Object Arrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28280)
*Chucheng Xiang,Runze Wang,Zhi Deng,Ruchao Bao,Yuanwei Zhang,Yuxuan Xie,Hanliu Wang,Liangzhen Fei,Wenzheng Wu,Cheng Wan,Peifeng Li,Zhongyuan Liu,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: GALA：用显式点云感知的几何感知语言模型自回归预测6D位姿，实现满足支撑/包含等细粒度约束的可控三维物体摆放，并配套多源场景数据集与plan-place-verify智能体流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维场景布局方法多用有向包围盒（OBB）表示物体，对细节几何"盲视"，无法推理支撑、包含等细粒度几何放置约束，导致生成场景不真实。

Method: 提出GALA模型：以场景上下文（显式点云）和放置指令为条件，自回归地逐个预测目标物体6D位姿。两阶段训练：第一阶段用预训练任务对齐几何与文本模态，第二阶段针对放置任务微调。并构建覆盖多房间类型、细粒度家具布局的多源场景数据集。下游构建多阶段plan-place-verify智能体流水线用于文本驱动场景生成。

Result: 在自建基准上显著优于无显式几何推理的基线；结合智能体流水线可完成文本驱动三维场景生成。

Conclusion: 点云级几何感知对细粒度放置约束推理至关重要；两阶段对齐+微调与plan-place-verify智能体框架是有效的可控场景生成范式，数据集将开源。

Abstract: Reasoning about fine-grained placement constraints, such as support and containment, is essential for realistic 3D scene creation. It remains a significant challenge for existing methods that rely on simplified object representations like oriented bounding box (OBB), which are blind to the detailed geometry. To address this limitation, we introduce GALA, a geometry-aware language model with explicit point cloud perception for controllable object placement. Our model performs autoregressively by predicting the 6D pose of one target object at a time conditioned on the scene context and placement instruction. We utilize a two-stage training strategy: the first stage aligns geometric and textual modalities through pretraining tasks, and the second stage fine-tunes the model for placement. To facilitate this task, we also curate a multi-source scene dataset covering diverse room types and fine-grained furniture arrangements, which will be open-sourced for community benefit. Experiments on our benchmark show our model significantly outperforms baselines without explicit geometric reasoning. We further develop a multi-stage plan-place-verify agent pipeline that uses the model for text-driven scene generation. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Codec-Compatible Learned Compression for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26920)
*Feiyang Yu,Qi Yang,Wenjie Huang,Yunfeng Guan,Yiling Xu,Zhu Li*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: CCT-GS：面向视频码器的3DGS压缩框架，学习跨场景泛化的SH-AC系数变换，将高阶球谐图转为紧凑码器友好瓶颈图，并用LoRA做场景自适应恢复细节，MPEG GSC上相对视频基线平均BD-rate降低51.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频式3DGS压缩中，非DC球谐(SH-AC)图通道最多、占据码流主体，但其统计特性与自然图像差异大，且SH-AC系数间非线性依赖未被充分挖掘，导致压缩效率受限。

Method: CCT-GS：先学习一个广义SH-AC变换，捕捉跨场景规律，把SH-AC图映射为紧凑、对视频编解码器友好的瓶颈图；再引入基于LoRA的场景自适应，以轻量边信息补偿场景特定外观细节。整体与标准视频编码器兼容。

Result: 在MPEG GSC序列上稳定优于代表性视频式基线，相对视频锚点平均BD-rate降低51.30%。

Conclusion: 针对SH-AC统计失配与非线性相关性问题，采用可学习变换+轻量场景自适应是提升视频式3DGS压缩效率的有效途径，且保持码器兼容、部署成本低。

Abstract: Video-based 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) compression methods provide a practical route by representing Gaussian attributes as 2D maps and coding them with standard video codecs. Among these maps, high-dimensional non-DC spherical harmonics (SH-AC) maps contain the largest number of channels and account for a dominant portion of the bitstream. However, their statistics differ substantially from those of natural images, and the nonlinear dependencies among SH-AC coefficients remain insufficiently exploited. To address these issues, we propose CCT-GS, a Codec-Compatible learned Transform framework for 3DGS compression. CCT-GS first learns a generalized SH-AC transform that captures cross-scene regularities and converts SH-AC maps into compact codec-friendly bottleneck maps. It further introduces LoRA-based scene adaptation to recover scene-specific appearance details with lightweight side information. Experiments on MPEG GSC sequences demonstrate that CCT-GS consistently outperforms representative video-based baselines, achieving an average BD-rate reduction of 51.30% over the video-based anchor.

</details>


### [159] [Channel-Level Mask Guidance for Slimmable Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27037)
*Chen Chen,Chenggang Wu,Yongbiao Chen,Weidong Qiu,Zhengwei Qi,Haibing Guan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出通道级掩码引导的可精简图像压缩方法：用带稀疏正则的可学习通道掩码实现非连续top-w通道选择，经Mask-to-Slimmable(M2S)转换为标准slidable网络；并用λ与宽度条件调制+量化步长缩放+冻结微调渐进训练实现稳定变码率控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可精简LIC依赖连续通道激活，两方面缺陷：一是连续通道选择在低码率下损害窄模型性能；二是缺乏稳定的变码率控制，难以适应带宽变化。

Method: 可学习通道掩码+稀疏促进正则实现非连续top-w通道选择；训练后通过掩码合并与通道重排序（M2S转换）得到标准可精简网络，推理无需改结构；变码率方面结合λ与宽度条件调制、量化步长缩放，以及freeze-then-fine-tune的渐进训练流程。

Result: 在Kodak上于E2E、Hyperprior、Checkerboard三种骨干网络、不同宽度与码率下均取得更优率失真性能；w=48时相对同宽度Slimmable基线BD-rate节省分别达19.22%、24.11%、22.76%；单个VBR模型替代40个独立模型，存储降低14.8–15.9倍。

Conclusion: 非连续通道选择结合稳定变码率控制可显著提升可精简图像压缩的窄模型与低码率性能，并以单一模型覆盖多样平台约束，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has achieved promising rate-distortion performance, but most methods lack scalability for diverse platforms with varying computational and storage constraints. Existing slimmable LIC methods achieve flexible complexity via consecutive-channel activation but suffer from two limitations: forced consecutive-channel selection degrades narrow-model performance at low bitrates, and the lack of stable variable-rate control limits adaptability to varying bandwidth. We propose a channel-level mask-guided slimmable image compression method. A learnable channel-wise mask with sparsity-promoting regularization enables non-contiguous top-w channel selection, letting narrow models use the most informative channels. After training, mask merging and channel reordering convert the model into a standard slimmable network via Mask-to-Slimmable (M2S) conversion, enabling efficient inference without architectural changes. For variable-rate control, we combine lambda- and width-conditioned modulation with quantization-step scaling and a progressive training pipeline stabilized by a freeze-then-fine-tune strategy. Experiments on Kodak across E2E, Hyperprior, and Checkerboard backbones show superior rate-distortion performance across widths and bitrates, with large gains for narrow models at low bitrates. At w=48, it achieves up to 19.22%, 24.11%, and 22.76% BD-rate savings over the same-width Slimmable baseline on E2E, Hyperprior, and CKBD, respectively. A single VBR model replaces 40 independent models, reducing storage by 14.8x to 15.9x. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [160] [CAPS: Clinically-Verifiable Anatomy-Prioritized Synthesis for Missing MRI Imputation in Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28179)
*Cecilia Diana-Albelda,Arthur Longuefosse,Mario Rubén Paz Campos,Álvaro García-Martín,Jesus Bescos*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出CAPS框架：用解剖先验驱动的多模态生成架构与混合损失(L1+L_AFP+L_DualDice)从缺失模态合成完整4模态MRI，兼顾合成保真度、可验证性与下游分割（BraTS平均Dice 82.38%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床完整4模态采集常不可行；现有方案二选一：鲁棒分割器是不可解释黑箱（跳过生成），或合成模型输出模糊、缺乏诊断可靠性。目标是让缺失模态补全既精确又可供临床核验。

Method: 多模态生成架构（合成器+分割下游），采用混合目标L1 + L_AFP（解剖特征保持）+ L_DualDice（拓扑/边界约束），显式约束深层解剖特征与严格拓扑边界，而非仅像素级优化；在BraTS成人胶质瘤上评测，并做零样本跨域测试（脑膜瘤、儿童、撒哈拉以南非洲队列）与盲法放射科评估。

Result: 强度类指标上合成保真度更优；下游分割平均Dice 82.38%；在完全未见过的三类队列上无域适应仍保持较高结构保真度；盲法放射评估显示感知质量与临床可用性显著提升。

Conclusion: 解剖感知的混合损失合成+可解释分割可恢复可核验的视觉上下文，具备零样本跨域泛化能力，为临床信任自动化流程提供依据；属医学影像缺失模态合成工作，方法思路（结构/拓扑约束的生成目标）可迁移但非核心区。

Abstract: Multi-parametric MRI is the gold standard for neuro-oncology, yet acquiring complete 4-modality protocols may be unfeasible in real-world clinical practice. When handling missing modalities, existing automated systems force a critical compromise: robust segmenters operate as uninterpretable "black boxes" that bypass image generation, while standard synthesis models often produce blurry reconstructions lacking diagnostic reliability. To address this issue, we propose CAPS, a Clinically-Verifiable Anatomy-Prioritized Synthesis framework that reconciles computational precision with clinical transparency. Our multi-modal generative architecture is driven by a hybrid objective function (L_1 + L_AFP + L_DualDice) that explicitly enforces the preservation of deep anatomical features and strict topological boundaries, rather than relying solely on pixel-wise optimization. Evaluated comprehensively on the BraTS Adult Glioma dataset, the framework achieves superior synthesis fidelity across intensity-based metrics, and demonstrates robust downstream segmentation performance (82.38% mean Dice), successfully restoring verifiable visual context. Furthermore, the framework exhibits exceptional zero-shot cross-domain generalization, maintaining high structural fidelity across completely unseen Meningioma, Pediatric, and Sub-Saharan African cohorts without domain-specific adaptation. Finally, a blinded radiological assessment confirms that our anatomy-aware approach significantly enhances both perceived image quality and clinical utility, providing clinicians with the reliable visual evidence required to trust downstream automated workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [DexGraspFlow: Topology-Aware SE(3) Flow Matching for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26962)
*Bin Zhou,Boyu Ma,Xinyu Zhou,Jiahua Dong,Chuhao Zhou,Yizhou Liu,Hao Wan,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexGraspFlow：基于条件 link 级 SE(3) 流匹配的跨具身灵巧抓取生成框架，融合局部几何与运动学拓扑（双流 DiT + 运动链距离注意力偏置 + 平移/旋转速度场联合建模 + IK 恢复关节角），并支持推理期手掌朝向引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨具身灵巧抓取面临手部几何、关节参数化与运动学拓扑差异的挑战；仅用 link 间空间关系不足以刻画手部运动学拓扑，因此需要显式融合几何关系与运动学结构来驱动 link 级 SE(3) 位姿生成。

Method: 1) 编码局部物体几何、link 几何与当前位姿；2) 双流 DiT 分别建模 link–link 协调与 link–object 交互，并由运动学树距离导出注意力偏置，提供显式结构先验；3) 在 link 级 SE(3) 上做条件流匹配，联合学习平移与旋转速度场并积分得到 link 位姿；4) 逆运动学恢复关节配置；5) 推理期手掌朝向引导实现免重训的可控生成。

Result: 3 种机械手、30 组物体–手组合上平均仿真成功率 95.33%，含 IK 的平均生成时延较 SOTA 基线降低约 49%；零样本适配 7 种去指变体并支持手掌朝向控制；在部署手上用示范训练后，100 次真实机器人抓取试验成功率 91%。

Conclusion: 显式引入运动学拓扑先验可显著提升跨具身灵巧抓取的泛化性与效率，且 link 级 SE(3) 生成 + IK 的流程可直接迁移到真实机器人执行。

Abstract: Differences in hand geometry, joint parameterization, and kinematic topology pose challenges for cross-embodiment dexterous grasp generation. However, spatial relations among links alone are insufficient to fully capture the hand's kinematic topology. We propose DexGraspFlow, a conditional link-level $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ flow-matching framework integrating geometric relations and kinematic topology. The framework encodes local object geometry, link geometry, and current poses. A dual-stream DiT models link--link coordination and link--object interaction, while attention biases derived from kinematic-tree distances provide explicit structural information for modeling evolving geometric interactions. The model jointly learns translational and rotational velocity fields, integrates them to generate link poses, and recovers joint configurations through inverse kinematics. Additionally, inference-time palm-orientation guidance enables controllable grasp generation without retraining. Across three robotic hands and 30 object--hand pairs, DexGraspFlow achieves a 95.33\% average simulation success rate, with an average generation latency, including inverse kinematics, approximately 49\% lower than that of a state-of-the-art baseline. Further experiments demonstrate zero-shot adaptability to seven finger-removal variants and palm-orientation control. A model trained on demonstrations for the deployed hand achieves a 91\% success rate over 100 real-robot grasping trials, supporting the framework's applicability to physical grasp execution.

</details>


### [162] [Replan for Error Recovery: Training Embodied VLMs through Verifiable Interaction with Action-Grouped Turn-GSPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27003)
*Zhuoran Wang,Keqing Fan,Yang Li,Jinbo Su,Ke Wang,Sijia Luo,Yuanzheng Tan,Junken Peng,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ERR-Env 可验证交互环境与 ERR-Bench 基准（覆盖正常/中间/错误状态），并用在线强化学习 AGT-GSPO（turn 级奖励 + action-grouped 优势估计）训练 2B 模型 ERR-VLM，提升执行可靠性与错误恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身 VLM 执行复杂操作任务时，执行错误会把环境置于需要重规划恢复的状态；现有恢复方法要么依赖推理期的固定策略推理机制，要么从失败样本与参考纠正中学习，而非从经过验证的动作结果中学习，缺乏可验证的交互式恢复学习途径。

Method: 1) 构建 ERR-Env，提供任务进度与执行错误反馈；2) 构建 ERR-Bench，分层评估 nominal / intermediate / error 状态下的恢复能力与执行可靠性；3) 提出 AGT-GSPO：将 turn 级奖励（任务进度 + 错误状态）与 action-grouped 优势估计结合，用作在线 RL 方法；4) 基于该方法训练紧凑的 2B 具身 VLM ERR-VLM，并进行消融与零样本迁移验证。

Result: ERR-Bench 揭示多种 VLM 在任务进度与执行可靠性之间存在错配；AGT-GSPO 使 ERR-VLM 的无错误完成 Strict Success Rate 提升 10.25 个百分点，Recovery Rate 提升 22.95 个百分点；消融实验与零样本迁移进一步验证方法有效性。

Conclusion: 可验证的交互式 RL（结合 turn 级进度/错误奖励与 action-grouped 优势估计）能有效提升具身 VLM 的执行可靠性与错误恢复能力，说明"从可验证结果学习恢复"优于依赖固定推理机制或参考纠正的做法。

Abstract: Embodied VLMs can execute increasingly complex manipulation tasks, yet execution errors can leave the environment in states that require replanning for recovery. Existing recovery methods either rely on inference-time mechanisms with fixed policies or learn from failure examples and reference corrections rather than verified action outcomes. To enable recovery learning through verifiable interaction, we introduce the Embodied Replanning for Recovery Environment (ERR-Env), which provides feedback on task progress and execution errors. We further construct ERR-Bench, a benchmark with held-out tasks spanning nominal, intermediate, and error states for evaluating recovery capability and execution reliability. We then propose Action-Grouped Turn-GSPO (AGT-GSPO), an online reinforcement learning method combining turn-level rewards for task progress and error states with action-grouped advantage estimation, and use it to train ERR-VLM, a compact 2B embodied VLM. Evaluation on ERR-Bench reveals a mismatch between task progress and execution reliability across diverse VLMs. AGT-GSPO improves ERR-VLM by 10.25 percentage points in Strict Success Rate for error-free completion and 22.95 percentage points in Recovery Rate, while ablations and zero-shot transfer further validate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [163] [Embodied Playground: A Long-Horizon Agentic Robotics Benchmark for Physical, Strategic, and Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27039)
*Feng Chen,Pei Zhou,Lixuan He,Yunchao Yao,Li Sun,Shenghua Gao,Mingyu Ding,Kaifeng Zhang,Kaichen Zhou,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Embodied Playground 基准，用 410 个任务环境（含 EP-100/EP-Easy·Hard/EP-20 子集）评估基础模型智能体在长时程任务中协调物理、策略与空间推理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型被寄予机器人智能体的厚望，但长时程任务要求其在相互依赖的阶段间保持连贯推理与可靠物理执行；现有评测缺乏对"进度跟踪—中间约束—基于执行反馈改计划"这一闭环能力的统一检验平台。

Method: 构建统一基准：410 个环境，覆盖基础操作、序列拼图、多阶段家务活动，并在规划深度、几何约束、物理执行难度上做变化；精选 EP-100（5 个套件、16 类任务）、EP-Easy/EP-Hard 难度划分与 EP-20 轻量子集；定义共享协议，规范观测与工具访问、资源预算、重复试验，以及目标完成、任务规则合规、物理有效性三类独立检查。

Result: 摘要未给出模型实验数值结果，主要贡献为可复现的基准设计、分层子集与多维评测协议，为后续智能体能力对比提供可比口径。

Conclusion: Embodied Playground 为研究基础模型智能体能否在扩展任务时程上维持有效推理与行动提供统一框架；属基准/评测类工作，方法细节与实证结论有限。

Abstract: Foundation models offer a promising basis for robotic agents, but long-horizon tasks require them to sustain coherent reasoning and reliable physical execution across interdependent stages. We introduce Embodied Playground, a benchmark for evaluating how foundation-model agents coordinate physical, strategic, and spatial reasoning throughout such tasks. Agents must track task progress, satisfy intermediate constraints, and revise their plans using execution feedback as earlier actions shape subsequent decisions. The playground comprises 410 task environments spanning basic manipulation, sequential puzzles, and multi-stage household activities, with variations in planning depth, geometric constraints, and physical execution demands. We curate EP-100, a representative selection of 100 tasks across five suites and 16 task types, together with the EP-Easy and EP-Hard difficulty splits and the compact EP-20 selection. A shared protocol specifies observation and tool access, resource budgets, repeated trials, and independent checks of goal completion, task-rule compliance, and physical validity. Embodied Playground provides a unified framework for studying whether foundation-model agents can sustain effective reasoning and action over extended task horizons. this https URL

</details>


### [164] [ACE-Brain-0.6: Scaling Spatial Grounding for Physical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27040)
*Yinhe Wang,Tao Tao,Zexin He,Shuang Luo,Wei Mao,Yao Mu,Feng Pan,Hanming Deng,Lewei Lu,Zhi Hou,Xingang Pan,Dahua Lin,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 统一数据管线将机器人操作录像与多源视觉数据自动标注为可验证的视觉定位（grounding）监督，微调 Qwen3.5-9B 得到 ACE-Brain-0.6，在 7 个公开 grounding 基准中 4 个超过 GPT-6 Astra（平均 75.41 vs 73.68），并展示数据规模扩展带来的稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态模型（如 Astra、Opus 5.5）在具身操作上的泛化能力源于强空间感知，而空间感知是高层推理与物理场景之间的桥梁；因此作者希望通过对视觉 grounding 的规模化来强化具身操作的感知基础。

Method: 构建统一数据管线：融合机器人操作录制数据与多源互补视觉数据，对机器人录像自动标注后转换为可验证的 grounding 监督信号（约 1.5M 样本）；在固定 Qwen3.5-9B 微调配置下训练 ACE-Brain-0.6，并做数据规模逐步扩张的消融观察。

Result: ACE-Brain-0.6 在 7 个公开 grounding 基准中的 4 个超过 GPT-6 Astra，总平均 75.41 对 73.68；随着数据版本扩充覆盖更多 grounding 场景，整体 grounding 性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 从多源数据规模化可验证的 grounding 监督，可显著增强开放 9B 模型的空间感知能力，为通用具身操作提供更坚实的感知基础。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal models, including GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5, demonstrate broad capabilities in embodied manipulation. Our analysis indicates that this generality is accompanied by strong spatial perception, as Astra can determine where to interact with task-relevant entities amid visual distractors. Because spatial perception connects high-level reasoning to the physical scene, we investigate scaling visual grounding to strengthen the perceptual foundation of general embodied operation. We develop a unified data pipeline that combines robot manipulation recordings with complementary visual data from diverse sources. After automatically annotating the robot recordings, the pipeline converts the collected data into verifiable grounding supervision. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on approximately 1.5 million samples produced by this pipeline yields ACE-Brain-0.6. The model outperforms GPT-6 Astra on four of seven public grounding benchmarks, with an overall average score of 75.41 compared with Astra's 73.68. Under a fixed Qwen3.5-9B fine-tuning setup, aggregate grounding performance improves steadily as successive data releases expand the training collection to cover a broader range of grounding scenarios. These results show that scaling verifiable grounding supervision from diverse sources can strengthen the spatial perception of an open 9B model for general embodied operation.

</details>


### [165] [Towards Interaction State Recognition in Sensorized Gecko-Inspired Adhesive Interfaces for Orbital Servicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27141)
*Eduart Abdullahu,Francesco Grella,Carol Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于嵌入式电容传感的实时“交互感知”框架，使仿壁虎干黏附界面在整个黏附生命周期中估计接触状态，用增强数据训练轻量模型并与规则状态机融合实现在线部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务（OOS）中对非合作目标的可靠接触建立是核心难题；仿壁虎干黏附虽轻量、被动、无残留，但现有系统开环运行，交互中无法自估接触/黏附状态。

Method: 用嵌入式电容传感采集数据并经数据增强，定义五个离散交互状态（idle、proximity、contact、engaged、disengaged）；对比六种适合嵌入式部署的轻量机器学习模型；在线阶段将随机森林与基于物理一致性的规则状态机级联。

Result: Random Forest 离线分类准确率最高达 95.65%；在线部署时，RF+规则状态机将准确率由 78.02% 提升至 93.14%，macro F1 为 0.9343，推理周期 53 ms。

Conclusion: 引入物理一致的状态约束可显著提升实时推理鲁棒性，是实现未来自主在轨服务“交互感知式捕获”的一步；当前验证以离线/在线分类实验为主，尚缺实际捕获任务的闭环验证。

Abstract: Reliable contact establishment remains a critical challenge for autonomous on-orbit servicing (OOS), particularly when interacting with non-cooperative targets. Gecko-inspired dry adhesives provide a lightweight, passive, and residue-free solution for robotic capture in space, yet current systems operate in open loop, without the ability to estimate their own contact or adhesion state during interaction. This paper presents a real-time interaction-awareness framework that enables sensorized gecko-inspired adhesive interfaces to estimate their contact state throughout the adhesion lifecycle using embedded capacitive sensing. Five discrete interaction states (idle, proximity, contact, engaged, and disengaged) are classified using experimentally collected and augmented sensor data, enabling a comparative evaluation of six lightweight machine learning models suitable for embedded deployment. Among the evaluated models, Random Forest achieved the highest offline classification accuracy of 95.65%. For online deployment, combining Random Forest with a rule-based state machine improved accuracy from 78.02% to 93.14%, achieving a macro F1-score of 0.9343 while maintaining a 53 ms inference cycle. These results demonstrate that incorporating physics-consistent state constraints significantly improves robustness during realtime inference, representing a step toward interaction-aware capture in future autonomous orbital servicing missions.

</details>


### [166] [Visual Task Modeling and Automated Planning for Humanoid Robots in Education of Individuals with Autism Spectrum Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27162)
*Lada Kudláčková*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种由视觉大语言模型驱动的 Shoebox Task 求解器：将任务照片转换为 PDDL 表示并生成动作计划，再由 Nao 机器人通过语音、手势与动作示范执行任务并指导学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特殊教育（尤其是 ASD 儿童）中机器人辅助教学需要机器人自主理解任务并规划，而现有系统依赖人工编程且难以泛化；Shoebox Tasks 的目标可由其视觉结构直接推断，适合作为视觉-符号推理的切入点。

Method: 基于视觉 LLM 将任务照片转为 PDDL 领域/问题描述，调用规划器生成解题动作序列；实现 Nao 机器人接口，自主执行任务并以动作示范、语音指令或手势提示下一步或报告失败；求解器仅以拍摄图像为输入，教师无需编程。

Result: 系统可在多种 Shoebox Tasks 上自主执行并给出提示/失败反馈；经适配的机器人接口可迁移到其他教育机器人。

Conclusion: 视觉 LLM + PDDL 规划的流水线为特殊教育机器人提供了无需编程的任务理解与执行方案，有望增强 ASD 学生的学习机会。

Abstract: In robot-assisted education, a robot must understand how to complete a task in order to effectively guide a student through it. We present a novel method that enables a semi-autonomous, camera-equipped robot to recognise the task goal and plan actions to achieve it. Our work focuses on Shoebox Tasks, an activity commonly used in the education of individuals with Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD). These tasks are designed so that the goal can be inferred directly from their visual structure. We have developed a solver that uses a vision-based Large Language Model to convert photos of a Shoebox Task into a Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) representation and generate action plans for solving it. We have also implemented a robotic interface that enables a Nao robot to autonomously execute various Shoebox Tasks and assist a student by suggesting next steps or signalling detected failures through action demonstrations, voice commands, or gestures. With an adapted robotic interface, the solver can be integrated into a variety of educational robots. No programming expertise is required from teachers, and the solver operates exclusively on captured images as input. Research in special educational needs indicates that students with ASD can benefit substantially from robot-assisted teaching, and our system aims to strengthen these learning opportunities while also fostering interest in robotics among individuals with ASD.

</details>


### [167] [Learning to Be Different: Component Specialization for Compositional Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27194)
*Alec Laprevotte,Haonan Chen,Xiaoshen Han,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可即插即用的“分数去相关”辅助损失，基于投影式组合理论惩罚各因子的噪声预测之间的重叠，从而促使扩散策略中的得分因子专门化、提升多任务分解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性组合分数的扩散策略被视为多任务机器人学习的有效框架（因子化、组合泛化、可解释功能分解），但现有训练目标并不鼓励因子专门化，导致去噪器退化为同质副本的均值预测，破坏其架构前提。

Method: 受投影式组合理论启发，设计可即插即用的分数去相关辅助损失：直接在分量噪声预测之间施加重叠/相关惩罚，不需要改动架构，也几乎不增加计算开销。

Result: 在多个基准上，去相关损失稳定降低分量间可度量的“重复计数”，改善任务分解并产生行为上有意义的专门化，同时不损害多任务性能。

Conclusion: 以极低成本的辅助正则即可修复组合式扩散策略的因子专门化问题，为其模块化前提提供训练层面的支撑；但摘要仅展示基准结果，缺少实机部署与开源证据。

Abstract: Diffusion policies with linearly composed scores have emerged as a promising approach to multitask robotic learning by combining separate factors to enable modular, task-wise adaptation, compositional generalization, and interpretable functional decomposition. However, current training objectives do not encourage factor specialization, often resulting in uninterpretable denoisers whose composed prediction is an average over identical copies, undermining their architectural premise. To address this, we propose a drop-in score-decorrelation auxiliary loss, motivated by projective composition theory. Our approach encourages specialization by penalizing overlap directly between component noise predictions, with little architectural change or computational overhead. We demonstrate across multiple benchmarks that our decorrelation loss consistently reduces measurable double-counting between components, improves task decomposition, and produces behaviorally meaningful specialization without compromising multitask performance. Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [168] [Which Depth Estimator for Jetson AGX Orin? A 2026 Benchmark of Monocular and Stereo Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27199)
*Leaf Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在 Jetson AGX Orin 上系统评测 9 个单目/双目深度估计模型的精度、速度、能耗与许可，结论为双目远优于单目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jetson AGX Orin 是机器人与车载平台常用计算单元，但现有深度估计器在其上的实测精度、速度与能耗数据稀缺，缺乏部署选型依据。

Method: 将 Depth Anything V2/V3、Depth Pro、MoGe-2、Metric3D v2、UniDepth V2（单目）与 libSGM、NVIDIA ESS、Fast-FoundationStereo（双目）统一部署为 TensorRT FP16 引擎或原生 CUDA，最多三种输入尺寸（至 5.7 MP），在 Middlebury 2014 非遮挡像素上评测 AbsRel、GPU 时间、能耗与许可证，并分析边缘深度不连续处的点散布率。

Result: 30 fps 下 ESS 达 0.70% AbsRel / 8.6 ms，libSGM 0.73% / 25 ms，Fast-FoundationStereo 最佳 0.33%；单目最好约 12%（Depth Anything 3 Metric-L，33 ms；Depth Pro 803 ms），仅在 oracle 逐图尺度下为 3.8–4.0%。双目误差随分辨率提升而下降，单目不提升且度量尺度在训练分辨率外漂移。深度不连续处单目边缘点散布 17–46%，学习式双目 2–9%，全分辨率 libSGM 1%。

Conclusion: 对嵌入式机器人部署，立体匹配在精度、实时性与边缘质量上均显著优于单目基础模型；作者给出按帧率预算的选型建议并开源代码、逐场景结果与引擎构建脚本。

Abstract: The Jetson AGX Orin is a common computer for robots and vehicles, yet measured accuracy, speed and energy of current depth estimators on it are hard to find. We benchmark nine model families as of October 2026: the monocular Depth Anything V2 and 3, Depth Pro, MoGe-2, Metric3D v2 and UniDepth V2, and the stereo methods libSGM, NVIDIA ESS and Fast-FoundationStereo. Every method runs on the Orin as a TensorRT FP16 engine or native CUDA code at up to three input sizes, up to the full 5.7 MP, and is scored for metric accuracy against Middlebury 2014 ground truth (non-occluded pixels of the 10 scenes whose disparity stays below 256 px), GPU time, energy and licence. Stereo is far more accurate. At 30 fps, NVIDIA's ESS reaches 0.70% AbsRel in 8.6 ms and libSGM 0.73% in 25 ms; with more compute, Fast-FoundationStereo reaches 0.33%. The best monocular models reach 12% with the true focal length (Depth Anything 3 Metric-L in 33 ms, Depth Pro in 803 ms), and 3.8-4.0% only with an oracle per-image scale (MoGe-2-L, UniDepth V2-B, Metric3D v2-L). Stereo error falls as the input resolution grows; no monocular model improves, and the metric scale of most drifts away from the truth outside their training resolution. At depth discontinuities, which AbsRel barely registers, the monocular models scatter 17-46% of the edge points between foreground and background, learned stereo 2-9% and libSGM at full resolution 1%. We give recommendations per frame-rate budget and release all code, per-scene results and engine build scripts.

</details>


### [169] [EmbodyHOI: Embodiment-Aware Generation of Hand-Object Interactions for Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27242)
*Chaitanya Chawla,Kartik Sharma,Ananya Bal,Laszlo A. Jeni,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodyHOI在冻结的flow-matching手-物交互轨迹采样循环内嵌入可微IK求解器，用机器人可达性梯度引导生成，实现无需重训的人形跨本体迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的HOI生成器在MANO人体空间产出多样演示，但本体差异只能靠事后重定向弥合，导致机器人在执行时丢失接触并损害策略学习。作者主张可达性约束应在生成时施加而非事后修正。

Method: 在冻结的flow-matching HOI采样器中融入特定人形本体的可微逆运动学求解器：将本体感知IK的梯度回传至HOI采样过程，并联合优化人形相对物体的位置；引导仅在推理阶段生效，因此单个人手轨迹生成器可跨本体复用。

Result: 在ARCTIC轨迹上，以Unitree G1与H1-2为对象，重定向手腕误差由8.9 cm降至0.6 cm，文本到HOI生成质量保持不变；50个随机G1任务成功率由68%升至79%，H1-2四项任务套件由62%升至76%。事后手腕精修与算力匹配的best-of-N选择虽IK能量更低，但训练出的策略更差。

Conclusion: 将本体可达性作为生成时的引导信号优于事后重定向或采样择优，可在不重训的前提下把人体HOI数据有效迁移到不同人形平台并提升下游策略表现。

Abstract: Humanoid dexterous manipulation needs reference motions that are both semantically rich and kinematically feasible for the target robot. Text-conditioned hand-object interaction (HOI) generators supply diverse, language-specified demonstrations, but they synthesize motion in human (MANO) space. The embodiment gap is therefore deferred to post-hoc retargeting, where trajectories deployed on the robot can fail to maintain contact with objects and also degrade policy learning. Our key idea is that robot reachability should constrain the interaction trajectories while they are being generated. We present EmbodyHOI, which runs a differentiable inverse-kinematics (IK) solver for a specific humanoid embodiment inside the sampling loop of a frozen flow-matching HOI trajectory generator. We feed back gradients from the embodiment-aware IK solver into the HOI sampler and jointly optimize the position of the humanoid with respect to the object. Because guidance acts only at inference, one HOI trajectory generator trained on human hand poses transfers across humanoid embodiments without retraining. On ARCTIC trajectories with two contrasting humanoids (Unitree G1 and H1-2), our proposed guidance-based method cuts the retargeted wrist error from 8.9 cm to 0.6 cm while leaving text-to-HOI generation quality intact, and raises task success from 68% to 79% on 50 random G1 tasks and from 62% to 76% on a four-task H1-2 suite. Critically, cheaper alternatives for bridging the embodiment gap fail to beat EmbodyHOI. Post-hoc wrist refinement and compute-matched best-of-N selection both reach lower IK energy than our guidance yet train worse policies. For simulation results, see our website: this https URL

</details>


### [170] [XoNav: An Exoskeleton as a Personalized Haptic Interface for Assisted Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27271)
*Debadrata Sarkar,Soumen Sen,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XoNav是一种四执行器髋部外骨骼，用矢状面执行器呈现"前进"与两级"制动"提示、额状面执行器呈现两级"转向"提示，以双侧LSTM步态分类器（双大腿IMU）实现步态同步触觉引导，并通过人体在环贝叶斯优化个性化触觉强度，最后在盲导航与自主导航中验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴导航辅助多以音频或振动信号编码方向，用户需自行将其"翻译"为运动指令，实时性与无歧义性不足；作者希望直接在髋关节处施加力学提示，使引导与步态相位协调，从而降低认知负担并提升行进效率。

Method: 硬件为四执行器髋部外骨骼（矢状面2个用于Advance与两级Braking，额状面2个用于两级Steering）。步态识别用双大腿IMU输入的双侧LSTM分类器判别步态状态，实现提示的步态同步投放并在站立期抑制。触觉参数（Braking最小阻力、Steering最大舒适度）通过带约束的人体在环贝叶斯优化进行个性化，同时约束可辨识性与安全性。评测包括盲fold Wizard-of-Oz三条路径导航，以及基于佩戴相机+RTAB-Map+Nav2的自主导航与未知障碍绕行可行性研究。

Result: 步态分类器可泛化到未见用户，召回率95.2–97.9%，站立→摆动与摆动→站立转换的平均检测延迟分别为38.6 ms和63.1 ms。所有参与者均能可靠区分个性化触觉提示的等级，最强Braking提示仍低于安全上限的53%。盲导航中，98.8%的行走距离保持在0.75 m宽通道内，平均行进速度每轮提升4.9%。自主导航与未知障碍绕行仅完成可行性验证。

Conclusion: 将方向与制动意图直接映射为髋关节力学提示并结合步态相位同步，是一种低认知负荷、可被可靠识别的可穿戴导航范式；但其评测以盲fold人类操作为主，自主导航仅为初步可行性，闭环自主性能与更大规模用户验证仍待补充。

Abstract: Walking without vision requires timely, unambiguous guidance, yet many wearable aids encode directions as audio or vibrotactile signals that users must translate into movement. We present XoNav, a four-actuator hip exoskeleton that renders guidance cues directly at the hip joints. It renders an Advance cue and two-level Braking cues through sagittal-plane actuators and two-level Steering cues through frontal-plane actuators. A bilateral LSTM-based gait-state classifier uses two thigh-mounted IMUs to identify gait states, enabling haptic cues to be delivered in coordination with the wearer's gait while suppressing them during standing. The classifier generalized to unseen users with recalls of 95.2-97.9%, with mean detection delays of 38.6 and 63.1 ms for stance-to-swing and swing-to-stance transitions, respectively. Constrained human-in-the-loop Bayesian optimization personalized Braking for minimum resistance and Steering for maximum comfort while ensuring detectability and safety. All participants reliably distinguished between grades of their personalized cues and the firmest Braking cue remained below 53% of the safety limit. In blindfolded Wizard-of-Oz navigation trials along three paths, the participants kept 98.8% of the walked distance within a 0.75 m wide passage, with a 4.9% increase in mean progression speed per trial. A body-worn camera, RTAB-Map and Nav2 further enabled a feasibility study of autonomous navigation and detours around previously unmapped obstacles.

</details>


### [171] [On Maximum Clique and Maximum Consensus Formulations in Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27275)
*Abolfazl Babanazari,Kaveh Fathian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文从理论上研究最大团（maximum clique）与最大一致性（maximum consensus）两种鲁棒点云配准形式的关系：证明在任意固定正容差下，最大团的对齐误差可以任意大、其规模也可任意大于最大一致性集，并给出约束该误差的几何条件；在旋转固定时对平移子问题给出紧致误差界与精确刻画最大一致性的检验；最后研究 group-k 一致性，确定与点云尺度、几何无关的误差界所需最小群规模为 d+1（旋转+平移）与 d+2（含反射），d≥2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大一致性（追求最多内点的单一变换）与最大团（基于成对距离检验选点）在机器人鲁棒配准中被广泛使用，且常以最大团替代或近似最大一致性，但两者究竟有多接近缺乏理论理解。已有工作多依赖经验证据，缺少对二者一致性程度、误差上界以及如何通过高阶一致性（group-k）保证尺度/几何无关误差界的系统分析。

Method: 理论分析：构造性反例证明最大团对齐误差可任意大、且团规模可任意大于最大一致性集；推导误差有界的几何条件；在旋转固定情形下，对平移子问题推导紧致误差界与可精确刻画最大一致性的检验方法；将分析扩展到 group-k 一致性（检验 k 个匹配的成组相容性），求解在 d≥2 维下使对齐误差界独立于点云尺度与几何的最小群规模；并借助 Lean 定理证明器进行机器验证、提供交互示例。

Result: 最大团在任意固定正容差下可能出现任意大的对齐误差，且规模可任意大于最大一致性集，说明二者在最坏情况下并不等价；给出使误差有界的几何条件；平移子问题在固定旋转下获得紧致误差界与精确检验；group-k 一致性的最小充分群规模为 d+1（仅旋转与平移）与 d+2（允许反射），对所有 d≥2 成立，且该误差界不依赖点云尺度与几何。

Conclusion: 最大团并不天然逼近最大一致性，将其作为替代需谨慎；几何条件与高阶一致性（d+1/d+2 群规模）可提供有保证的对齐误差上界。论文提出了一套可机器验证的理论框架，明确了两种公式的适用边界，后续需在真实点云与机器人配准流程中检验这些界在实际噪声与离群点分布下的紧致性与计算可行性。

Abstract: Maximum clique and maximum consensus are widely used formulations for robust point cloud registration in robotics. Maximum consensus seeks one transformation that aligns as many matches as possible, while maximum clique selects matches through pairwise distance tests. Maximum clique is often used as a substitute for or an approximation to maximum consensus, with empirical evidence suggesting a close relationship. However, how closely the two formulations agree is not fully understood. We study this relationship theoretically. We show that a maximum clique can have arbitrarily large alignment error and be arbitrarily larger than the maximum consensus set at any fixed positive tolerance, and derive geometric conditions that bound this error.
