Motivation: Classical HJR and neural HJR variants (Lipschitz Continuous Reachability Learning, DeepReach) model disturbances adversarially with a known bounded disturbance set, yielding safe but overly conservative reach-avoid sets when disturbances are actually stochastic; the disturbance set also must be specified a priori.

Method: Self-supervised learning of a Probabilistic reach-avoid Value Function (PVF) for black-box systems; the reach-avoid set is thresholded by reach-avoid probability under stochastic, potentially unbounded disturbances (no explicit disturbance-set specification, no adversarial worst-case optimization).

Result: Evaluated against HJR-based baselines on systems of increasing complexity, up to a Hopper locomotion task; the probabilistic sets are less conservative and more accurate than adversarial HJR sets under non-adversarial disturbances.

Conclusion: Probabilistic reach-avoid modeling is preferable to adversarial HJR when disturbances are stochastic and unbounded; black-box self-supervised learning suffices for runtime feasibility monitoring and safety prediction.

Abstract: Reach-avoid sets, defining the states from which a task can be completed while avoiding unsafe regions, are fundamental tools for deploying robots in environments subject to disturbances and uncertainty, as well as for runtime task-feasibility monitoring, allowing prediction of whether a state is safe and will lead to successful task completion. Classical Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability (HJR) and its recent neural extensions (e.g., Lipschitz Continuous Reachability Learning, DeepReach) treat disturbances adversarially, computing the reach-avoid set under worst-case disturbances from a bounded set. This guarantees safety but can yield overly conservative sets when disturbances are stochastic rather than adversarial, and additionally requires the disturbance set to be known or specified in advance. We propose Probabilistic reach-avoid Value Function (PVF), a self-supervised method, applicable to black-box systems, for computing reach-avoid sets as the states with a certain reach-avoid probability under stochastic, potentially unbounded disturbances. We evaluate our approach against HJR-based baselines on systems of increasing complexity, up to a Hopper locomotion task, and show that our probabilistic sets are less conservative and more accurate than adversarial HJR sets when disturbances are non-adversarial.

</details>


### [208] [FIT-MAC: A Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Framework for Robust Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28109)
*Jiatong Li,Jisheng Dang,Qiangqiang Ma,Bimei Wang,Jingwen Zhao,Wenjun Ma,Juncheng Li,Hong Peng,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIT-MAC: 一种推理时的失效感知闭环多智能体控制框架，通过Planner/Grounder/Verifier/Correction四个智能体为预训练VLA策略增加运行时干预层，区分"目标绑定错误"与"局部执行失败"并路由到相应恢复动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略在标准benchmark上表现良好，但对物体位置扰动和指令改写缺乏鲁棒性，且没有显式的运行时机制来判别失效类型并选择恢复策略，导致扰动下整任务失败。

Method: 在不改动VLA权重的前提下增加推理时控制层：Planner Agent将语言指令解析为结构化任务规范（操作目标+空间约束）；Grounder Agent把指令级指称解析为视觉目标；Verifier Agent跟踪分阶段执行进度并判别目标绑定错误 vs 局部执行失败，判断是否触发干预；Correction Agent按失效类型路由到目标重绑定或有界抓取/放置恢复；无需干预时由原VLA生成动作。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO上跨多个VLA骨干验证，成功率一致提升，在不同扰动下达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式的失效判别与分类型恢复是提升VLA推理时鲁棒性的有效手段，模块化多智能体封装可直接叠加于现有VLA策略上。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance on standard robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, their reliability can degrade substantially when task conditions deviate from the nominal setting. Perturbations in object positions and task instructions may lead to complete task failure. Existing VLA policies generally lack an explicit runtime mechanism for distinguishing these failure modes and selecting an appropriate recovery strategy. To systematically mitigate this issue, we propose FIT-MAC, a failure-aware, closed-loop multi-agent control framework for improving the robustness of VLA policies at inference time. FIT-MAC augments a VLA policy with an inference-time control layer. First, the Planner Agent parses the language instruction into a structured task specification that captures the manipulation goal and spatial constraints, and the Grounder Agent resolves each instruction-level reference to a visual target. Next, the Verifier Agent tracks phase-specific execution progress, distinguishes target-binding errors from local execution failures, and determines whether intervention is warranted. The Correction Agent then routes permitted interventions to target rebinding or bounded grasping or placement recovery, depending on the failure type. When no intervention is required, the pretrained VLA remains the default action generator. Extensive experiments on LIBERO-PRO demonstrate that FIT-MAC improves robustness across multiple VLA backbones, consistently increasing success rates and achieving state-of-the-art performance under diverse perturbations.

</details>


### [209] [De-Diffusing WAMs: Action-Sufficient Direct Prediction for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28122)
*Shaoyi Zheng,Zhongtao Jiang,Yue Peng,Wenbo Lu,Shunchi Zhang,Xi Wang,Shengji Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DirectWAM将预训练的视频专家与动作专家通过"动作充分蒸馏"转为单步预测器，保留未来→动作级联结构，用下游控制误差而非表示匹配来监督未来预测，实现单步推理且成功率超越Flash-WAM，延迟降低28倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联world-action模型（WAM）用预测的视觉未来条件化机器人动作，但继承了扩散预训练专家的昂贵迭代采样推理。作者追问：单步预测的未来究竟需要保留什么信息，才能支持准确的单步动作预测。

Method: 先做诊断实验：(1) 重采样视频噪声对下游动作影响很小；(2) 简单地把两条流都截断为单步会显著降低任务成功率；(3) 仅用视频空间监督只能部分弥补gap；(4) 局部分析显示视觉预测误差对动作精度的影响极不均衡。据此提出DirectWAM：无需随机采样，把视频专家和动作专家蒸馏为单步预测器，同时保留显式的未来→动作级联。先学单步动作模型，再训练未来预测器——用其自身预测的未来解码动作，将示范动作误差经专家反传，并可选加未来潜变量重构损失。

Result: 在LIBERO四个套件上平均成功率97.1%，RoboTwin 2.0上85.4%，在相同"每流一次评估"预算下优于Flash-WAM；优势延伸到三个自设真实世界任务；相比完整迭代教师推理延迟降低28.0倍。

Conclusion: 单步未来预测的价值应以其对下游控制的贡献来衡量，而非表示相似度；以动作充分性为目标蒸馏级联WAM，可在保持显式未来→动作结构的同时获得单步效率与更高成功率。

Abstract: Cascade world-action models (WAMs) use predicted visual futures to condition robot actions, but inherit costly iterative inference from diffusion-pretrained experts. We ask what a single-step predicted future must preserve to support accurate single-step action prediction. In the evaluated WAM, resampling video noise has little effect on downstream actions, yet naively truncating both streams to one step substantially reduces task success. Video-space supervision only partially closes this gap, and a local sensitivity analysis shows that vision prediction errors have highly unequal consequences for action accuracy. These findings motivate DirectWAM, which converts pretrained video and action experts into single-step predictors without stochastic sampling through action-sufficient distillation, while retaining the explicit future-to-action cascade. After learning a one-step action model, we train the future predictor by decoding actions from its own predicted futures and backpropagating demonstration action error through the expert, with an optional future-latent reconstruction loss. The resulting objective evaluates the student-generated future through its downstream control consequences rather than relying on representation matching alone. DirectWAM achieves 97.1% average success across four LIBERO suites and 85.4% on RoboTwin 2.0, outperforming Flash-WAM at the same one-evaluation-per-stream budget. This advantage has also extended to three real-world tasks that we designed. Compared with the full iterative teacher, it reduces inference latency by $28.0\times$.

</details>


### [210] [Design and implementation of thermal control strategy for micro-size lunar exploration rover hakuto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28127)
*Toshiki Tanaka,Takuto Oikawa,Keeni Shruti,Teruhito Iida,Kazuya Yoshida,John Walker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hakuto月球漫游车FM的热界面设计与热真空验证：用六面辐射解析模型确定朝向依赖的表面温度，据此选择航电安装位置与太阳板角度，兼顾功率与<4kg质量约束，再用数值仿真细化并做热平衡试验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球表面辐照条件严酷且朝向变化剧烈，漫游车需在极严格的质量（<4 kg）约束下同时满足热控与发电需求，因此需要系统化的热接口设计与验证流程。

Method: 首先构建解析热模型，将车体划分为六个独立辐射面，基于车体相对太阳辐射通量的朝向，并计入月球红外辐射、太阳反照率及月壤导热，求解表面温度；据此确定航电设备安装位置与太阳翼角度；随后进行数值计算仿真细化FM设计的热响应，最后通过真空热平衡试验验证。

Result: 形成满足质量约束的FM热接口方案，仿真热响应与真空热平衡试验结果相符，验证了设计的热性能与发电能力。

Conclusion: 「解析建模—数值仿真—热真空试验」的递进流程对微小月球漫游车的热设计有效，可作为同类小型行星车热控设计的参考范式。

Abstract: The research focuses on the thermal interface design of the HAKUTO Rover Flight Model (FM) and its evaluation using the final thermal vacuum experiment. Initially, an analytical thermal model was developed, dividing the rover into six distinct radiating surfaces. The surface temperature of the rover was then determined based on its orientation relative to the solar radiative flux, as well as other thermal factors such as lunar radiation, solar albedo, and conduction from the lunar regolith. By considering the impact of these heat sources, suitable mounting locations for the avionics and the angle of the solar panels were determined to optimize the rover's thermal performance and power generation while ensuring the rover's mass remained under 4 kg. After the preliminary thermal analysis, numerical computational simulations were conducted to further elaborate on the thermal response of the final HAKUTO Rover FM design, which was then verified through a thermal balance test in a vacuum environment.

</details>


### [211] [From Predictive Geometry to Action: Learning Complementary Predictive Representations for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28164)
*Xiaoxu Xu,Hao Li,Hongtao Zhang,Yili Tang,Jiafei Cao,Junhao Cai,Haoxiang Ma,Weixin Li,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架，预训练阶段用冻结3D基础模型提取稠密几何特征，压缩为预测性几何表示并保留场景结构及时序演化，再以动作监督学习互补的运动表示；下游后训练通过几何查询与中间特征引导迁移，推理时不需要未来观测。在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、SimplerEnv及真实双臂/单臂任务上稳定提升，WidowX绝对提升10.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的操作策略不仅要感知当前场景几何，还要预测几何在交互下的演化。已有方法要么仅用当前观测的几何增强策略，要么把未来预测当作辅助监督，未解决"预测几何应以何种表示服务动作生成"这一关键问题。

Method: 两阶段训练：1）预训练阶段，用冻结的3D基础模型从多视角视频片段生成稠密几何特征，压缩为预测性几何表示，并以保持场景结构与时间演化为目标优化；在此基础上对几何转移施加动作监督，提取强调动作相关转移的互补运动表示。2）下游后训练阶段，通过几何查询将预测几何注入策略，同时用运动表示引导动作生成通路的中间特征。未来观测仅作为训练期特权监督，推理时无需使用。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、SimplerEnv以及真实世界双臂和单臂任务上一致提升下游策略，WidowX评测绝对提升10.4个百分点；并可跨多种VLA架构与WAM迁移，未来世界预测与动作预测均有改进。

Conclusion: 预测性几何表示应同时包含"场景结构演化"与"动作相关转移"两类互补信息，分离为几何表示与运动表示、并以查询和特征引导方式注入，是一种推理期无需未来观测、可跨架构复用的有效范式。

Abstract: Effective manipulation requires a policy to capture not only about the current scene geometry, but also about how that geometry will evolve under interaction. Existing approaches either augment policies with geometric information from current observations or introduce future prediction as auxiliary supervision, leaving open how predictive geometry should be represented for action generation. Therefore, we propose a two-stage framework, which learns complementary predictive representations from multi-view video clips. During pretraining, a frozen 3D foundation model provides dense geometric features that are compressed into predictive geometric representations and optimized to preserve scene structure and its temporal evolution. Action supervision is then applied to geometric transitions to extract a complementary motor representation that emphasizes action-relevant transitions. During downstream post-training, predictive geometry is transferred through geometry queries, while the motor representation guides intermediate features along the action-generation pathway. Future observations are used only as privileged supervision during training and are not required at inference time. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, SimplerEnv, and real-world bimanual and single-arm tasks, our approach consistently improves downstream policies, including an absolute gain of 10.4 percentage points on the WidowX evaluation. We further demonstrate transfer across multiple VLA architectures and WAMs, with improvements in both future-world and action prediction. The code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [212] [Reasoning as a Distribution: Generative Plan Latents for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28166)
*Matteo Cercola,Katie Z Luo,Milan Ganai,John Alora,Simone Formentin,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出生成式计划潜变量（GPL），将VLA的策略推理状态表示为对"计划"（物体/抓取/路径）的低维条件分布，从而无需重训即可采样、按指令选择、按部署结果重加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA推理模型把中间决策压缩为单一表示，当存在多个有效方案时，这种"承诺"会掩盖本可选择或修正的备选方案，使推理状态不可寻址。

Method: 在π0等VLA上，将推理状态建模为计划空间上的低维条件分布（生成式潜变量），支持三类操作：采样（覆盖）、请求驱动的选择（引导）、部署结果反馈的重加权（结果自适应）。

Result: 在SimplerEnv上用π0测试三种操作，并扩展至RoboCasa、LIBERO与迷宫域；按请求引导使执行跟随率从0.48提升至0.94，而对原始动作分布施加同样引导无可靠增益；显式计划在携带观测未确定之决策信息时最有用。

Conclusion: 显式、可寻址的计划表示优于单一压缩推理表示，但收益依赖于计划是否携带观测之外的决策信息；计划需具备条件分布结构才能实现选择与自适应。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies increasingly reason before acting, but existing reasoning models compress this intermediate decision into a single representation. When several plans are valid, this commitment hides alternatives that could otherwise be selected or revised. We introduce the Generative Plan Latent (GPL), which represents the reasoning state as a low-dimensional conditional distribution over plans, such as which object, grasp, or route to use. This makes the reasoning state addressable: without retraining the VLA, plans can be sampled (coverage), selected on request (steering), and reweighted by deployment outcomes (outcome adaptation). We test all three on SimplerEnv manipulation with $\pi_0$, and extend the analysis to RoboCasa, LIBERO, and a maze domain. Steering the plan raises the fraction of executions that follow the request from 0.48 to 0.94, while the same guidance on the native action distribution shows no reliable gain. Finally, we show that an explicit plan is most useful when it carries decision information not already determined by the observation.

</details>


### [213] [Perceives but Doesn't Brake: A Perception-Action Binding Gap in Reasoning Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28184)
*Matteo Cercola,Katie Z Luo,Simone Formentin,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文指出推理增强VLA驾驶模型存在"感知-动作绑定鸿沟"：危险信息在视觉表征中已编码，但轨迹生成主要由自车速度历史主导。作者在冻结的flow-matching采样器中注入危险条件能量引导，无需重训即可恢复耦合，使足迹侵入率从58.1%降至14.5%并消除刹车相关闭环碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理增强型VLA驾驶模型能感知和推理安全关键危险，却无法把证据转化为安全动作。作者将这一失败机制化为"感知-动作绑定鸿沟"，希望诊断其成因并寻找低成本修复手段。

Method: 用匹配的仿真反事实与实车日志回放做诊断分析：探测视觉表征中是否编码了路径内障碍，并检验纵向规划对障碍距离、自车速度历史及模型推理轨迹的敏感度。随后在Alpamayo-1.5冻结的flow-matching采样器中注入危险条件能量引导（推理时引导），不重训、不改权重。

Result: 路径内危险确实被编码于视觉表示中，但纵向规划受自车速度历史主导，对障碍邻近度与自身推理文本响应较弱；轨迹生成器具备刹车能力但因绑定鸿沟而未执行。注入能量引导后，在精确障碍几何下足迹侵入率由58.1%降至14.5%，在刹车相关闭环测试中只要有足够制动距离即可恢复安全刹车并完全消除碰撞，同时将规划依赖从跟踪自车速度转变为响应障碍距离。

Conclusion: 该工作将危险感知与危险控制解耦，证明模型即便同时具备证据与执行能力仍可能产生不安全行为，并提出一种简单的生成时引导方法弥合绑定鸿沟。局限在于主要依赖仿真环境与精确障碍几何假设。

Abstract: Reasoning-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) driving models can perceive and reason about safety-critical hazards yet fail to translate that evidence into safe actions. We characterize this failure as a perception-action binding gap: hazard information is available inside the model but exerts insufficient control over trajectory generation. Using matched simulated counterfactuals and logged driving replay, we show that in-path hazards are actually encoded in the visual representation, but longitudinal plans are dominated by ego-speed history and respond only weakly to hazard proximity or the model's own reasoning trace. Further analysis shows that the trajectory generator is capable of braking but sometimes fails to do so due to the perception-action binding gap. Without retraining or modifying any weights, we inject a hazard-conditioned energy into the frozen flow-matching sampler of Alpamayo-1.5, restoring the missing coupling at generation time. With exact obstacle geometry, guidance cuts footprint intrusion from 58.1% to 14.5% and recovers safe braking whenever sufficient stopping distance remains, eliminating collisions entirely in our braking-relevant closed-loop battery. It also reverses the diagnosed dependence of the plan, from tracking ego speed to responding to obstacle range. These results separate hazard perception from hazard control in driving VLAs, show that unsafe behavior can arise even when the model has both the evidence and the ability to act on it, and propose a simple fix for bridging this binding gap.

</details>


### [214] [Safe Multi-Robot Collaboration With Uniform Exclusion Using Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28251)
*Thomas Kaminsky,Áron Vékássy,Hammad Izhar,Angelia Nedić,Stephanie Gil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为机器人集群的通信链路提供一个"安全包装器"，用Safe Anytime-Valid Inference在线检验异常检测器的信任值流，仅要求检测器优于随机，即可在任意时刻一致地剔除所有被攻陷链路，同时快速学会信任合法链路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群机器人通过有向、时变的局部通信链路协调，部分链路可能被攻陷并注入任意恶意数据。现有工作难以在不对连通性结构和被攻陷链路数量做实践中无法保证的强假设前提下，同时获得"永久剔除恶意链路"与"快速信任合法链路"两种保证。

Method: 设计一个包裹在机器人通信外层的wrapper：当机器人要用若干入链数据更新状态时，wrapper基于每条链路上异常检测器输出的信任值序列，采用Safe Anytime-Valid Inference（随时有效推断）进行顺序假设检验，从而在线筛选出可信链路子集。仅假设检测器优于随机猜测，无需连通性结构或恶意链路数量上限等限制性假设。

Result: 给出两项统计保证：(1) 以用户选择的高概率，在所有时间上一致地排除全部被攻陷链路；(2) 仍能快速学习信任合法链路。硬件实验在两个对比任务上验证：神经隐式建图（3台LoCoBots）与航向一致性（8台GoPiGos）；并用从硬件采样的信任值做仿真，表明方法可扩展至数百机器人的集群。

Conclusion: 该wrapper以极弱的检测器假设实现了统一时间有效的安全保证与高效学习，兼顾安全性与自主性能，适用于通用任务，且在小规模硬件与大规模仿真中均表现良好。

Abstract: Robot swarms coordinate through directed, time-varying local communication links, some of which may be compromised to allow the transmission of arbitrary malicious data. We provide an algorithm that acts as a wrapper around robot communication---when a robot seeks to update its state using data from some incoming links, our wrapper provides a subset of trusted links whose data can be safely incorporated. It selects these links by testing a stream of trust values---data collected from an anomaly detector on each robot---using Safe Anytime-Valid Inference. We require only that the detector be better than chance. This wrapper ensures safe and performant autonomy by providing two powerful statistical guarantees: it uniformly excludes all compromised links for all time with high user-chosen probability, but nevertheless quickly learns to trust legitimate links. Both guarantees are vital to secure generic tasks, but no previous work provides them simultaneously without restrictive assumptions on connectivity structure or the number of compromised links that are not guaranteed in practice. We study our Safe Wrapper's performance in hardware for two contrasting tasks---neural implicit mapping and heading consensus---deploying on 3 LoCoBots and 8 GoPiGos, respectively. We provide simulations using trust values sampled from hardware to validate that our approach scales well to swarms of hundreds of robots.

</details>


### [215] [Large Multimodal Models for Direct Robot Control: GPT-6 as a Beginning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28259)
*Youwei Zhou,Bohan Hou,Yankai Fu,Yaoxu Lyu,Yifan Ye,Shilin Shan,Chen Guo,Jiaming Liu,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Shanghang Zhang,Jianfei Yang,Zhihe Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统评测 GPT-6 Astra 在闭环机器人操作中的具身能力：探查深度感知、具身上下文学习与触觉理解，并在 5 个操作基准上按 6 个能力维度评估，提出新的几何约束基准 LIBERO-GeoMotion，揭示语义推理与物理执行之间的显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态模型已能与机器人直接结合执行操作，但其具身能力边界尚不清楚，缺乏系统性评测，尤其缺少对"空间理解能否转化为物理可行运动"这一关键环节的诊断工具。

Method: 采用轻量 agent 执行框架做闭环操作评测。1) 三项具身能力探针：深度感知、具身 in-context learning、触觉反馈纠错；2) 在五个既有操作基准上按六个能力维度量化；3) 构建 LIBERO-GeoMotion 几何约束任务基准（精确位姿对齐、平移-旋转协同、动作后果预测）；4) 补充真机零样本实验与推理延迟分析。

Result: RoboDojo 成功率 26.92%，RoboTwin 2.0 76.0%，部分基准具竞争力；LIBERO-GeoMotion 零样本成功率仅 11.1%。语义推理与物理执行之间存在明显落差，尤其体现在后果感知规划、精度和复杂运动协调上。真机零样本仅能完成简单任务，运动可行性、精确控制与物理安全性仍有持续缺陷，推理延迟高。

Conclusion: 结果为通用多模态模型的具身能力提供了实证刻画，明确了走向安全、高效、可靠机器人操作的关键瓶颈（物理可行性、精密控制、安全性、延迟），并给出可复用的诊断性基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in visuospatial understanding and agentic reasoning enable direct robotic manipulation with general-purpose multimodal models, but their embodied capabilities remain poorly understood. This paper systematically evaluate GPT-6 Astra for closed-loop robotic manipulation using a lightweight agent execution framework. We first probe three fundamental embodied capabilities-depth perception, embodied in-context learning, and tactile understanding-and demonstrate that the model can exploit depth information, acquire simple manipulation strategies from contextual demonstrations, and use tactile feedback to correct its actions. We then evaluate GPT-6 Astra across five established manipulation benchmarks along six capability dimensions. Despite competitive performance on several benchmarks-26.92\% success on RoboDojo and 76.0\% on RoboTwin 2.0-our analysis reveals a pronounced gap between semantic reasoning and physical execution, particularly in consequence-aware action planning, precision, and complex motion coordination. To further examine these limitations, we introduce LIBERO-GeoMotion, a new benchmark of geometry-constrained tasks that require precise pose alignment, coordinated translation and rotation, and anticipation of physical action consequences. GPT-6 Astra achieves only 11.1\% zero-shot success, exposing the difficulty of translating spatial understanding into physically feasible motion. Real-world experiments corroborate these findings: zero-shot execution is feasible for simple tasks, yet persistent deficits remain in motion feasibility, precise control, and physical safety, while substantial inference latency further hinders practical deployment. Overall, our results characterize the emerging embodied capabilities of general-purpose multimodal models and delineate the key challenges toward safe, efficient, and reliable robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [216] [Reinforced Iterative Learning Control for elastic joint arms: Guaranteed cross-trajectory generalization under model uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28329)
*Yuri De Santis,Michele Pierallini,Franco Angelini,Manolo Garabini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出强化迭代学习控制（RILC）框架，将ILC与RL并发耦合：ILC提供高质量样本并保证收敛性，RL提供可泛化的策略，实现新轨迹首次试验即高精度跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性/欠驱动机器人的轨迹跟踪受非线性动力学与建模不确定性困扰。传统ILC精度高但每条新轨迹都需独立学习阶段；RL可泛化到新轨迹但样本复杂度高、跟踪精度有限。作者希望融合二者，兼得快速适应与收敛保证。

Method: 构建ILC与RL的并发耦合架构：ILC在线生成高精度迭代样本，RL学习跨轨迹的"尽力而为"（best-effort）策略作为初值/前馈，从而降低初始误差、加速适应。理论上证明RL分量的引入不破坏ILC稳定性，并在特定条件下可渐近降低误差上界。

Result: 在欠驱动4自由度串联弹性机器人上进行仿真与实物实验，针对不同轨迹与建模失配情形验证了首轮即具备高性能跟踪能力，且保持收敛性保证。

Conclusion: ILC-RL并发耦合可有效解决柔性机器人多轨迹跟踪中"高精度vs.可泛化"的矛盾，兼具理论稳定性保证与实证效果，为无显式参数辨识的学习型控制提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Trajectory tracking for compliant robots is challenging due to nonlinear dynamics and modeling uncertainties, often requiring learning-based methods to avoid explicit parameter identification. Iterative Learning Control (ILC) ensures theoretically high precision and preserves the passive compliance of the robot. However, traditional ILC requires a separate learning phase for each new trajectory. Conversely, Reinforcement Learning (RL) generalizes to new trajectories, but practically suffers from high sample complexity and limited tracking precision. This work proposes Reinforced Iterative Learning Control (RILC) that combines the strengths of both approaches to achieve fast adaptation and guaranteed tracking convergence across diverse trajectories. By establishing a concurrent coupling between the two controllers, RILC overcomes the high initial errors and slow adaptation of traditional ILC, enabling high-performance tracking from the very first trials. In this architecture, ILC provides high-quality samples, while RL contributes as a generalizing best-effort policy across iterations. Additionally, a theoretical proof is presented to show that the RL component preserves ILC stability and, under specific conditions, can also reduce the error bound asymptotically. The efficacy of this framework is demonstrated through simulation and experiments on an underactuated 4-degree-of-freedom serial elastic robot using different trajectories and modeling mismatches.

</details>


### [217] [SPAR: Search-trained Push Action Ranker for Efficient Navigation Among Movable Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28362)
*Dhruv Metha Ramesh,Tri-An Nguyen,Junshan Huang,Jingjin Yu,Kostas E. Bekris,Abdeslam Boularias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPAR：用模拟搜索结果训练 transformer 排序器，为可移动障碍物导航（NAMO）中的推动原语排序，区分"立即开路"与"铺垫推"，大幅减少物理仿真调用并在真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAMO 中机器人需重排阻挡物体；部分推进（setup push）本身不开路但为后续开门创造条件，候选序列随长度指数增长，基于物理的搜索代价高。作者希望学习一个能识别"铺垫推"价值的原语优先级模型，以替代昂贵搜索。

Method: 在仿真中对每个物体做最多两推的序列搜索，得到监督信号（区分立即开门与 setup push）。训练 transformer 打分器：以接触令牌（contact tokens）为输入，结合场景几何与可达区域条件，采用回归+排序（ranking）损失。同一模型用于两处：SPAR Search（引导仿真评估的束搜索/迭代扩展）与 SPAR Policy（直接执行选取动作），嵌入 NAMO 规划器处理连续的 keyhole 子问题。

Result: 300 个两推 keyhole 问题上，SPAR Search 相对 Random Search 将中位仿真调用次数降低 7.0×，规划时间降低 3.8×；不重训迁移到 600 个两推内无解的 keyhole 问题，深度上限 5 推、100 次仿真调用内成功率 50.7% vs 随机搜索 20.2%；硬场景 NAMO 规划 47% vs 21%。真机实验中 SPAR Policy 37/40 成功（无物理数字孪生），SPAR Search 33/40，Random Search 27/40。

Conclusion: 学习式原语排序可有效压缩 NAMO 的物理搜索空间，且以"接触—几何—可达性"为条件、从短序列搜索中蒸馏出铺垫推动的长期价值；模型无需重训即可泛化到更难问题，并可直接作为策略在真机无数字孪生下部署。

Abstract: Navigation Among Movable Obstacles (NAMO) requires a robot to rearrange objects that block access to its goal. For a pushing robot, progress may depend on a setup push that enables a later opening without itself clearing a passage. The number of candidate sequences can grow exponentially with sequence length, making physics-based search costly. We introduce the Search-trained Push Action Ranker (SPAR), which learns to prioritize push primitives from simulated search outcomes. Searches over sequences of up to two pushes per object provide supervision that distinguishes immediate openings from setup pushes. A transformer trained with regression and ranking losses scores primitives using contact tokens conditioned on scene geometry and reachability regions. The same model guides simulator evaluations in SPAR Search and selects actions for direct execution in SPAR Policy. Both modes solve successive keyhole problems that open access to neighboring free-space regions within a NAMO planner. Across successful runs on $300$ simulated two-push keyhole problems, SPAR Search reduces median simulator calls by $7.0\times$, leading to a $3.8\times$ reduction in planning time relative to Random Search. Without retraining, the same ranker guides search on $600$ keyhole problems which have no solution within two pushes. At a depth limit of five pushes, SPAR Search achieves $50.7\%$ success within $100$ simulator calls, compared with $20.2\%$ for Random Search. These gains carry over to NAMO planning: on hard simulated scenes, SPAR Search achieves $47\%$ success within $100$ simulator calls, compared with $21\%$ for Random Search. In real-robot experiments, SPAR Policy succeeds in $37/40$ trials without a physics-based digital twin, compared with $33/40$ for SPAR Search and $27/40$ for Random Search.

</details>


### [218] [CAPSafe: A Benchmark for Contextual Action and Plan Safety Reasoning in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28381)
*Youmna Farag,Svetlana Stoyanchev,Simon Keizer,Mohan Li,Rama Doddipatla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPSafe基准在动作级与长程计划级两个层面评测LLM具身智能体的安全推理，发现"危害识别"与"安全规划"存在显著落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的具身智能体需要安全推理能力，但现有评测缺乏对环境相关（situational）危害、多危害共现及危害严重度分层建模，也少有对长程安全计划的考察。

Method: 构建两级基准：(1) 动作级数据集，判断给定环境上下文下单步动作是否安全；(2) 计划级数据集，要求为复杂危险家务任务生成安全长程计划。危害由物体属性推断、允许多危害共现，并按相对严重度分级标注；评测9个闭源与开源LLM。

Result: 危害识别准确率较高（最优超过90%），但安全长程规划明显更难：Gemini-3-pro安全成功率最高78.19%，其余模型均低于65%。

Conclusion: 长动作序列上的鲁棒安全推理仍是开放问题，识别与规划能力之间存在系统性鸿沟。

Abstract: Reasoning about safety is essential for embodied agents powered by large language models (LLMs). We introduce the Contextual Action and Plan Safety benchmark (CAPSafe), designed to evaluate safety reasoning at two levels: (1) an action-level dataset that tests whether a single action is safe given its environmental context, and (2) a plan-level dataset that evaluates whether agents can generate safe long-horizon plans for complex hazardous household tasks. CAPSafe models safety conditions where hazards are situational (inferred from object properties), multiple hazards can co-occur within the same environment, and each is annotated with a relative severity level for fine-grained analysis. Evaluating nine closed and open-source LLMs reveals a substantial gap between hazard recognition and safe long-horizon planning. Several models achieve high accuracy in recognizing unsafe actions (exceeding 90% in the best cases), yet safe long-horizon planning remains substantially harder. Gemini-3-pro achieves the highest safe success rate (78.19%), while other models remain below 65%. These results highlight that robust safety reasoning over long action sequences remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [219] [Plausible Future, Wrong Choice: Steering World-Action Models toward Intended Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28431)
*Zhuohao Li,Jiawei He,Yuxuan Yan,Lang Zhou,Yinghao Li,Yanrui Li,Tianyu Zhang,Yi-Yang Song,Xirui Wu andYi-Lin Wei,Fating Hong,Qing Zhang,Zhilin Zhao,Jingdong Wang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaskWAM：面向世界-动作模型的阶段感知闭环框架，用Mode Tokens表征细粒度任务要求、State Tokens跟踪执行进度，构成结构化任务状态以抑制长程操作中的“阶段混淆”，并发布StageBench多阶段操作基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM虽能联合建模未来世界演化与机器人动作，但仅“生成多样未来”不够，还需使未来匹配当前任务要求与执行进度。长程操作中不同任务/阶段存在相似的观测-动作片段，导致模型生成局部合理但方向错误的rollout，漂移到错误任务阶段（Stage Confusion），现有方法缺乏对任务阶段的结构化约束。

Method: 提出TaskWAM闭环阶段感知框架：以Mode Tokens编码细粒度任务需求（目标模式），以State Tokens跟踪执行进度（阶段状态），二者构成结构化任务状态作为条件，引导世界-动作联合生成朝向预期未来；同时构建StageBench——参数化阶段变化、可自动化评测的多阶段操作基准。

Result: 在仿真基准与真实世界任务上，TaskWAM有效降低阶段混淆，性能与SOTA方法竞争；并展现强即插即用迁移能力，跨异构WAM架构与多类本体（单臂、双臂夹爪、灵巧手、人形机器人）均适用。

Conclusion: 将任务阶段显式结构化为“模式+状态”token是缓解长程操作中世界-动作模型漂移的有效途径，且该方法与架构和本体解耦，具备良好通用性；StageBench为该问题提供了受控评测手段。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied generalist policies by jointly modeling future world evolution and robot actions across diverse manipulation tasks. However, representing diverse futures is only the first step: a capable WAM must also generate futures that match the requirements of the current task and execution progress. In long-horizon manipulation where similar observation-action segments occur across different tasks and stages, existing approaches may generate locally plausible yet misdirected rollouts to drift toward wrong task stage, i.e., a phenomenon we term Stage Confusion. To address this problem, we propose TaskWAM, a closed-loop, stage-aware framework that represents fine-grained task requirements with Mode Tokens and tracks execution progress with State Tokens, forming a structured task state to steer world--action generation toward intended future. We further introduce StageBench, a controlled multi-stage manipulation benchmark with parameterized stage variations and automated this http URL experiments on both simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks demonstrate that TaskWAM effectively reduces confusion and achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods. Moreover, TaskWAM provides strong plug-and-play transfer across heterogeneous WAM architectures and robot embodiments, including single-arm robots, dual-arm grippers, dexterous hands, and humanoid robots.

</details>


### [220] [Physical AI for Intralogistics: Benchmarking the NVIDIA Sim-to-Real Ecosystem for Cosmos-Enhanced COMPASS Navigation on Industrial Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28432)
*Charbel Abi Hana,Rayan Zaitouni,Kameel Amareen,Mohamad Mostafa,Hasan Sayour,Wei Liu,Yan Chang,Mihir Acharya,Anthony Rizk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文系统评测 NVIDIA X-Mobility + COMPASS 工作流在 Idealworks 工业机器人上的 sim-to-real 可靠性，通过数字孪生微调与 Cosmos 生成式 sim-to-real 增强，将真实场景成功率从 65.3% 提升到 91.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典导航栈可靠但每换机器人或场地都需重新工程化；学习式导航用仿真训练/微调替代手调参数，但"按此流程适配的策略在真实工业机器人上是否仍可靠"缺乏系统验证。

Method: 在仿真中将 COMPASS 基准扩展到两种 embodiment（差速驱动机器人与全向 Humanoid Mobile Platform）及 Idealworks 研发中心数字孪生；真实世界用数字孪生数据微调 X-Mobility（分别加/不加 NVIDIA Cosmos 生成的 sim-to-real 变体），并在孪生上从头训练 COMPASS，部署至机载 Jetson AGX Xavier，在真实研发中心对比纯仿真训练策略、原始 Idealworks 导航栈，指标含成功率、效率、安全性、平滑度与机载算力；静态场景孪生/实机双跑以量化 sim-to-real gap。

Result: 数字孪生训练 + Cosmos 增强使四场景真实成功率 65.3%→91.7%，加权行驶时间近乎减半，与产线导航栈差距缩至 8.3 个百分点，全程机载运行；结合全局规划器可在所有试验中到达远处被遮挡目标。

Conclusion: 数字孪生 + 生成式 sim-to-real 数据增强能显著缩小学习式导航的实机差距，接近产线栈水平并具备远距遮挡目标可达性，为工业机器人策略适配提供了可复现的评测流程。

Abstract: Classical robot navigation stacks are reliable but often require re-engineering for each new robot type and site. Learning-based navigation shifts this effort from hand-tuned parameters to data generation and training, where a policy adapts to each new embodiment or facility by fine-tuning in simulation. Whether policies adapted this way stay reliable on real industrial robots has seen little systematic testing. We study this for NVIDIA's X-Mobility and COMPASS workflow for training learned navigation policies on Idealworks robots. In simulation, we extend the COMPASS benchmark with two embodiments, the differential-drive this http URL and an omnidirectional Humanoid Mobile Platform (HMP), and with a digital twin of the Idealworks R&D center. For the real world, we fine-tune X-Mobility on data from this digital twin, with and without sim-to-real variants generated by NVIDIA Cosmos, train COMPASS on top of it from scratch in the digital twin, deploy the resulting policies on the this http URL 's onboard Jetson AGX Xavier, and benchmark them in the Idealworks R&D center against COMPASS trained only on NVIDIA's environments and the existing Idealworks navigation stack on task success, efficiency, safety, smoothness and onboard compute. Running every static scenario both in a digital-twin replica and in the real facility measures the sim-to-real gap and how far Cosmos-augmented data closes it. Training in the digital twin with Cosmos sim-to-real augmentation raises the real-world success rate of learned navigation from 65.3% to 91.7% across four scenarios and nearly halves its weighted travel time compared with simulation-only training, bringing it within 8.3 points of the production navigation stack, fully onboard; with an integrated global planner, it also reaches far, occluded goals in every trial.

</details>


### [221] [GPT-6-Astra Understands Touch and Outperform Vision--Tactile--Language--Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28457)
*Ziyong Wang,Yupei Lin,Runxuan Huang,Liang Lin,Dongyu Zhang,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPT-as-Tactile-Policy: 用 GPT-6-Astra 作为通用多模态策略，把专家演示总结成任务指导与触觉参考，结合多模态观测与交互历史输出控制指令，并在无机器人专用预训练/逐任务微调下于 5 个 UniVTAC 接触丰富操作任务上取得 93.8% 平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉操作策略依赖专门的触觉预训练与逐任务优化，数据与训练成本高；同时通用多模态模型是否真正理解交互物理、能否据触觉反馈修正动作仍缺乏验证。作者以接触丰富操作为试验台，检验通用多模态模型的物理理解与接触驱动纠错能力。

Method: 将专家演示离线压缩为"任务指导 + 触觉参考"（用于接触状态判定与纠错策略），在推理时把该上下文与多模态观测、交互历史一并输入 GPT-6-Astra，由模型直接生成控制命令，并依据在线触觉反馈调整动作；不使用机器人领域专用预训练，也不进行逐任务策略微调。评估采用 UniVTAC 五个接触丰富任务。

Result: 五个 UniVTAC 任务平均成功率 93.8%，与 SOTA 策略总体相当，部分任务更高；Insert Hole 任务上触觉反馈使成功率从 31% 提升至 99%；执行轨迹分析显示出接触引导的探询与纠错行为。

Conclusion: 免训练的通用多模态模型配合结构化专家上下文与在线触觉反馈，可作为有效的接触丰富操作策略，说明显式专家经验 + 在线反馈能支撑操作推进；但证据限于仿真基准与较强先验模型，真机迁移与泛化性尚待验证。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal models have shown promise as direct robot action policies, yet whether they grasp the physics underlying real-world interactions and can apply that understanding to guide action remains underexplored. We investigate this question using contact-rich manipulation as a representative testbed, examining tactile understanding as a concrete instance of this broader capability. Contact-rich manipulation requires interpreting tactile feedback and adjusting actions after obstruction. Existing learned tactile policies acquire these capabilities through specialized pretraining and per-task optimization, with substantial data and training costs. We present GPT-as-Tactile-Policy, which summarizes expert demonstrations into task guidance and tactile references for contact assessment and corrective action. GPT-6-Astra combines this context with multimodal observations and interaction history to generate control commands and adjust actions through contact feedback. Without additional robotics-specific pretraining or per-task policy fine-tuning, our method achieves 93.8\% mean success across five UniVTAC tasks. Overall performance is comparable to state-of-the-art policies, with higher success on selected tasks. On Insert Hole, tactile feedback improves success from 31\% to 99\%. Execution analysis further demonstrates contact-guided probing and correction, supporting manipulation progress through explicit expert experience and online feedback.

</details>


### [222] [MARE: An Open-Source Lunar Rover Simulator with Shadow Ground Truth for Perception and Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28465)
*Raúl Castilla-Arquillo,Miguel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARE is an open-source Unreal Engine 5 + ROS 2 simulation suite for lunar rover perception and autonomy, comprising a terrain/DEM generator, a physics-based rover project with stereo/lidar/event/SPAD sensors and pixel-accurate ground truth, and a web-based GNC mission-control stack.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polar lunar exploration requires autonomous rovers that can perceive and traverse unstructured terrain under extreme low-sun illumination, where long high-contrast shadows break stereo vision, segmentation, and localization. Collecting real sensor data with accurate ground truth via field campaigns or analog facilities is costly and lacks diversity, motivating a photorealistic, physics-based simulator.

Method: Terrain Studio procedurally generates cratered and rocky lunar DEMs to build scenes; a dedicated UE5 project hosts two physics-based rovers with plugins for stereo, lidar, event, and SPAD cameras that stream time-stamped sensor data with pixel-accurate ground truth; a GNC stack provides web-based mission control. Validation compares rendered vs. predicted shadows via IoU and analyzes stereo depth coverage in shadowed vs. sunlit regions.

Result: Mean shadow IoU of 0.79 between predicted and rendered shadows at low sun elevations; stereo depth analysis confirms decreased pixel coverage in shadowed regions relative to sunlit terrain, confirming the simulator reproduces the intended illumination-induced perception degradation.

Conclusion: MARE offers a low-cost, open-source, extensible platform for developing and benchmarking lunar rover perception and navigation under realistic illumination, with validated shadow modeling and shadow-aware stereo evaluation; it is primarily an infrastructure/data-generation tool rather than a new perception algorithm.

Abstract: Lunar surface exploration increasingly relies on autonomous rovers that must perceive and traverse unstructured terrain under extreme illumination conditions. Near the lunar poles, low sun elevations produce long, high-contrast shadows that challenge stereo vision, terrain segmentation, and localization. Developing perception and navigation algorithms for these conditions requires large amounts of sensor data with accurate ground truth, which field campaigns and analog facilities can only provide at high cost and with limited variety. This paper presents MARE (Modular Autonomy and Rendering Environment), an open-source simulation suite for lunar rover perception and autonomy built on Unreal Engine 5 and ROS 2. MARE consists of three integrated components. Terrain Studio generates cratered and rocky lunar Digital Elevation Maps (DEMs) to build simulation scenes. A dedicated Unreal Engine project hosts two physics-based rovers with plugins for stereo, lidar, event, and Single-Photon Avalanche Diode (SPAD) cameras, streaming time-stamped sensor data and pixel-accurate ground truth. Lastly, a Guidance, Navigation, and Control (GNC) stack provides web-based mission control. Validation shows a mean Intersection over Union (IoU) of 0.79 between predicted and rendered shadows at low sun elevations, and stereo depth analysis verifies decreased pixel coverage in shadowed regions relative to sunlit terrain.

</details>


### [223] [Fast Motion Retargeting Pipeline for Scalable Humanoid Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28470)
*Daniel Marew,Sangjun Park,Nisal Perera,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRDV is a scalable human motion retargeting framework that couples a fast constrained kinematic optimization (custom SQP + Real-Time Iteration + chunked parallelism, up to 9,156 FPS) with a contact-implicit iLQR dynamics validation stage, and validates retargeting quality via RL tracking policies and sim-to-real on a Unitree G1.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large-scale human motion capture data is a valuable demo source for humanoid learning, but retargeting diverse motions to different humanoid embodiments is hard; existing kinodynamic trajectory optimization pipelines are computationally expensive and need manual per-motion tuning.

Method: Two-stage pipeline: (1) fast constrained kinematic retargeting using a custom SQP solver with Real-Time Iteration and parallel chunk-based processing, embedding robot-specific constraints to suppress artifacts such as ground penetration and self-collision; (2) optional dynamics validation with a contact-implicit iLQR controller that tests references under full rigid-body dynamics, flags dynamically infeasible motions and refines contact artifacts (foot floating, foot skating).

Result: Kinematic stage reaches up to 9,156 FPS (~136× faster than GMR and ProtoMotions). Retargeting quality is assessed by training motion-tracking RL policies with several SOTA pipelines and comparing tracking fidelity and success rates; sim-to-real transfer demonstrated on a Unitree G1. Code and videos released.

Conclusion: Decoupling fast constrained kinematics from dynamics validation yields a scalable, artifact-reduced retargeting pipeline suitable for large mocap corpora and validated end-to-end by policy tracking performance and real-robot deployment.

Abstract: While large-scale human motion capture datasets provide a rich source of demonstrations for humanoid robot learning, retargeting the diverse motions to different humanoid robots remains a nontrivial challenge. Existing kinodynamic trajectory optimization pipelines are computationally expensive and often require manual adjustments per motion. We present FRDV (Fast Retargeting with Dynamics Validation), a scalable framework consisting of rapid constrained kinematic retargeting and an additional dynamics validation pipeline. Its kinematic stage integrates a custom Sequential Quadratic Programming solver, Real-Time Iteration, and parallel chunk-based processing, achieving up to 9,156 frames per second, approximately up to 136 times faster than GMR and ProtoMotions, while robot-specific constraints are incorporated into the optimization to reduce common retargeting artifacts, such as ground penetration and self-collision. For motions requiring additional verification, a contact implicit iLQR controller is utilized to test the retargeted reference under full rigid body dynamics. This dynamics-based validation identifies dynamically infeasible motions and refines contact-related artifacts such as foot floating and foot skating. We evaluate retargeting quality by training motion tracking reinforcement learning policies using several state-of-the-art pipelines and comparing their tracking fidelity and success rates. Furthermore, sim-to-real transfer is demonstrated on a Unitree G1 humanoid. Videos and code are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [224] [SuperMimic: Scaling Humanoid Whole-Body Control with One Differentiable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28480)
*Qiayuan Liao,Yiyang Shao,Junfeng Long,Kevin Zakka,Roberto Horowitz,Pieter Abbeel,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperMimic 用可微世界模型（基于刚体仿真日志拟合的共享动力学模型）摊销大规模人体动作跟踪学习，世界模型与跟踪策略在同环训练、短时想象 rollout 上用 BPTT 提供解析梯度，单 GPU 3 小时即超越 128 GPU 训练 7 天的 SOTA 基线，GPU 工时降超 1000 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模人体动作数据集跟踪已成为通用、富有表现力的人形全身控制主流路线，但规模化成本极高：现有流水线要么靠逐动作专家策略再经有损蒸馏合并，要么用集群级算力硬训单一策略。作者希望消除算力瓶颈，使动作跟踪的可扩展性（scaling law）可被实际测量。

Method: 以标准刚体仿真器记录的 transition 拟合一个跨所有动作共享动力学的可微世界模型，对所有动作摊销学习；世界模型与跟踪策略在同一循环中联合训练，对短"想象 rollout"做 backpropagation-through-time 为策略提供解析梯度，替代 model-free RL 的样本低效探索。

Result: 单 GPU 3 小时训练的回报超过在 128 GPU 上训练 7 天的 SOTA 基线；GPU 工时减少 >1000×；129,514 条基准动作中仅 25 条失败（基线 179 条）；2 GPU 训练 9.8 小时后失败数为 0；从 1 到 264 小时动作数据，世界模型预测误差保持稳定，达到目标回报所需 GPU 工时增长缓慢；策略迁移到实体 Unitree G1，完成多样且具杂技性的动作。

Conclusion: 基于共享动力学可微世界模型的训练范式将人形全身动作跟踪的算力瓶颈移除，使动作跟踪 scaling law 的测量变得可行，并已完成真机人形部署验证。

Abstract: Tracking large human-motion datasets has become the dominant route to general, expressive humanoid whole-body control, but scaling across many motions is expensive. Current pipelines use model-free reinforcement learning, either merging per-motion specialists through lossy distillation, or brute-forcing a single policy with cluster-scale compute. SuperMimic amortizes learning across all motions with one differentiable world model of their shared dynamics, fit from logged transitions of a standard rigid-body simulator. The world model and the tracking policy train together in one loop, and backpropagation-through-time over short imagined rollouts gives the policy analytic gradients. Fast imagined rollouts and analytic gradients make policy learning efficient. In 3 hours on a single GPU, SuperMimic surpasses the return of a state-of-the-art motion tracker baseline trained for 7 days on 128 GPUs. It reduces GPU-hours by more than $1000\times$ and fails on only 25 of 129,514 benchmark motions, versus 179 failures for the baseline. After 9.8 hours on two GPUs, SuperMimic achieves zero failures. From 1 to 264 hours of motion, the world model's prediction error stays stable, and GPU-hours to a target return grow slowly. Our policy transfers to a physical Unitree G1 across diverse and acrobatic motions. SuperMimic removes compute as the bottleneck to scaling humanoid whole-body control, making it practical to measure motion-tracking scaling laws.

</details>


### [225] [VLM-Guided Contact Retargeting and Generative Partner Modeling for Humanoid-Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28523)
*Yuto Shibata,Kotaro Amaya,Kashu Yamazaki,Lalit Jayanti,Yoshimitsu Aoki,Mariko Isogawa,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REACT：从人到人演示学习可部署的人形机器人交互策略，以语义接触结构为监督信号，经VLM引导的接触建模与重定向、特权接触奖励学习交互策略，再蒸馏为仅用机载自视角深度的策略，并用生成式伙伴状态建模应对严重部分可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在近距离物理接触时，人类伙伴被遮挡、截断或出视野，位姿估计失败，常依赖外部跟踪或特权人体状态；同时人-人动捕数据本身存在重互相遮挡误差。

Method: 1) VLM引导的接触建模与重定向流水线，识别人-人演示中的语义接触并迁移到人形-人几何上，保持交互结构；2) 用这些标注训练带交互感知接触奖励的特权交互策略，学习物理上合理的行为；3) 蒸馏为可部署的自视角深度策略，训练完全基于机载传感与照片级真实的人体深度渲染；4) 引入生成式伙伴状态建模目标，从被遮挡的自视角预测伙伴运动。

Result: 仿真与真机Unitree G1上验证五种接触丰富交互。语义接触建模显著提升演示交互的保持度；生成式伙伴状态建模在五种交互中的四种提升接触召回率，遮挡严重时最明显；真机上仅靠机载传感完成全部五种交互。

Conclusion: 以语义接触结构作为连接演示处理、交互学习与机载控制的统一监督信号，可有效弥合人-人演示与人形部署间的差距，并在严重部分可观测下实现纯机载传感的接触丰富人-人交互。

Abstract: Humanoid-human interaction requires robots to operate under severe partial observability: during close physical contact, the human partner is occluded, truncated, or outside the robot's field of view, causing pose estimation to fail and often necessitating external tracking or privileged human state. At the same time, heavy mutual occlusions introduce errors into the human-human motion-capture data used for learning. We present REACT, a framework for learning deployable humanoid-human interaction policies from human-human demonstrations. REACT treats semantic contact structure as the key supervisory signal connecting demonstration processing, interaction learning, and onboard control. First, a VLM-guided contact modeling and retargeting pipeline identifies semantically meaningful contacts and transfers them onto humanoid-human geometry while preserving interaction structure. Using these annotations, privileged interaction policies learn physically grounded behaviors through interaction-aware contact rewards and are then distilled into deployable egocentric depth policies trained entirely from onboard sensing using photorealistic human depth rendering. To address severe partial observability, we introduce generative partner-state modeling objectives that predict partner motion from occluded egocentric views. We evaluate REACT in simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid across five contact-rich interactions. Semantic contact modeling substantially improves preservation of demonstrated interactions, while generative partner-state modeling improves contact recall on four of five interactions, most under heavy occlusion. On real hardware, REACT performs all five interactions using onboard sensing alone.

</details>


### [226] [MAPLE: Mondrian-Anchored Prior for Learning-free Indoor Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28566)
*Kelvin Aladum,Nisar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAPLE：一种免训练的室内探索预测先验。将 Mondrian 过程改造为在线生成式先验，以当前部分地图中的几何线索为条件，采样多张可能的地图假设，并在每个假设内独立计算可见性以估计 frontier gain，从而保留不同假设间视野预测的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人探索未知室内环境时需在大部分区域未观测的情况下决策移动方向。预测传感器范围外的占据情况有助于决策，但现有方法多依赖需离线训练、探索中反复推理的学习模型，计算开销大。

Method: 基于 Mondrian 过程构建免训练的程序化生成先验，条件化于当前部分地图的已观测几何线索，在线生成多个合理的地图假设；在每个假设内部单独评估可见性，聚合得到 frontier gain 估计，而非对预测图做单一平均。

Result: 在 KTH 楼层平面图与真实 SLAM 环境、多种传感器距离设定下一致优于非预测式探索，并与基于学习的预测方法相当；程序化构造计算量低。实验还显示更准确的地图预测不一定带来更好的探索效果，预测的表示与使用方式至关重要。

Conclusion: 免训练的生成式地图先验可在有限算力下实现有效的预测式探索；探索性能更依赖预测的表示与决策融合方式，而非单纯的预测精度。

Abstract: Robots exploring unknown indoor environments must decide where to move while much of the environment remains unseen. Predicting the occupancy of unseen space beyond the current sensor range can improve these decisions, but recent approaches commonly rely on learned models that require offline training and repeated inference during exploration. We introduce the Mondrian-Anchored Prior for Learning-free Indoor Exploration (MAPLE), a training-free predictive approach that adapts the Mondrian process into an online generative prior conditioned on observed geometric cues in the current partial map. MAPLE generates multiple plausible map hypotheses and evaluates visibility separately within each to estimate frontier gain, preserving differences in predicted visibility across hypotheses. Across KTH floor-plans and real-SLAM environments at multiple sensor ranges, MAPLE consistently improves over non-predictive exploration and remains competitive with learned predictive approaches. Its lightweight procedural construction enables predictive exploration with limited computational resources. Experimental results further show that more accurate map prediction does not necessarily produce better exploration, highlighting the importance of how predictions are represented and used in exploration decisions.

</details>


### [227] [SceneRig: An Agentic System for Simulation-Ready 3D Scene Reconstruction from Single Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28585)
*Rundong Luo,Yunong Liu,Di Cao,Matthew Tancik,Shyamal Buch,Colton Stearns,Wenqi Xian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SceneRig：单图生成可编辑、物理可用的仿真场景的智能体系统。它结合几何/视觉/物理工具重建3D场景，再由智能体在物理仿真器内执行"编辑-验证"闭环以保证物理稳定性，提升实例分割、物体外观与3D布局质量，并在真实机器人策略预测与操作回放上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真需要大量多样化、可直接仿真的场景，而从单张图像重建是最有前景的替代方案；但仅追求视觉相似不够——物体必须被正确恢复、准确摆放并具备物理接地。现有依赖事后物理沉降（post-hoc physics settling）的方法常导致物体错位，场景"看着对但操纵表现差"。

Method: SceneRig 是一个智能体（agentic）系统：调用几何、视觉与物理工具从单图重建忠实3D场景；随后由智能体在物理仿真器内进行"编辑-验证"迭代循环（edit-and-validate loops），逐轮修正物体位姿与相互穿插，达成物理稳定。

Result: 在多样输入上，SceneRig 提升了实例分割、以物体为中心的外观表现和3D布局精度，同时保持场景物理稳定；下游机器人任务中，其生成场景对真实机器人策略结果的预测比基线更准确，并能更可靠地支持已录演示操作的回放而无需调整轨迹。

Conclusion: 该文指出"视觉相似≠仿真可用"，提出以智能体闭环编辑-验证替代事后物理沉降，把物理接地纳入重建流程；结果表明其在重建质量与机器人下游实用性（策略评估预测、演示回放）两方面均有增益，属于"智能体驱动的物体/场景重建与生成"核心方向。

Abstract: Simulation is a powerful tool for robotics, but building diverse, simulation-ready scenes at scale remains difficult. Reconstructing scenes from single images is a promising alternative, but visual similarity alone is insufficient: objects must also be recovered, accurately placed, and physically grounded. Existing methods that rely on post-hoc physics settling often misplace objects, producing scenes that look correct but behave poorly under manipulation. We present SceneRig, an agentic system that turns a single image into an editable, simulation-ready scene. SceneRig combines geometric, visual, and physical tools to reconstruct a faithful 3D scene. An agent then performs edit-and-validate loops inside a physics simulator to achieve physical stability. Across diverse inputs, SceneRig improves instance segmentation, object-centric appearance, and 3D placement while keeping the reconstructed scenes physically stable. Furthermore, SceneRig is effective for downstream robotics: its scenes predict real-robot policy outcomes more accurately than the baseline method and more reliably support replay of recorded manipulations without trajectory adaptation.

</details>


### [228] [AgenticRoboRL: Bringing Coding-Agent Post-Training to Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28589)
*Xinxin Liu,Bingxin Xu,Wenrui Bao,Ming Li,Wentao Bao,Chen Chen,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticRoboRL：通过SFT+多轮RL后训练机器人规划器，用执行框架专属适配器统一VLA技能调用与控制代码生成两类执行机制，9B规划器在LIBERO-PRO上成功率18.1%→71.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic机器人系统依赖强闭源模型作规划器（如Codex/GPT-5.5），仅靠更好harness与外部记忆提升性能，规划器自身能力未被训练；编码agent已证明后训练可显著提升能力，故探究后训练能否同样提升机器人规划器。

Method: 提出AgenticRoboRL框架：SFT+多轮强化学习训练配方；针对不同执行机制（VLA技能调用、控制代码生成）设计harness-specific适配器，将记录的规划器交互与任务结果关联，使执行组件保持冻结的同时共用统一RL训练管线。

Result: LIBERO-PRO上，9B规划器成功率从18.1%提升至71.0%（无外部记忆），接近Codex(GPT-5.5)带外部记忆的72.1%。

Conclusion: 后训练是提升机器人规划器的有效路径；将开源框架与训练后checkpoint。

Abstract: Agentic robotics systems combine planners with robot harnesses to perform complex manipulation tasks. Recent work improves these systems through better harnesses and external memory, but relies on powerful closed-source models as planners and does not improve the planners' own capabilities. In contrast, coding agents have improved substantially by post-training the model itself. We therefore investigate whether post-training can also improve robot planners. However, applying it across robot harnesses requires accommodating different execution mechanisms, including VLA skill invocation and control-code generation. To this end, we introduce AgenticRoboRL, a framework for post-training robot planners across different harnesses. The training recipe combines supervised fine-tuning with multi-turn reinforcement learning. To accommodate different execution mechanisms, harness-specific adapters connect recorded planner interactions with task outcomes, enabling a common RL training pipeline while keeping execution components fixed. On LIBERO-PRO, AgenticRoboRL raises the success rate of a 9B-parameter planner from 18.1% to 71.0% without external memory, approaching the 72.1% achieved by Codex (GPT-5.5) with external memory. These results demonstrate post-training as an effective approach for improving robot planners. We will release the framework and trained planner checkpoints.

</details>


### [229] [Physical Canvas: A Vision Tactile World Model For Robot Policy Evaluation And Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28613)
*Zebin Xing,Yuhang Zheng,Yupeng Zheng,Yichen Zhang,Zichen Li,Shangqing Zhou,Yujie Zang,Yuchen Liu,Zhongpu Xia,Haoran Li,Qichao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysCanvas：统一多视角RGB、触觉图像、marker位移、任务状态与动作的状态一致视触觉世界模型，用作闭环策略评估与GRPO后训练的交互模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实物理交互评估与后训练机器人策略成本高、难扩展；纯视觉世界模型虽预测合理但接触动力学失真，反馈不可靠。

Method: 所有模态共享视频VAE与预训练生成骨干，将触觉作为"光学触觉"复用视觉/时序先验；用正运动学+相机投影生成运动图作为空间对齐的动作条件，配初始RGB锚点与滚动参考观测做序列生成；冻结的Scene/Modality/State三个Reward Model进行一致性感知后训练。

Result: 四个真实操作任务上，加入State Reward Model使世界模型与真实成功率逐任务Pearson相关系数均值从0.602升至0.885；世界模型内GRPO后训练将π0.5真实成功率从59.1%（SFT）提升至70.8%。

Conclusion: 统一多模态建模+一致性感知后训练可显著提升世界模型作为策略评估器与训练环境的可靠性，视触觉融合是接触丰富操作的关键。

Abstract: Evaluating and post-training robot policies through physical interaction is costly and difficult to scale, motivating action-conditioned world models as interactive simulators. However, visually plausible predictions can misrepresent contact dynamics, providing unreliable feedback for policy evaluation and learning. We introduce PhysCanvas, a state-consistent visuo-tactile world model for contact-rich robot manipulation. PhysCanvas unifies multi-view RGB, tactile images, marker displacement, task state, and actions within a unified vision space. All modalities share a video VAE and pretrained generative backbone, allowing optical touch to reuse visual and temporal priors. Motion images derived through forward kinematics and camera projection provide spatially aligned action conditioning, while an initial RGB anchor and updated reference observations guide sequential generation. Our consistency-aware post-training uses the frozen Scene Reward Model, Modality Reward Model, and State Reward Model to supervise scene preservation, visual-tactile compatibility, and agreement with real task outcomes. The resulting world model supports closed-loop policy rollouts for evaluation and provides imagined interactions and rewards for GRPO policy updates. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, when policies are rolled out in the world model, State Reward Model supervision increases the arithmetic mean of task-wise Pearson correlations between world-model and real-world success rates from 0.602 to 0.885. Policy post-training in the reward-guided world model further increases $\pi_{0.5}$'s mean real-world success rate from a 59.1% SFT baseline to 70.8%. These results demonstrate the value of unified multimodal modeling and consistency-aware post-training for robot policy evaluation and learning. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [230] [Industrial ReMEmbR: Scaling Spatiotemporal Memory to Robot Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28624)
*Jad Yammine,Malek Wahidi,Abrar Anwar,Yan Chang,Mihir Acharya,Anthony Rizk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IndustrialRemembr：把 NVIDIA ReMEmbR 扩展到机器人车队，构建共享时空语义记忆；机器人端用 Jetson 提取检索嵌入、视觉 token 与重建 latent，按需生成场景描述，并用缓存视觉 token 的分离式 VLM 解码复现原模型输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库机器人车队持续观测环境，但把观测保存为可复用语义记忆的计算、通信与存储开销大（如解释反复发生的通道堵塞需跨多机、多班次回看视频）；同时原始视频上传存在隐私合规（GDPR）问题。

Method: 每台机器人用 Jetson 上的 Cosmos-Embed1 编码检索嵌入、Cosmos3-Edge 编码视觉 token、Wan2.2 VAE 编码重建 latent，不上传原始帧；在图像 patch 上复用编码模型检测人员并置零以实现 PII 抽象；将 Cosmos3-Edge 在视觉-语言边界切分，视觉编码器留在机器人、语言解码器部署于本地服务器；Nemotron agent 按内容/位置/时间检索记忆，并用缓存视觉 token 查询解码器。

Result: 写入时不做 caption 生成；缓存 token 解码在全部 450 次测试解码中与未切分模型输出一致；声称降低服务器端计算并避免原始图像外传。

Conclusion: 提出面向机器人车队的共享时空记忆系统，通过“按需描述 + 分离式 VLM + 端侧编码”在隐私、通信与算力约束下实现可复用的语义记忆，但缺少下游机器人任务性能与真实部署的定量评估。

Abstract: Warehouse robot fleets continuously observe operational conditions, but preserving these observations as reusable semantic memory incurs substantial computation, communication, and storage costs. For example, explaining recurring aisle blockages can require reviewing video from multiple robots across several shifts. We present IndustrialRemembr, a shared spatiotemporal memory system that extends NVIDIA ReMEmbR to robot fleets and reduces server-side computation by generating scene descriptions only on demand. On each robot, an NVIDIA Jetson encodes video clips into retrieval embeddings with Cosmos-Embed1, vision tokens with Cosmos3-Edge, and reconstruction latents with the Wan2.2 VAE, without uploading raw camera frames. The encoding models are re-used to detect people in image patches, zeroing them out to support PII abstraction for GDPR-compliant deployments. We split Cosmos3-Edge at its vision-language boundary, placing the vision encoder on the robot and the language decoder on an on-premises server. A Nemotron agent retrieves memories by content, position, and time and queries the decoder using cached vision tokens. This design eliminates caption generation at write time, while cached-token decoding reproduces the unsplit model's output in all 450 tested decodes.

</details>


### [231] [UniWALM: Unified World Action Language Model for Embodied Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28626)
*Rahul Sajnani,Diego Romeres,Mingyuan Zhang,Kai Gao,Carl Qi,Baichuan Huang,Srinath Sridhar,Dylan Randle,Lillian J. Ratliff,Jose Barreiros,Manikantan Nambi,Jeroen Vanbaar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniWALM：单一 Mixture-of-Transformers 骨干中统一视频、动作、语言三模态，各模态扩散头同步协同去噪，生成的语言作为下一步的记忆输入，形成无外部规划器的“语言即记忆”闭环；并提出 Flow Matching with Correction (FMC) 提升动作流鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能跨语言、感知与动作整合经验，而现有方法通常将推理/VLM 作为独立阶段挂接在视频-动作骨干上，存在阶段性割裂、需外部规划器与记忆库的问题。作者追问：联合生成语言能否丰富共享表征，从而改进操作并支持依赖记忆的行为。

Method: 统一世界-动作-语言模型，三模态共享表征且互为导向信号与监督目标；单骨干内每模态扩散头同步 co-denoise，一次前向同时解码子任务、文本记忆与动作，无独立规划阶段；生成语言在下一步被重新消费构成记忆闭环；训练目标 FMC 通过扰动动作轨迹学习纠错，推理零额外开销。

Result: 同骨干基线（如 FastWAM）上稳定提升：MolmoSpaces 组合式 pick-and-place；RMBench 记忆依赖子集达 34.5%（无预训练语言/推理模型），超过 Mem-0 配关键帧设置的 28.5%（仅用单锚帧）；真实 Find-the-Item 任务中生成文本记忆将成功率从 11.1% 提升至 61.1%，SOTA VLA 仅 ≤33.3%。

Conclusion: 将语言生成内嵌为共享表征中的记忆机制、并配合同步多模态去噪与纠错式流匹配，可在不依赖外部规划器/记忆库下显著提升长时序记忆依赖操作，并在真实机器人任务上带来大幅增益。

Abstract: Motivated by the human ability to integrate experience across language, perception, and action, we introduce UniWALM, a unified world-action-language model that learns video, action, and language in a single shared representation, where each modality is both a conditioning signal and a supervision target. We ask: can jointly generating language enrich this shared representation, improving manipulation and supporting memory-dependent behavior? Unlike prior approaches that attach a reasoning or vision-language model to a video-action backbone and run them as distinct stages, UniWALM unifies all three modalities inside a single mixture-of-transformers backbone whose per-modality diffusion heads co-denoise synchronously, decoding subtask and textual memory in the same pass as action with no separate planning stage; the generated language is re-consumed on the next step, closing a language-as-memory loop without an external planner or memory bank. To keep the action stream reliable under this richer objective, we further introduce Flow Matching with Correction (FMC), a training objective that teaches recovery from perturbed action trajectories at no inference-time cost. UniWALM consistently improves over same-backbone baselines (e.g., FastWAM) on compositional pick-and-place (MolmoSpaces) and across the memory-dependent subset of RMBench, where it reaches 34.5% without any pretrained language or reasoning model and exceeds Mem-0's keyframe-equipped setup (28.5%) using only a single anchor frame. On a real-world Find-the-Item task, generating textual memory lifts success from 11.1% to 61.1%, while state-of-the-art VLAs stay at or below chance (<=33.3%).

</details>


### [232] [Learning Adaptable Dexterous Skills via Hybrid Dexterous Mimicking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28628)
*Yuxuan Kuang,Jiashun Wang,Yixuan Sun,Yufei Ye,Sungjae Park,Umar Iqbal,Katerina Fragkiadaki,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaDex 提出目标条件强化学习框架，将数秒的单条手-物体运动轨迹（动捕或 RGB(D) 视频的 HOI 重建）转化为可泛化的灵巧技能；核心是"混合灵巧模仿"——用大量并行环境中并行的动作跟踪环境注入人类先验、泛化环境用噪声初始态/目标与稀疏成功奖励逼迫技能偏离参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是让多指灵巧手掌握功能性抓取、手中重定向、动态交接等技能。现有方法存在取舍：纯目标达成 RL 能泛化到任意物体位姿但缺乏人类先验，收敛到不自然、不可靠的指部策略；动作跟踪能复现人类行为但无法偏离单个片段，泛化性差。作者希望同时兼顾"人类先验"与"泛化能力"，且尽可能降低数据成本（每个技能仅需数秒人类运动）。

Method: 提出 AdaDex：目标条件 RL 框架，核心为 Hybrid Dexterous Mimicking（混合灵巧模仿）。在共享策略上混合两类大规模并行仿真环境：(1) 动作跟踪环境，施加稠密动作跟踪损失，从人类运动注入人类先验；(2) 泛化环境，施加噪声化的初始状态与目标，仅用稀疏的物体成功奖励，迫使技能在远离参考轨迹处也可用。输入为单条数秒的手-物体轨迹，来源可为动捕数据或野外 RGB(D) 视频的 HOI 重建。

Result: 从每个技能数秒的人类运动出发，学得可适应、多样化的灵巧技能：功能性抓取、物体重摆、带手指步态（finger gaiting）的手中重定向，以及抛接（throw-and-catch）等动态行为。方法在泛化性与功能性上优于基线，并展示了跨仿真器迁移与真机迁移。

Conclusion: 混合"稠密跟踪先验 + 稀疏目标泛化"的双环境并行训练，是解决灵巧手 RL 中"人类先验 vs 泛化"取舍的有效范式，且数据效率高（每技能数秒轨迹）。作者总结其实验仅基于单一演示来源，后续可扩展到多片段、多物体的技能库与更复杂接触任务，以及更系统的真机规模化评测。

Abstract: Dexterous multi-finger hands hold promise to unlock diverse skills like functional grasps, in-hand reorientation, and dynamic handover that the human-designed world assumes, yet existing learning recipes force a trade-off. Goal-reaching reinforcement learning generalizes to arbitrary object poses but, lacking any human prior, converges to unnatural and unreliable finger strategies; motion tracking reproduces human-like behavior from a single clip but cannot deviate from it. We present AdaDex, a goal-conditioned RL framework that turns a single hand-object trajectory of a few seconds---from mocap data or HOI reconstruction from in-the-wild RGB(D) video---into an adaptable dexterous skill. At the core of AdaDex is Hybrid Dexterous Mimicking: a shared policy is trained on two populations of massively parallel environments: motion tracking environments trained with dense motion tracking objective, which instill the human prior from human motion; and generalization environments with noised initial states and goals and a sparse object-success reward, which force the skill to work far off the reference. From seconds of human motion per skill, the resulting policies can learn diverse adaptable dexterous skills, such as functional grasping, object reposing, in-hand reorientation with finger gaiting, and dynamic behaviors like throw-and-catch. Our method outperforms baselines in terms of generalization and functionality, and we demonstrate transfer across simulators and to the real world. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Physical AI: Concepts, Techniques, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27045)
*Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen,Tianfan Xue,Bin Zhao,Bingqi Jiang,Zihan Guo,Zicai Cui,Yang Li,Ming Zhou,Xinyuan Chen,Lihe Ding,Xiao Chen,Yujin Wang,Shi Guo,Junting Dong,Tao Lu,Xing Shen,Haoyu Guo,Yao Mu,Haiyong Yu,Haoxiang Ma,Fuxian Huang,Qiaojun Yu,Yunsong Zhou,Kailin Li,Zirui Wang,Yufei Xue,Weishuai Zeng,Tao Huang,Zhigang Wang,Junli Liu,Pengyuan Wu,Pingrui Zhang,Haotian Wang,Dongzhan Zhou,Jiafei Cao,Feng Yuan,Xinyi Ye,Quanli Xuan,Mulin Yu,Shangding Gu,Kaiwen Song,Dong Wang,Jiarui Li,Kehui Liu,Zhongjie Jia,Dongjiang Li,Jiacheng Bao,Haoran Yang,Zhaoqing Chen,Yiming Sun,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一部关于"物理AI"的专著（monograph），系统界定物理AI概念、技术版图与核心挑战，构建统一框架并综述感知、物理仿真、世界模型、具身基础模型、运动控制、多智能体协同与软硬件协同设计及多领域应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI近年快速发展但学界业界对其定义、技术版图与核心挑战尚未形成共识，缺乏统一框架与系统性梳理，需要澄清其与传统数字AI、具身AI、信息物理系统、数字孪生之间的边界。

Method: 综述性专著方法：给出物理AI的定义（在真实世界约束下通过连续反馈感知、建模并作用于物理世界的人工系统），建立跨物理、控制、认知、学习与推理的基础理论，并按方法类别组织关键子领域（多模态感知、物理数据与仿真、世界模型、具身基础模型、运动控制系统、多智能体协同、软硬件协同设计）逐一深入讨论，同时梳理应用与评测、安全治理、长期部署等议题。

Result: 摘要未提供实验或量化结果；产出为统一概念框架、与相邻领域的系统辨析、方法论分类体系、应用谱系以及从窄域自动化通向通用物理AI乃至Physical AGI的挑战与研究议程。

Conclusion: 物理AI需要将感知—预测—决策—行动—适应闭合为闭环系统，其中sim-to-real迁移、可靠性、实时执行、评测、安全与治理是落地的关键瓶颈；该专著为领域提供路线图式参考，但本身不含可操作的新方法或实证。

Abstract: In recent years, physical artificial intelligence (physical AI) has experienced rapid development, capturing significant attention in academia and industry. However, the field has yet to reach consensus on its definition, technical landscape, and core challenges. To address this gap, this monograph seeks to define physical AI, present its technical landscape, and examine each key technical subfield in depth. In doing so, it establishes a unified framework for Physical AI: artificial systems that perceive, model, and act upon the physical world through continuous feedback under real-world constraints. Further, this monograph distinguishes physical AI from conventional digital AI, embodied AI, cyber-physical systems, and digital twins. It develops the foundations of physical AI across physics, control, cognition, learning, and reasoning. It reviews key methodologies, including multimodal perception, physical data and simulation, world models, embodied foundation models, motor-control systems, multi-agent coordination, and hardware-software co-design. Furthermore, it examines applications spanning robotics, manufacturing, infrastructure, scientific experimentation, and autonomous services. Particular attention is given to sim-to-real transfer, reliability, real-time execution, evaluation, safety, governance, and long-term deployment. By connecting perception, prediction, decision-making, action, and adaptation in a closed loop, this monograph identifies the technical challenges and research directions required to advance from narrow automation toward more generalizable physical AI and, ultimately, physical artificial general intelligence (Physical AGI).

</details>


### [234] [Omni-Woodpecker: Hallucination Correction via Evidence-Prior Rebalancing for Omni LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27103)
*Zijian Liu,Chaoyou Fu,Yan Yang,Caifeng Shan,Ran He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Omni-Woodpecker (OWP) 是一个免训练框架，用跨模态冲突检测与正交分解把grounding证据与先验分离，在解码前对融合表示做冲突条件化的重平衡，以缓解Omni LLM的跨模态幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Omni LLM的幻觉缓解方法多在输出层操作，忽视了融合表示中"grounding证据"与"学到的先验"相互纠缠这一根因。

Method: 在三模态互补的单模态视图上识别跨模态冲突；对隐状态用正交分解把grounding证据与竞争先验拆开；自适应抑制误导性先验、恢复被忽略证据，在解码前重平衡融合表示。全程免训练。

Result: Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上CMM提升12.9分、AVHBench提升8.6分，四个通用OLLM推理基准稳定提升0.67–2.25分；迁移到VideoLLaMA2-AV仍分别提升4.6与3.9分，显示跨架构泛化。

Conclusion: 幻觉缓解应前移到表示层；冲突条件化的表示重平衡免训练、跨架构有效，可作为多模态表示干预的可复用范式。

Abstract: Recent Omni LLMs (OLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in joint audio-visual reasoning, yet cross-modal hallucinations remain a major challenge. Existing mitigation methods mainly operate at the output level, overlooking the entanglement of grounding evidence and learned priors within fused representations. To address this issue, we propose Omni-Woodpecker (OWP), a training-free framework that shifts hallucination mitigation from output-level manipulation to conflict-conditioned representation rebalancing. Specifically, OWP first identifies cross-modal conflicts from complementary modality-specific views. It then employs orthogonal decomposition to separate grounding evidence from competing priors within the hidden states. Finally, OWP adaptively rebalances fused representations by suppressing misleading priors and restoring ignored evidence before decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and strong cross-architecture generalization of OWP. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the backbone, OWP improves performance by 12.9 and 8.6 points on CMM and AVHBench, respectively, while consistently achieving gains of 0.67-2.25 points across four general OLLM reasoning benchmarks. Furthermore, OWP generalizes effectively to VideoLLaMA2-AV, improving performance by 4.6 and 3.9 points on the same benchmarks. Code is publicly available at this https URL

</details>


### [235] [KPHarness: Co-Evolving Knowledge and Probing for Long-Horizon Gaming Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27417)
*Zhikang Chen,Yueqing Sun,Sen Cui,Ziang Ye,Yu Wang,Yan Feng2,Xierui Song,Yu Zhang,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KPHarness：通过“恢复知识—主动探测”协同演化，为冻结的VLA模型自适应其外部执行封装（harness），并用配对沙箱评估做有界修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失败轨迹只能表明需要干预，却不能说明执行封装应如何改；单纯提出新的恢复规则缺乏诊断证据，因此需要主动收集证据来指导封装的自适应修订。

Method: 双层结构：快循环执行冻结策略，慢循环把累积知识与探测证据转化为有界harness修订。恢复知识提供情境化干预假设以驱动主动探测，探测结果反过来判定假设适用性并精化知识库与探测选择；每次修订在与现任harness匹配的沙箱条件下做配对评估，仅当显著改进才接受；有效恢复保留用于跨任务复用，由即时交互反馈与延迟的episode成功信用分别更新动作与知识探测策略。

Result: 在Minecraft挖矿、战斗、合成任务上，任务覆盖优于裸策略执行、手工固定harness、以及非自适应探测的知识演化基线；在未见任务上，固定演化后的harness、知识库与探测策略，任务完成率仍有提升。

Conclusion: 将“诊断式探测”作为harness适配的核心机制优于规则替换式适配，且演化所得知识与探测策略具备跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: A failed gaming trajectory reveals that an agent needs intervention, but not necessarily how its execution harness should change. Effective harness adaptation therefore requires gathering diagnostic evidence, rather than merely proposing alternative recovery rules. We present KPHarness, a framework that adapts the external execution harness of a frozen vision-language-action model through knowledge-probing co-evolution. Recovery knowledge supplies context-specific intervention hypotheses that guide active probing; probe outcomes reveal when these hypotheses apply and provide evidence for refining both recovery knowledge and later probe selection. This reciprocal process allows the harness to improve not only which interventions it applies, but also how it investigates failures. KPHarness separates fast policy execution from a slow research loop that translates accumulated knowledge and probing evidence into bounded harness revisions. Each revision is compared with the incumbent under matched sandbox conditions and accepted only when the paired evaluation shows improvement. Recoveries supported by further validation are retained for cross-task reuse, while immediate interaction feedback and delayed episode-success credit update action- and knowledge-probing policies during adaptation. Experiments on Minecraft tasks spanning mining, combat, and crafting show improved task coverage over bare policy execution, a manually specified fixed harness, and knowledge-only harness evolution with non-adaptive probing. Evaluation on previously unseen tasks further shows improved task completion with the evolved harness, knowledge bank, and probing policies held fixed,

</details>


### [236] [Inferring continuous cognitive traits from limited neuroimaging data using pairwise convolutional neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27668)
*Marjan Rashidi,Robert Bain,Craig Stark,Jeffrey Krichmar,Mary Hegarty,Elizabeth Chrastil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出成对卷积排序（pairwise convolutional ranking）框架，直接从全脑T1 MRI学习比较式而非绝对式预测，小样本（N=108）下预测四项空间能力任务表现优于标准回归与逐点深度学习基线，并结合逐区消融定位共性与任务特异性脑区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体认知能力差异的脑结构基础尚不清楚；传统脑—行为分析依赖预定义指标（体积、皮层厚度）且假设线性关系，在小样本上敏感度不足。

Method: 以成对比较（ranking）损失替代绝对回归，直接以整脑T1加权MRI为输入训练卷积网络；对四个小规模空间能力任务做行为预测，并用迭代皮层区域剔除（后向消除）进行可解释性分析。

Result: 四个任务均实现高精度性能预测，预测力高于标准回归与pointwise深度学习基线；后向消除识别出四项任务共有的支撑脑区及任务特异性脑区。

Conclusion: 成对学习可从小样本结构MRI中提取有意义的行为信息，兼顾预测性能与神经解剖学可解释性。

Abstract: Individual differences in cognitive abilities vary widely, yet the structural brain features supporting these differences remain poorly understood. Traditional brain-behavior analyses rely on predefined metrics such as volume or cortical thickness and typically assume linear relationships with behavior, limiting sensitivity in small datasets. Here, we introduce a pairwise convolutional ranking framework that learns directly from whole-brain T1-weighted MRI by making comparative rather than absolute predictions, which performs well even with small samples (N=108). We tested this method with four small-scale spatial abilities tasks. Regression analysis predicted performance with high accuracy across all tasks, achieving greater predictive power over standard regression and pointwise deep learning baselines. Iterative cortical-region removal (backward elimination) revealed brain regions supporting individual performance common to all four tasks, as well as task-specific regions. These results demonstrate that pairwise learning extracts meaningful behavioral information from structural MRI, providing prediction and interpretable insight into the neuroanatomy of individual differences.

</details>


### [237] [When Does Experience Memory Help Closed-Loop LLM Driving Agents? A Controlled Evaluation with Resampled Warmup Pools, Token-Matched Fillers, and Audited Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27714)
*Yingxin Jia,Xuanyi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控评测揭示：闭环LLM驾驶智能体的"经验记忆"增益主要来自预热经验池的抽样运气与提示token效应，而非记忆架构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驾驶智能体的记忆/经验注入方法普遍报告正增益，但未控制两个最关键因素：预热经验池包含哪些经验，以及注入token对prompt的扰动效应。因此公开增益无法归因于记忆机制本身。

Method: 在单一经审计的HighwayEnv测试台（1Hz，gpt-4o-mini）上做受控评测：将预热池视为可重采样随机因子（10个不相交池），加入token匹配的无关填充对照、oracle认证诊断、caution定价指标，并量化审计基线移植质量。对比五类探针：纯文本检索（DiLu移植）、单帧栅格移植（RESPOND启发）、情节时序图记忆、无检索的整合规则手册、扁平K步观测历史基线。

Result: (1)检索记忆的标题级增益属于预热抽样：报告池是十个中最好的，仅一个池内优于No-Memory（符号检验p=0.011），规则手册的"池免疫"在五个新抽样上失效；(2)无检索设计超过token匹配填充，单条15-token谨慎指令即可触及仅碰撞合并上限，定价谨慎的指标抹平规则手册优势；(3)表征复杂性无稳健作用；(4)基线移植质量会静默反转结论。

Conclusion: 单池比较且缺少填充对照会报告出相反的记忆增益；经验记忆在闭环LLM驾驶中的收益不稳健，评测需引入池重采样、token匹配填充与移植审计。作者开源测试台、审计端口、种子与缓存。

Abstract: When does experience memory help a closed-loop LLM driving agent? Published gains cannot say, because the two factors we find to matter most, which warmup experiences the pool contains and what any injected tokens do to the prompt, are controlled in none of them. We answer with a controlled evaluation on one audited HighwayEnv testbed (1Hz, gpt-4o-mini for the decisive contrasts). We treat the warmup pool as a resampled random factor (ten disjoint pools) and add token-matched irrelevant-filler controls, oracle-certified diagnostics, caution-pricing metrics, and quantified audits of the baseline ports. The probe suite spans flat-text retrieval (a DiLu port), a single-frame grid port (RESPOND-inspired), our episodic temporal-graph memory, a retrieval-free consolidated rulebook, and a flat K-step observation-history baseline. Four findings follow, each a property of this testbed. (1) Headline memory gains belong to the warmup draw. The reported pool is the best of ten for both retrieval memories, which beat No-Memory in only one pool of ten (sign test p=0.011), and even the rulebook's apparent pool-immunity collapsed on five fresh draws. (2) No retrieval design tested exceeds token-matched filler. The collision-only merge ceiling is reached by a single 15-token caution instruction, and metrics that price caution erase the rulebook's lead. (3) Representational sophistication is nowhere robustly operative. (4) Baseline port quality silently inverts conclusions. On this testbed, a single-pool comparison without filler controls would have reported memory gains that these controls reverse. We release the testbed, audited ports, seeds, and caches.

</details>


### [238] [Give the Model a Finger: Developmentally Aligned Evaluation of Counting in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27756)
*Yingxin Jia,Hui Geng,Wenxin Yang,Mohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文以“儿童计数”为隐喻，为VLM构建无需训练的智能体循环，调用 dumb 图像工具（点标记、移动、分区）来辅助计数；核心贡献是把模型留在图像上的标记序列当作可解释的“串行视觉推理审计”工具，而非单纯提升准确率的干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在小集合上精确、在大集合上退化为估计，类似未掌握计数的儿童。作者希望借助发展心理学中的“认知性动作”（指认并念数、移开已数物体、分区）理解并测量VLM的串行视觉推理过程，填补“能测什么”而非“能否提升”的空白。

Method: 训练无关的智能体循环：直接调用无视觉智能的标记类图像工具为VLM提供指认能力。测量协议包括：(1) 将每个标签按点级真值分类为命中、重复、幻影、遗漏；(2) 用标签数与最终答案核对；(3) 强制相反遍历顺序检验顺序无关性；(4) 标签扰动探针检验答案是否被“读出”。实验覆盖9个模型、682张图像、7种测试条件。

Result: (i) 精确计数区间是模型特有的签名，跨越近两个数量级（R80从3到150个点）；(ii) 作为干预指认大多无效：标签仅帮助9个模型中的4个在小样本照片上，对不精确指针有害，且很少胜过口头流程或盲点列表，真正收益是可审计轨迹；(iii) 作为测量则成功：标签扰动证明精确指针从标签读取答案，指认精度可预测哪些模型受益，密集场景以遗漏方式失败，模型很少在看到自身标签后修正；(iv) 能力可分离：模型能遵循指定遍历顺序，但顺序反转时常改答案；儿童“规则优于散乱”的优势仅在流程条件下出现，且能抵抗行列乘法的格点捷径。

Conclusion: “指认”作为干预价值有限，但作为可解释的测量装置价值显著：标记把隐式的串行过程外化为可分类审计的对象，揭示计数区间、失败模式与顺序依赖等结构性属性，为诊断VLM的视觉-语言耦合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are exact on small sets and drift into estimation on larger ones, a profile reminiscent of children before they master counting. Children cross the barrier with epistemic actions: pointing at each item while reciting number words, moving counted items aside, partitioning the array. We give VLMs the same actions as dumb image tools with no visual intelligence of their own in a training-free agent loop. Our central claim is about measurement, not intervention: a model that counts by marking leaves a normally hidden serial procedure on the image, and the developmental principles of counting turn those marks into an interpretable audit of serial visual reasoning: every tag is classified against point-level ground truth as on-target, duplicate, phantom, or omitted, the answer is checked against the number of tags, and order irrelevance is tested by forcing opposite traversal orders.
