Across 9 models, 682 images, and 7 harness conditions we find that (i) the exact-counting range is a model-specific signature spanning nearly two orders of magnitude (R80 from 3 to 150 dots); (ii) as an intervention the finger mostly fails: tags help 4 of 9 models on small-count photographs, hurt imprecise pointers, and rarely beat a verbal routine or a blind point list; what the loop buys is an auditable trace, not accuracy; (iii) as measurement it succeeds: a tag-perturbation probe shows that precise pointers read their answers off the tags, pointing precision tracks which models benefit, dense scenes fail by omission, and models rarely revise after seeing their own tags; and (iv) the capacities dissociate: models follow an instructed traversal order yet often change their answer when it reverses, and children's regular-over-scattered advantage appears only under the routine and survives lattices that defeat a rows-times-columns shortcut.

</details>


### [239] [Staged Continual Adaptation of Multimodal Foundation Models for Japanese Financial Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27904)
*Genshin Kakimoto,Atsushi Yanagisawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 追踪8.4B多模态基础模型在日文金融文档上三阶段后训练（视觉-语言对齐、推理蒸馏、领域微调），发现各阶段贡献不可加性叠加：数学蒸馏会覆盖先前获得的金融知识但保留程序性数学技能，领域阶段更多是"修复"而非"安装"，跳过某阶段在领域知识上最优但损失数值推理增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型后训练普遍采用"对齐→推理蒸馏→领域微调"的分阶段流水线，但各阶段之间如何相互权衡、贡献是否可分解，缺乏系统性刻画，导致checkpoint保留与训练调度缺乏依据。

Method: 对8.4B参数多模态模型做逐阶段追踪实验：从无微调基线出发，依次执行视觉-语言对齐、纯数学推理蒸馏、日文金融领域微调三阶段，在五个文本输入基准上评测；并设计跳过某一阶段的消融实验，比较各阶段对能力矩阵的边际贡献。

Result: 阶段间存在非加性交互：纯数学蒸馏虽保留程序性数学能力，却会覆写前期获得的金融知识；领域微调阶段主要表现为修复已被破坏的知识而非新增能力；跳阶段消融在领域知识上取得最优，却放弃数值推理增益；最优checkpoint随任务而异，单阶段贡献不可分解。

Conclusion: 后训练应把重点从"增加训练阶段"转向"阶段调度与checkpoint保留策略"设计，并需针对目标任务选择检查点，而非假定各阶段能力可线性叠加。

Abstract: Staged post-training of multimodal foundation models chains vision-language alignment, reasoning distillation, and domain tuning, yet how each phase trades capabilities against the others remains under-characterised. Tracking an 8.4B-parameter model from an un-fine-tuned baseline through three training phases on Japanese financial documents (evaluated on five text-input benchmarks), we find the phases interact non-additively: math-only distillation overwrites the financial knowledge acquired earlier even as procedural math skill survives, and the domain phase repairs more than it installs. A skip-phase ablation is best for domain knowledge but forfeits numerical-reasoning gains, so per-phase contributions are not decomposable and the best checkpoint is task-dependent, with implications for checkpoint retention and scheduling rather than added stages.

</details>


### [240] [Slot-JEPA: Object-Structured Prediction Interfaces for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28074)
*Peisong Wen,Qianqian Xu,Minye Lei,Siran Dai,Yang Liu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Slot-JEPA 将 JEPA 的稠密 patch 表示分解为稳定语义槽与随时间的空间掩码，并配合转移记忆预测器与 UOT 监督，提升可迁移性与目标条件规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 的稠密 patch 表示对下游预测器接口冗余：物体信息分散在 patch 中、缺少跨时间的实体对应、点对点 l1/l2 距离对空间位移不敏感。

Method: 把稠密表示分解为稳定语义 slot 与时间变化的空间 mask，预训练后冻结复用；Transition Memory Predictor 以紧凑 mask 存储视觉转移，按 slot 统计量检索上下文，两支路分离 slot 特异与跨 slot 共享动力学；用 Unbalanced Optimal Transport 监督 mask 预测并在对齐 mask 间传输质量以评估候选规划。

Result: 七个视觉理解基准上 slot 重建特征保持可迁移；在六个平面环境与 REALM-JEPA 上四个机械臂规划任务中改善目标条件规划。

Conclusion: 面向对象的 slot+mask 分解为 JEPA 提供可复用的结构化接口，配合 OT 距离比点式损失更贴合空间进展，但缺乏真实机器人平台验证。

Abstract: As a prototypical latent world model, Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provide strong semantic representations. However, dense patch representations expose a redundant interface to downstream predictors, which must implicitly infer spatial organization and temporal correspondence, presenting three challenges. First, object information is distributed across patches, forcing each downstream predictor to redundantly recover object structure that could otherwise be reused across tasks before modeling interactions. Second, the lack of explicit entity correspondence across time makes historical transitions expensive, restricting the predictor's flexibility in utilizing context. Third, without explicit transport structure, existing JEPA models typically use pointwise l1/l2 distances, which are insensitive to spatial displacement when predicted and goal supports are largely disjoint. We propose the Slot-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (Slot-JEPA), which decomposes dense representations into stable semantic slots and time-varying spatial masks. Pretrained once and frozen downstream, this decomposition exposes a reusable object-structured interface. Building on it, our Transition Memory Predictor (TMP) stores visual transitions as compact masks and retrieves useful context using statistics of corresponding slots. Its two branches separate slot-specific dynamics from shared cross-slot dynamics. We further use Unbalanced Optimal Transport (UOT) to supervise mask prediction and evaluate candidate plans by transporting mass between aligned predicted and goal masks, providing a signal better aligned with spatial progress. Across seven visual-understanding benchmarks, the slot-reconstructed features remain transferable. Slot-JEPA also improves goal-conditioned planning across six planar environments and four robotic arm planning tasks on the REALM-JEPA benchmark.

</details>


### [241] [Interruption-Aware Recurrent Reinforcement Learning for UAS Navigation Under Temporary Overrides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28121)
*Christopher Conrad,Deepak Panda,Weisi Guo,Antonios Tsourdos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将 UAS（无人航空系统）自主导航中的"人工/自动系统临时接管后交还控制权"（handback）问题形式化，指出中断会破坏循环策略的隐状态，导致任务变量可解码性下降并放大碰撞风险；提出三种中断感知循环架构以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测下的战术去冲突需要循环强化学习整合观测-动作历史，但实际部署中远程飞手、机载 DAA 或运行时保证机制会临时接管并覆盖学习策略。已有研究缺乏对"接管后交还"如何影响循环策略的理解；作者发现中断会使任务变量从隐状态中更难解码，且提升中断后碰撞风险，形成动作-观测反馈回路带来的持续退化。

Method: 在基准环境中诊断中断对循环隐状态的影响（隐状态任务变量可解码性、事后接管碰撞统计），并据此设计三种中断感知架构：①分离特征流的条件化循环处理；②专用中断记忆模块；③门控环境特征。三种结构均以中断上下文为条件调节循环更新。

Result: 三种架构均比标准循环策略保留更多可解码任务信息；最强架构相对循环基线将中断后碰撞降低 74%、中断触发的任务失败降低 69%，且无中断场景性能不下降。

Conclusion: "安全中断"不等于"安全交还"：接管会通过动作-观测反馈回路延长交还后的性能退化。中断感知的循环架构设计可提升 UAS 自主性的韧性与运行时保证兼容性。

Abstract: Safe autonomous navigation of UASs requires tactical deconfliction to address conflicts that strategic coordination cannot prevent. This task is challenging under partial observability, when sensor or traffic data are missing, delayed, or degraded. Geometry-based methods can offer separation assurances but face limitations in dense encounters. Recurrent reinforcement learning has therefore been proposed to learn navigation policies that integrate observation-action histories to optimise long-term safety-efficiency trade-offs. However, operational safeguards may require a remote pilot, onboard detect-and-avoid system, or runtime assurance mechanism to temporarily override the learned policy. A research gap remains in understanding how these interruptions affect recurrent policies when the agent regains authority. Benchmark evidence shows that interruptions make task variables less decodable from the recurrent hidden state and increase post-interruption collision risk. The findings suggest that altered recurrent context prolongs post-handback degradation through an action-observation feedback loop, in which changed actions alter the flight path and subsequent recurrent updates. To address this vulnerability, we propose three interruption-aware architectures that condition recurrent processing on interruption context through separate feature streams, dedicated interruption memory, or gated environmental features. Across simulations, all three architectures preserve more decodable task information than a standard recurrent policy. Moreover, the strongest architecture reduces post-interruption collisions by 74% and interruption-induced mission failures by 69% relative to the recurrent baseline, without degrading interruption-free performance. These results show that safe interruption does not guarantee safe handback, and support interruption-aware recurrent design for resilient UAS autonomy.

</details>


### [242] [ChartEvo: Self-Evolving Agent Harnesses for Long-Tail Chart Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28282)
*Qi Wang,Peijie Wang,Jun Cao,Haoyang Tong,Xiao-Hui Li,Fei Yin,Cheng-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChartEvo：一个让图表智能体针对表现差的长尾图表类型进行递归自我改进的框架，同时演化可复用的图表专用 Skills 与可执行 Tools，并学习局部工具调用链以复用已演化能力、控制推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在常见图表上表现良好，但在视觉结构和推理需求多样的长尾图表上准确率骤降；人工为异构且持续演化的长尾设计策略与工具难以规模化。

Method: 递归自我改进框架（harness 层面）：联合演化可复用的图表专用 Skills 与可执行 Tools，并通过学习可复用的局部工具调用链来组合已演化能力、约束工具增强推理的推理开销。

Result: 在 ChartBench 与 ChartX 的长尾图表上，相对基座模型 Qwen3.7-Plus 准确率分别提升 45.6% 和 32.5%。

Conclusion: 递归自我改进可有效提升智能体对长尾图表的适应性，且以工具调用链方式控制开销；但缺少与通用具体基线在完整集上的对比及技能/工具消融等细节。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have substantially advanced chart understanding and reasoning, yet their performance remains highly uneven across chart types. While modern MLLMs perform well on common charts, their accuracy drops sharply on long-tail charts with diverse visual structures and reasoning requirements. Tool-augmented agents can improve complex chart reasoning by introducing specialized visual capabilities, but manually engineering strategies and tools for the heterogeneous and evolving long tail is difficult to scale. We introduce \textbf{ChartEvo}, a recursive self-improvement framework that enables chart agents to adapt to underperforming chart types through experience-driven harness-level self-improvement. ChartEvo jointly evolves reusable chart-specific Skills and executable Tools, while learning reusable local tool-call chains to compose evolved capabilities and control the inference overhead of tool-augmented reasoning. On long-tail charts from ChartBench and ChartX, ChartEvo improves accuracy over its base MLLM, Qwen3.7-Plus, by \textbf{45.6\%} and \textbf{32.5\%}, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of recursive self-improvement for long-tail chart adaptation.

</details>


### [243] [S-Agent 2.0: Spatial Reasoning with a General Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28354)
*Rui Gong,Ruoyu Wang,Yalun Dai,Hao Li,Sidi Yang,Qihao Zhao,Hongyu Li,Zeyang Guan,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Kim-Hui Yap,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: S-Agent 2.0 将现成通用 coding agent 与命令行 3D 感知工具集结合，围绕"闭合证据回路"（获取证据→持久场景记忆→用空间语言解释读数）设计接口，在 MMSI-Bench（66.5）与 ReVSI（82.3）上超过同骨干的专用系统 SpatialClaw，且可跨 Codex、Claude Code 等 harness 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic VLM 在空间推理上虽能主动调用 3D 感知工具，但存在"模型—工具鸿沟"：工具已返回正确读数，模型仍无法得出正确结论，即原始读数转化为可用结论的环节未被填补。

Method: 提出 S-Agent 2.0：不训练专用空间模型，而是给 off-the-shelf 通用 coding agent 配备命令行感知工具集；接口设计遵循"闭合证据回路"原则——(1) 主动获取场景证据；(2) 存入持久化场景记忆以支持多步复用；(3) 将每个几何读数用直白空间语言转述，使后续推理锚定在工具几何结果上。保留 coding agent 的完全灵活性，并可与不同 harness 正交组合。

Result: 在 MMSI-Bench / ReVSI 上，工具集使 Qwen3.x-27B 相对无工具同 agent 提升 +13.9 / +20.5 分，Gemini-3.x-Flash 提升 +11.2 / +9.6 分；开源骨干下达 66.5 / 82.3（SpatialClaw 为 62.6 / 74.5），闭源骨干下达 74.1 / 90.5（SpatialClaw 为 67.0 / 79.7）。两者增益随骨干增强而缩小，但 SpatialClaw 在 ReVSI 上从 +13.2 骤降至 +2.7，本文仍保持 +9.6，显示增益更稳健。

Conclusion: 空间推理 agent 的瓶颈正从"工具能力"转向"证据—结论的接口设计"，持久场景记忆与空间语言转述是关键；该设计在推理基准上验证了跨骨干、跨 harness 的通用性，但尚未在真实机器人闭环或 3D 资产重建任务中验证。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning asks a model to work out where objects are in 3D space, how they relate to one another, and how they move. Agentic VLMs have recently brought large improvements on such tasks by letting the model actively gather evidence with 3D perception tools instead of answering in a single pass. Even so, on many questions the tools return the right evidence and the model still does not arrive at the correct answer: the gap between model and tools, where raw readings become usable conclusions, is left unfilled. We present S-Agent 2.0, a spatial reasoning agent that equips an off-the-shelf general coding agent with a command-line perception toolkit, and we design the interface around one principle: help the VLM close the evidence loop: acquire scene evidence, remember it in a persistent scene memory, and interpret each reading in plain spatial language. This design keeps the full flexibility of a coding agent, and every reading it acts on stays grounded in the tools' geometry. On MMSI-Bench and ReVSI, the toolkit improves a strong open-source model, Qwen3.8-27B, by +13.9 and +20.5 points over the same agent without tools, and a strong closed-source model, Gemini-3.8-Flash, by +11.2 and +9.6 points. With the open backbone S-Agent 2.0 reaches 66.5 on MMSI-Bench and 82.3 on ReVSI, ahead of the specialized system SpatialClaw on the same backbone (62.6 and 74.5), and with Gemini-3.8-Flash it reaches 74.1 and 90.5 against its 67.0 and 79.7. Both tool gains shrink as the backbone grows more capable, but SpatialClaw's shrinks far more on ReVSI, from +13.2 to +2.7, while ours stays at +9.6. S-Agent 2.0 also works across different general coding agent harnesses, such as Codex and Claude Code.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Steerable Pluralistic Policy Alignment from Anonymized Crowd Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26914)
*Akansha Kalra,Ellen R. Novoseller,Vernon J. Lawhern,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: P3L：无需标注者身份的多元偏好对齐框架，用PCA把异质群体反馈分解为共享偏好基，联合学习轨迹嵌入，测试时可快速做个性化奖励学习与策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元对齐需要从偏好异质甚至冲突的群体中学习并个性化；现有方法依赖标注者身份标签和强预训练表征，难以在匿名众包反馈与可变标注预算下泛化。

Method: 将异质人群反馈视为偏好方向的低秩结构，用PCA对轨迹嵌入与偏好方向共享基进行联合分解，得到无需身份标签的偏好表示；测试期仅需少量用户查询即可线性拟合个体奖励，再进行下游策略优化（RL）。

Result: 多个具身控制任务实验显示：可实现对个体用户的可控个性化对齐，对分布外新用户具备泛化能力，RL与测试性能具竞争力，同时显著降低计算开销并支持可变的适应查询预算。

Conclusion: 以PCA低秩偏好基替代身份条件化的个性化建模，是低成本、可扩展的多元对齐实用方案；局限在于仅在具身控制仿真任务验证，缺乏真实机器人部署与更强基线的比较。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment requires AI systems to learn from diverse populations whose preferences may reflect heterogeneous or even conflicting objectives, and to personalize their behavior to individual users. Existing approaches in pluralistic alignment often assume access to annotator identities and rely on powerful pretrained representations to model individual preference variations. We propose Preference-PCA Pluralistic Learning (P3L), a novel framework for pluralistic alignment that jointly learns trajectory embeddings and the shared basis of preference directions via PCA decomposition from heterogeneous crowd feedback without requiring annotator identities. At test time, our approach enables rapid and accurate personalized reward learning and downstream policy optimization. Experiments across multiple embodied control tasks demonstrate that P3L enables steerable personal alignment that generalizes beyond the offline dataset to out-of-distribution new users and attains competitive RL and test performance, while substantially reducing computational cost and supporting variable adaptation query budgets.

</details>


### [245] [Latent Projection for Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26979)
*Yoo Sung Jang,Michael Ryoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用 SIGReg 正则化导出的高斯-马尔可夫结构与噪声协方差，在状态动态矩阵 A 的特征基下把规划用的 ℓ₂ 代价限制到低不确定性子空间，无需改动编码器、预测器与规划器即可提升潜空间规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型中的规划代价通常用预测末端潜表示与目标潜表示的 ℓ₂ 距离，但潜表示本身含有部分噪声：世界模型训练中的正则项会把无信号方向的方差抬高。作者希望在不改动既有模型与规划器的前提下，剔除这些噪声方向对规划代价的干扰。

Method: 1）指出 SIGReg（驱动每步潜边缘分布趋于标准高斯）不仅防塌缩，还提供了剪枝噪声所需的结构；2）假设潜序列为高斯-马尔可夫过程，则转移是线性的且协方差由映射本身确定，z'|z ~ N(Az, I − AAᵀ)；3）拟合状态动态 A 即可零额外代价得到协方差，并在其特征基下逐方向获得不确定度；4）将 A 约束为正规矩阵（normal matrix）以固定该特征基跨时间步不变，从而用一组特征基度量任意步数的不确定度，作为稳定基计算规划代价；5）在 ℓ₂ 代价上只在状态动态低不确定度前向传导的 k 维子空间内计算。整体为后处理式修正：编码器、预测器、rollout 与规划器均不改动，仅拟合一个受约束矩阵 A。

Result: 在六个环境中评测，在 2D 导航任务 TwoRoom 与 PointMaze 上取得显著增益；在更难的规划设置下，成功率相对朴素 ℓ₂ 代价最高提升 +54.7 个点。

Conclusion: 正则项诱导的高斯结构可被显式利用：通过单个受约束动态矩阵 A 估计各潜方向不确定度，并在低不确定度子空间上计算代价，实现即插即用、零结构改动的潜空间规划改进；主要收益集中在 2D 导航类任务。

Abstract: Latent world models let an agent plan in latent space. The planner searches for an action sequence that minimizes a cost, and that cost is often the $\ell_2$ distance between the predicted terminal latent and the goal latent. The latent, however, is partly noise: the regularizer that prevents collapse during world model training promotes variance in the directions carrying no signal. We show that the same regularizer also supplies the structure to prune that noise in the latent. SIGReg drives the latent marginal to a standard Gaussian at every step, and assuming further that the latent sequence is a Gauss-Markov process, the transition is linear with its covariance pinned by the map itself, $z' \mid z \sim \mathcal{N}(Az,\, I - AA^\top)$. Fitting the state dynamics $A$ therefore yields the covariance at no additional cost, and in its eigenbasis each latent direction carries its own uncertainty. Furthermore, constraining $A$ to be a normal matrix fixes that basis across timesteps, so one eigenbasis measures the uncertainty over an arbitrary number of steps and serves as a stable basis to compute the planning cost on. We then restrict the $\ell_2$ cost to the $k$-dimensional subspace the state dynamics carry forward with the least uncertainty. The correction is post hoc: the encoder, the predictor, the rollout, and the planner are all left untouched, and only a single constrained matrix, $A$, is fit. We evaluate across six environments and show large gains on the 2D navigation tasks, TwoRoom and PointMaze, raising success rate over the plain $\ell_2$ cost by up to $+54.7$ points under a harder planning setting. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [246] [Using Set Learning to Improve Set-Valued Classifiers on Long-tailed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27443)
*Kevin Kasa,Scott C. Lowe,Graham W. Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长尾/不平衡数据下集合值分类器(SVC)性能退化问题，作者系统研究了训练策略与损失函数的影响，并改进 odd-K-out (OKO) 集合式损失（提升样本效率、开销从 3.2x 降至 1.36x），显著提升 top-k、average-k、conformal 等 SVC 的宏平均精度且不显著增大预测集合规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合值分类器本应适合表达不确定性，但在长尾不平衡数据上宏平均评估下性能明显退化；而 SVC 通常在事后校准阶段形成，训练动态对其影响缺乏研究。

Method: 系统性对比不同网络训练策略与损失函数（label smoothing、class balancing 等）对下游 SVC 的影响；改进 OKO 损失——按批次内样本集合而非独立样本学习，提升样本效率并降低计算开销（3.2x→1.36x 交叉熵训练）。

Result: 在高度长尾数据集上，改进 OKO 在 top-k、average-k 与 conformal prediction 等多种 SVC 方法上显著提升 macro-accuracy，同时保持有竞争力的 micro-accuracy，且未明显增大预测集合大小，说明少数类性能提升来自更好的特征/决策边界而非放宽集合。

Conclusion: 精心设计的基于集合的训练可产生更适合不平衡数据的网络，为 SVC 的训练阶段设计提供了实践指导；但工作聚焦分类不确定性表达，与具身/3D 等领域关联较弱。

Abstract: Set-valued classification (SVC) offers a practical approach to communicating uncertainty while maintaining accuracy by predicting variable-sized sets per input rather than fixed-sized sets (e.g. top-1 or top-5). However, set-valued classifiers (SVCs) exhibit significantly degraded performance on long-tailed and imbalanced datasets, a setting for which they are ostensibly well-suited. This becomes apparent under macro-average evaluation, which weighs all classes equally. SVCs are typically formed during a post-hoc calibration stage, so little is known about how training dynamics affect their behaviour. In this work, we investigate how different neural network training strategies and loss functions impact downstream SVC performance on highly imbalanced data. In addition to methods such as label smoothing and class-balancing, we investigate whether loss functions that operate over sets of examples in a batch, rather than treating all examples as independent, lead to better downstream SVCs. We adapt the recently proposed odd-K-out (OKO) loss function, which learns from sets of training samples, improving its sample efficiency whilst introducing algorithmic modifications that reduce its computational overhead from 3.2x to just 1.36x that of standard cross-entropy training. Experiments on highly long-tailed datasets demonstrate our modified OKO approach significantly improves macro-accuracy across multiple SVC methods - including top-k, average-k, and conformal prediction - while maintaining competitive micro-accuracy. This improved performance on minority classes occurs without substantially increasing prediction set sizes, suggesting that carefully constructed set-based training can produce networks better suited to imbalanced data.

</details>


### [247] [Pairwise Learning Increases Data Efficiency in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27669)
*Robert Bain,Marjan Rashidi,Elizabeth Chrastil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出成对比较(pairwise comparison)框架，将回归任务重构为相对排序聚合，在无需额外监督的情况下大幅扩充训练信号，提升小样本回归与分类的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在小样本回归中易塌缩为低方差预测，丢失个体差异信息；作者希望用相对比较替代绝对回归，以更充分利用有限标注。

Method: 将回归重构为成对样本间的相对排序，聚合大量比较结果得到数值预测；在UTKFace(2.3万图像)上生成超10亿对训练比较，用从零训练的ResNet10验证；分类任务上以成对监督替代10路交叉熵。

Result: UTKFace上MAE达3.64年，超过含大规模预训练的先前模型；STL10 500–2000标注样本下分类准确率较相同10路交叉熵模型最高提升5.2%。

Conclusion: 成对监督可跨架构与数据规模提升数据效率，在标注稀缺场景(尤其临床)下同时改善回归与分类性能。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often fail at small-sample regression, collapsing to low-variance predictions that miss individual differences. Here, we introduce a pairwise comparison framework that reframes regression as aggregated relative rankings. This method improves data efficiency across model architectures and dataset sizes. On the UTKFace dataset (23k images), pairwise learning generates over one billion training comparisons without additional supervision. The paired ranking allows a lightweight ResNet10 trained from scratch on home hardware to achieve a 3.64-year MAE-surpassing previous models, including those with large-scale pretraining. For classification into categories, pairwise supervision consistently outperforms an otherwise identical 10-way crossentropy model on STL10 with 500-2000 labeled examples, improving accuracy by up to 5.2%. Overall, pairwise supervision enhances data efficiency and enables strong regression and classification performance when labeled data are limited, particularly in clinical settings.

</details>


### [248] [How Much Do $\ell_1$ Attacks Need Concentrated Perturbations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27773)
*Melika Shirian,Kianoosh Vadaei,Arshia Hemmat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将 ℓ1 对抗预算分解为总量约束与逐坐标上限两个维度，通过在优化循环内精确投影加入逐坐标硬约束，系统评估 APGD-ℓ1、SLIDE、FMN-ℓ1 的"扰动集中度敏感性"，发现小上限（≤32/255）能保留约97%中位攻击成功率，同时峰值幅度降80%、有效支撑近3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ℓ1 攻击评估只关注全局预算大小，忽略了扰动在坐标间的分布形态（集中度）。作者希望回答一个此前未被研究的问题：当限制单坐标最大改动量时，ℓ1 攻击的显著成功有多少来自"少数坐标的极大修改"，从而提出扰动集中度作为 ℓ1 攻击评估的补充维度。

Method: 在 ℓ1 预算基础上引入逐坐标上限（ℓ∞ 式 box cap），通过精确的 in-loop 投影在 APGD-ℓ1、SLIDE、FMN-ℓ1 三个攻击中联合执行 ℓ1 与 cap 双约束，不改变各攻击原始目标函数，并对上限值做扫描；在 18 个标准模型设定上统计攻击成功率、峰值幅度与有效支撑大小，与各自无上限基线对比；另在 ImageNet 上用 LPIPS/DISTS 评估感知差异，并与事后裁剪（post-hoc clipping）方案对照。

Result: 18 个设置下，cap ≤32/255 时中位攻击成功率保留约 97%，中位峰值幅度下降 80%，中位有效支撑近乎三倍；在匹配实际 ℓ1 消耗的条件下，加帽扰动在结构上仍与原生扰动不同，并在部分 cap 下获得更低 LPIPS 与 DISTS。循环内约束优化相比事后裁剪最高多保留 88 个百分点的攻击成功率。最佳 cap 因攻击与模型而异，对抗训练模型对集中度的敏感性差异更大。

Conclusion: ℓ1 攻击的成功主要不依赖极端集中式的大幅坐标扰动，扰动集中度是独立于总预算、且模型/攻击相关的评估轴；约束必须在优化内精确施加，事后裁剪会严重低估约束攻击的效力。该工作为 ℓ1 威胁模型研究提供了新的评估维度，但对本领域（机器人/3D/世界模型）仅为安全评估背景工作。

Abstract: A global $\ell_1$ budget limits how much an adversary may change an image, not how that change is distributed. We ask how much of an $\ell_1$ attack's observed success remains when large coordinate changes are restricted. We add a per-coordinate cap to the budget, enforce the joint constraint by exact in-loop projection, and sweep the cap for APGD-$\ell_1$, SLIDE, and FMN-$\ell_1$ without altering their objectives. Across 18 standard-model settings, caps of at most $32/255$ retain a median $97\%$ of attack success relative to each method's native uncapped baseline, while median peak magnitude falls by $80\%$ and median effective support nearly triples. At matched realized $\ell_1$, capped perturbations remain structurally distinct; separate ImageNet comparisons also show lower LPIPS and DISTS at selected caps. Direct in-loop constrained optimization preserves up to $88$ percentage points more attack success than post-hoc clipping, highlighting the importance of constraint enforcement. Suitable caps nevertheless vary across attacks and models, and adversarially trained classifiers exhibit more heterogeneous concentration sensitivity. These results establish perturbation concentration as a complementary, model- and attack-dependent axis of $\ell_1$ attack evaluation.

</details>


### [249] [From Generic to Dedicated: A Novel Optimizer for Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27835)
*Yongyi Wu,Zheng Wang,Sen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出优化器SPIN，将Polyak-Ruppert平均视为"可塑性"引擎、Primal平均视为"稳定性"锚点，通过在两序列间插值显式解耦可塑性与稳定性，可即插即用替换SGD/AdamW以缓解在线持续学习中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线持续学习（OCL）在严格单遍约束下极易灾难性遗忘；现有工作多从数据、结构、优化策略入手，但底层优化器仍直接沿用SGD、AdamW等通用方法，优化动力学与OCL需求之间的关联未被显式利用。

Method: 将优化动力学与OCL需求重新关联：Polyak-Ruppert平均对应"可塑性"、Primal averaging对应"稳定性"。据此设计SPIN优化器，在快速移动的"可塑性"序列与自适应的"稳定性"序列之间做插值，实现二者解耦；理论上证明SPIN隐含逆Hessian近似，相当于提供Tikhonov正则，抑制单样本Hessian估计的噪声并减轻遗忘。使用时以现有OCL方法产生的梯度为输入，直接替换标准优化器。

Result: 在多种OCL方法上以SPIN替换标准优化器均获得一致且显著的性能提升，包括具有挑战性的无回放（rehearsal-free）设定以及严格内存约束下的场景。

Conclusion: 优化器层面是OCL中被忽视但有效的改进维度；SPIN以插值形式统一可塑性与稳定性，具备理论解释、通用性与即插即用性，可作为现有OCL方法的通用优化组件。

Abstract: Online Continual Learning (OCL) requires models to learn from non-stationary data streams under a strict single-pass constraint, making them highly susceptible to catastrophic forgetting. While existing studies have explored various strategies from data, architecture, and optimization perspectives, the optimizer often directly uses generic approaches such as SGD and AdamW. In this work, we reveal a new link between optimization dynamics and OCL needs by recasting Polyak-Ruppert averaging as the engine of "plasticity" and Primal averaging as the anchor of "stability". This inspires our novel optimizer, SPIN (Stability-Plasticity INterpolation), which explicitly decouples plasticity and stability by interpolating between a fast-moving "plasticity" sequence and an adaptive "stability" sequence. We theoretically demonstrate that SPIN implicitly employs an inverse Hessian approximation, providing crucial Tikhonov regularization to damp noisy, single-sample Hessian estimates and mitigate forgetting. More importantly, SPIN can be seamlessly integrated into existing OCL methods by taking resulted gradients as input and replacing standard optimizers. Extensive experiments show that using SPIN instead of standard optimizers yields consistent and significant performance gains across multiple OCL approaches, including the challenging rehearsal-free setting and under strict memory constraints.

</details>


### [250] [LocaLeJEPA: Maximum Entropy Representations under Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28458)
*Kartik Tandon,Alessio Borgi,Pavel Snopov,Inés García-Redondo,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano,Fabrizio Silvestri,Pietro Liò,Francesca Dominici,Randall Balestriero,Claudio Battiloro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LocaLeJEPA，通过对 LeJEPA 的熵最大化性质进行流形理论重构，把全局各向同性高斯分布匹配推广到局部欧氏分布匹配（LocaLeReg 正则项），使 JEPA 类自监督学习可直接用于双曲 ViT、等变 CNN 等非欧几里得嵌入几何，在 ImageNet-1K 上线性探测与 k-NN 及下游任务均优于 LeJEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 易发生表征坍缩；LeJEPA 用分布匹配目标（各向同性高斯）解决该问题，但该目标本质绑定欧氏假设，且全局目标无法反映领域特定的几何先验（如等变 CNN、双曲 ViT 的非欧氏嵌入几何）。作者希望得到一个在原则上尊重数据内在几何、并能推广到奇异嵌入几何的 JEPA 形式化框架。

Method: 在流形理论层面重新表述 LeJEPA 的熵最大化性质，据此提出 LocaLeJEPA 框架；核心组件是 LocaLeReg——一种局部欧氏分布匹配正则器，在每个嵌入点切空间内做分布匹配，从而实现稳定、可扩展的实现，并兼容双曲/等变等非欧几里得骨干网络。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 训练、视觉设定下的充分实验中，LocaLeJEPA 搭配双曲 ViT-S/8 与等变 ResNet-50 骨干时显著优于 LeJEPA；在普通欧氏 ViT-S/8 与 ViT-L/14 骨干上同样优于 LeJEPA。提升同时体现在 ImageNet 线性分类与 k-NN 分类，以及一系列下游任务上。

Conclusion: LocaLeJEPA 是 JEPA 范式中更具原则性的形式化：既更好尊重数据内在几何，也支持向奇异嵌入几何的泛化；代码已开源。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) offer an appealing approach to self-supervised learning, but are typically prone to representation collapse. LeJEPA resolves this by introducing a theoretically grounded distribution-matching objective that drives the embedding towards an isotropic Gaussian. However, this approach is fundamentally tied to Euclidean assumptions, and its global target does not reflect domain-specific geometric priors, such as the use of non-Euclidean embedding geometries, e.g., equivariant CNNs or hyperbolic ViTs. We thus introduce LocaLeJEPA, a framework motivated by a manifold-theoretic reformulation of LeJEPA's entropy-maximization property. The accompanying LocaLeReg, a novel locally Euclidean distribution-matching regularizer, enables a stable and scalable implementation of the LocaLeJEPA framework. We then perform extensive experiments in the vision setting on models trained on ImageNet-1K. LocaLeJEPA significantly outperforms LeJEPA when paired with hyperbolic ViT-S/8 and equivariant ResNet-50 backbones, and also with ordinary Euclidean ViT-S/8 and ViT-L/14 backbones. This holds both in terms of linear and k-NN classification performance on ImageNet and on a series of downstream tasks tested. We view the LocaLeJEPA formalism as a principled approach to the JEPA paradigm that better respects intrinsic data geometry while also accommodating generalizations to exotic embedding geometries. Code available at this https URL .

</details>


### [251] [Planning under Noise with Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28623)
*Michael Chen,Hong Chul Nam,Xi Deng,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PUN 是一种神经算子，学习噪声感知的值场，用于在未知几何中进行随机最优控制规划；采用障碍物 Neumann 反射边界+目标 Dirichlet 条件，并将 Walk on Stars 蒙特卡洛 PDE 求解器扩展到高维，用部分收敛的 MC 标签做摊销训练，推理时结合全局场引导的有界局部搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在控制执行噪声不确定的情况下，需要在多样几何中可靠地反馈规划。现有 screened-Poisson 随机最优控制表述面临两个问题：零 Dirichlet 障碍边界会终止采样路径、削弱长路径上的目标信号；以及无网格高效求解 PDE 且梯度可靠困难。

Method: 1) 在障碍物上施加反射 Neumann 边界、目标处施加 Dirichlet 条件，使采样路径继续探索自由空间；2) 将 Monte Carlo 求解器 Walk on Stars (WoSt) 扩展到更高维，无需配置空间网格；3) 训练神经算子从带噪声、部分收敛的 MC 标签直接学习几何→值场映射（摊销计算），避免每条样本的昂贵收敛；4) 推理时用预测全局场引导的有界局部搜索实现测试时扩展。

Result: 在多个维度的规划任务上，四种噪声水平下 PUN 均匹配或优于所有对比规划器；4D 重规划基准成功率 95–100% 且运行时间最低；在 4D 最高噪声水平下，成功率相对提升 10.7–60.6%，速度快 3.29–32.15 倍。

Conclusion: 通过合理边界条件设计与高维 MC PDE 求解的神经算子摊销，可在大范围未知几何和强执行噪声下获得高成功率、低延迟的规划策略，说明学习几何到噪声感知值场的映射是可行且高效的规划路径。

Abstract: Planning under uncertain control execution noise requires reliable feedback policies across diverse environments. We introduce Planning Under Noise (PUN), a neural operator that learns noise-aware value fields for planning in unseen geometries. Building on an established screened-Poisson formulation of stochastic optimal control, we address two challenges: specifying boundary conditions that support navigation across varying geometries and efficiently computing fields with reliable gradients. Zero-Dirichlet obstacle boundaries terminate sampled paths at walls, weakening the goal signal along long routes and making it harder to estimate reliably. We instead impose reflecting Neumann conditions on obstacles and a Dirichlet condition at the goal, allowing sampled paths to continue exploring free space until they reach the target. To solve the resulting PDE without a configuration-space mesh, we extend a Monte Carlo (MC) solver, Walk on Stars (WoSt), to higher dimensions. However, local MC fluctuations can reverse estimated gradient directions and cause planning failures, making direct planning require costly sampling. PUN amortizes this computation by learning geometry-to-field mappings from noisy, partially converged Monte Carlo labels, avoiding expensive convergence for every training example. At inference, test-time scaling uses bounded local search guided by the predicted global field. We evaluate PUN on planning tasks across dimensions. Across four noise levels, PUN matches or outperforms all compared planners, achieving 95-100% success on the 4D replanning benchmark while maintaining the lowest runtime. At the highest tested noise level in 4D, PUN achieves 10.7-60.6% relative gains in success rate over other methods while running 3.29-32.15 times faster.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Robotic Video World Models: A Survey of Applications, Research Challenges, Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07823)
*Zhiting Mei,Tenny Yin,Ola Shorinwa,Apurva Badithela,Zhonghe Zheng,Joseph Bruno,Madison Bland,Lihan Zha,Asher Hancock,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Philip Dames,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 综述视频世界模型作为具身世界模型在机器人中的用作：数据生成、策略学习、动力学与奖励建模、策略评估与视觉规划，并总结指令跟随差、物理违规幻觉、不安全生成、数据与算力开销等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的仿真器长期受限于简化假设（如刚体、可形变体建模难），表达能力不足；纯语言抽象难以刻画细粒度物理交互。视频世界模型有望以高保真、物理一致的方式合成交互视频，因而成为具身世界模型的有力候选。

Method: 综述性质（survey）：梳理视频世界模型的技术脉络与应用范式，覆盖高效数据生成、policy learning、强化学习中的动力学与奖励建模、policy evaluation 与 visual planning，并归纳可信集成中的关键瓶颈。

Result: 无新实验或量化结果；给出分类梳理、挑战清单（指令遵循差、物理违规幻觉、不安全内容生成、数据与算力开销）及未来方向，并提供策展文献库。

Conclusion: 视频世界模型可绕开物理仿真器的简化假设并作为富有表现力的基础世界模型，但物理一致性与安全性仍未解决；需在安全关键场景落地前解决幻觉、可控性与成本问题。

Abstract: Video world models have emerged as promising candidates for high-fidelity world models, offering the potential to synthesize high-quality videos capturing fine-grained interactions between agents and their environments conditioned on multi-modal user inputs. Their impressive capabilities address many of the long-standing challenges faced by physics-based simulators, driving broad adoption in many problem domains, e.g., robotics. For example, video models can generate photorealistic, physically consistent deformable-body simulation without making prohibitive simplifying assumptions, which is a major bottleneck in physics-based simulation. Moreover, video models can serve as foundation world models that capture the dynamics of the world in a fine-grained and expressive way. They thus overcome the limited expressiveness of language-only abstractions in describing intricate physical interactions. However, despite their potential, video models still generate physics-violating future predictions, often manifesting as hallucinations. In this survey, we provide a review of video models and their applications as embodied world models in robotics, including efficient data generation and policy learning, dynamics and rewards modeling in reinforcement learning, policy evaluation, and visual planning. Further, we highlight important challenges hindering the trustworthy integration of video models, such as poor instruction following, hallucinations like violations of physics, unsafe content generation, in addition to significant data and compute overhead. We present potential future directions to address these open research challenges to motivate research and ultimately facilitate broader applications, especially in safety-critical settings. We provide a curated bibliography at this https URL .

</details>


### [253] [Distributed Platoon Control for Safe and Smooth Crossing at an Unsignalized Intersection-Part I: Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28290)
*Bin-Bin Hu,Shuwei Pei,Saeed Ahmed,Bayu Jayawardhana*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出分布式车队控制器，在闭环路径上分析无信号交叉口安全平滑穿越，并对由反复换车诱导的切换闭环系统给出局部指数收敛证明，CARLA多车型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统开环路径上的双层解耦设计只关注交叉口控制，忽视其与车队收敛的耦合，难以做严格的收敛性分析。

Method: 构建含单一无信号交叉口的闭环路径多车车队场景，设计分布式platoon控制器；将交叉口处反复的车辆切换建模为切换闭环系统，在控制器增益的温和条件下进行局部指数收敛分析。

Result: 理论上证明瞬态阶段保证安全穿越，稳态实现平滑穿越——所有车辆以共同恒速行驶且交叉口无碰撞；并在高保真CARLA仿真中用多种商用车型验证有效性。

Conclusion: 闭环路径设定使严格收敛分析成为可能，所提分布式控制器兼顾安全性与平滑性，具备工程可行性。

Abstract: We propose a distributed platoon controller that guarantees safe and smooth crossing of multi-vehicle systems at an unsignalized intersection. To support the stability analysis, we consider a multi-vehicle platoon maneuvering along a closed path with a single unsignalized intersection. Unlike traditional bi-level decoupled designs on open paths, which primarily focus on intersection control while neglecting its coupling with platoon convergence, and thus hindering rigorous convergence analysis, the closed-path setting allows us to analyze the asymptotic behavior of the platoon. The local exponential convergence analysis of the switched closed-loop system, induced by repeated vehicle switching at the intersection, is presented. Under some mild conditions on the controller gains, we show that safe intersection crossing is ensured during the transient phase and smooth crossing is achieved in steady state, where all vehicles travel at a common constant speed while avoiding collisions at the intersection. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed distributed controller is demonstrated in a high-fidelity CARLA simulator using multiple commercial vehicle models.

</details>
